## Response:
7/15/2026, 1:13:00 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 3/12**. Fokusnya membedakan Maven multi-module vs JPMS module dalam implementasi nyata, karena banyak bug arsitektur muncul dari asumsi bahwa keduanya otomatis sama.

# Seri 3/12 - JPMS vs Maven Multi-Module dalam Java Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas perbedaan praktis antara:

```text id="u43s8z"
Maven multi-module ≠ JPMS module
```

Keduanya sering dipakai bersamaan, tetapi tanggung jawabnya berbeda:

| Konsep | Fungsi utama |
|---|---|
| **Maven multi-module** | Mengatur struktur build, dependency artifact, version, plugin, lifecycle, dan build order |
| **JPMS module** | Mengatur boundary Java: dependency antar module, package visibility, reflection access, dan module graph |

Maven reactor mengumpulkan module yang tersedia, mengurutkan build order, lalu membangun project sesuai urutan. Maven juga menegaskan bahwa `dependencyManagement` dan `pluginManagement` tidak memengaruhi reactor sort order karena yang dihitung adalah reference yang benar-benar instantiated, seperti project dependency antar module. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/guides/mini/guide-multiple-modules.html))

---

# 2. Core Distinction

## 2.1 Maven Module

Maven module adalah unit build.

Contoh:

```text id="rh1wu4"
payment-service/
├── pom.xml
├── payment-domain/
│   └── pom.xml
├── payment-application/
│   └── pom.xml
├── payment-rest/
│   └── pom.xml
└── payment-persistence-mybatis/
    └── pom.xml
```

Maven melihat ini sebagai beberapa project yang dibangun dalam satu reactor.

Maven module mengatur:

```text id="e4iv4p"
1. Artifact ID
2. Version
3. Build lifecycle
4. Dependency antar artifact
5. Plugin configuration
6. Packaging
7. Test execution
8. Publish/deploy artifact
```

---

## 2.2 JPMS Module

JPMS module adalah boundary di level Java language/runtime.

Contoh:

```java id="xj2oo1"
module com.company.payment.domain {
    exports com.company.payment.domain.model;
    exports com.company.payment.domain.port;
}
```

JPMS mengatur:

```text id="gmitxr"
1. Module name
2. requires dependency
3. exports package
4. opens package
5. uses/provides SPI
6. module path resolution
7. strong encapsulation
```

OpenJDK menjelaskan bahwa module path berbeda dari classpath: classpath mencari definisi individual type/resource, sedangkan module path mencari definisi whole modules. Ini alasan kenapa “JAR ada di classpath” tidak sama dengan “JAR menjadi named module di module path”. ([OpenJDK](https://openjdk.org/jeps/261))

---

# 3. Diagram Perbandingan

```text id="k7t6kc"
Maven Multi-Module View
│
├── parent pom.xml
│   ├── dependencyManagement
│   ├── pluginManagement
│   ├── properties
│   └── modules
│
├── payment-domain/pom.xml
├── payment-application/pom.xml
├── payment-api/pom.xml
├── payment-rest/pom.xml
├── payment-persistence-mybatis/pom.xml
└── payment-bootstrap/pom.xml

JPMS View
│
├── module com.company.payment.domain
│   └── exports model, port
│
├── module com.company.payment.application
│   └── requires domain
│
├── module com.company.payment.api
│   └── exports DTO + opens DTO
│
├── module com.company.payment.rest
│   └── requires jakarta.ws.rs, api, application
│
├── module com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis
│   └── requires domain
│
└── module com.company.payment.bootstrap
    └── requires rest, application, persistence
```

---

# 4. Practical Rule

Untuk project baru atau module yang rapi, pattern paling mudah dipahami adalah:

```text id="ov04mr"
1 Maven module = 1 JPMS module = 1 deployable/internal artifact boundary
```

Contoh:

| Maven artifact | JPMS module |
|---|---|
| `payment-domain` | `com.company.payment.domain` |
| `payment-application` | `com.company.payment.application` |
| `payment-api` | `com.company.payment.api` |
| `payment-rest` | `com.company.payment.rest` |
| `payment-persistence-mybatis` | `com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis` |
| `payment-bootstrap` | `com.company.payment.bootstrap` |

Tapi untuk project enterprise existing, jangan memaksakan semuanya langsung. Banyak aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS, MyBatis, serializer, validator, dan testing tool masih lebih aman dimigrasikan bertahap.

---

# 5. Parent POM vs Aggregator POM

## 5.1 Aggregator POM

Aggregator POM berisi daftar module yang dibangun bersama:

```xml id="cg34j7"
<project>
    <modelVersion>4.0.0</modelVersion>

    <groupId>com.company.payment</groupId>
    <artifactId>payment-service-parent</artifactId>
    <version>1.0.0</version>
    <packaging>pom</packaging>

    <modules>
        <module>payment-domain</module>
        <module>payment-application</module>
        <module>payment-api</module>
        <module>payment-rest</module>
        <module>payment-persistence-mybatis</module>
        <module>payment-bootstrap</module>
    </modules>
</project>
```

Fungsi praktisnya:

```bash id="r7ds7y"
mvn clean verify
```

akan membangun semua module dalam satu reactor.

---

## 5.2 Parent POM

Parent POM dipakai untuk inheritance konfigurasi:

```xml id="jjz3z9"
<parent>
    <groupId>com.company.payment</groupId>
    <artifactId>payment-service-parent</artifactId>
    <version>1.0.0</version>
</parent>
```

Parent POM biasanya menyimpan:

```text id="ykj7ah"
1. Java version
2. Maven plugin version
3. Dependency version alignment
4. Common build rules
5. Test plugin configuration
6. Static analysis config
```

---

## 5.3 Parent dan Aggregator Bisa Sama, Tapi Tidak Harus

Dalam banyak project internal, file root `pom.xml` menjadi:

```text id="o4kgpb"
1. aggregator
2. parent
```

Itu umum dan valid.

Tapi secara konsep:

| Role | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Aggregator | Module apa saja yang dibangun bersama? |
| Parent | Konfigurasi apa yang diwariskan ke child? |

---

# 6. Struktur Project yang Direkomendasikan

Untuk Jersey/JAX-RS microservice yang ingin JPMS-friendly:

```text id="k9j9nr"
payment-service/
├── pom.xml
│
├── payment-domain/
│   ├── pom.xml
│   └── src/main/java/module-info.java
│
├── payment-application/
│   ├── pom.xml
│   └── src/main/java/module-info.java
│
├── payment-api/
│   ├── pom.xml
│   └── src/main/java/module-info.java
│
├── payment-rest/
│   ├── pom.xml
│   └── src/main/java/module-info.java
│
├── payment-persistence-mybatis/
│   ├── pom.xml
│   └── src/main/java/module-info.java
│
├── payment-integration/
│   ├── pom.xml
│   └── src/main/java/module-info.java
│
└── payment-bootstrap/
    ├── pom.xml
    └── src/main/java/module-info.java
```

Prinsipnya:

```text id="p1am2x"
Domain module harus paling bersih.
Bootstrap module boleh paling tahu banyak.
REST module tahu Jersey/JAX-RS.
Persistence module tahu MyBatis/PostgreSQL.
Application module tahu domain, bukan framework.
```

---

# 7. Mapping Maven Dependency ke JPMS `requires`

Ini titik penting.

## 7.1 Maven Dependency

Di `payment-application/pom.xml`:

```xml id="vwbyy2"
<dependencies>
    <dependency>
        <groupId>com.company.payment</groupId>
        <artifactId>payment-domain</artifactId>
        <version>${project.version}</version>
    </dependency>
</dependencies>
```

Ini memberi artifact dependency ke Maven.

---

## 7.2 JPMS Dependency

Di `payment-application/src/main/java/module-info.java`:

```java id="stx0c6"
module com.company.payment.application {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    exports com.company.payment.application.usecase;
}
```

Ini memberi module dependency ke Java compiler/runtime.

---

## 7.3 Dua-duanya Perlu Konsisten

Jika Maven dependency ada, tetapi `requires` tidak ada:

```text id="d02c1i"
Maven bisa resolve artifact,
tapi compiler modular bisa menolak akses module.
```

Jika `requires` ada, tetapi Maven dependency tidak ada:

```text id="k2wm3l"
module-info.java menyebut module,
tapi artifact tidak tersedia di build path/module path.
```

Rule praktis:

```text id="etmy5d"
pom.xml menjawab: JAR mana yang tersedia?
module-info.java menjawab: module mana yang boleh dibaca?
```

---

# 8. Root POM Praktis untuk Java 17+

Contoh parent/root POM minimal:

```xml id="or5dvg"
<project>
    <modelVersion>4.0.0</modelVersion>

    <groupId>com.company.payment</groupId>
    <artifactId>payment-service-parent</artifactId>
    <version>1.0.0-SNAPSHOT</version>
    <packaging>pom</packaging>

    <modules>
        <module>payment-domain</module>
        <module>payment-api</module>
        <module>payment-application</module>
        <module>payment-rest</module>
        <module>payment-persistence-mybatis</module>
        <module>payment-bootstrap</module>
    </modules>

    <properties>
        <maven.compiler.release>17</maven.compiler.release>
        <project.build.sourceEncoding>UTF-8</project.build.sourceEncoding>
    </properties>

    <dependencyManagement>
        <dependencies>
            <!-- Centralize versions here -->
        </dependencies>
    </dependencyManagement>

    <build>
        <pluginManagement>
            <plugins>
                <plugin>
                    <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
                    <artifactId>maven-compiler-plugin</artifactId>
                    <version>3.15.0</version>
                </plugin>
            </plugins>
        </pluginManagement>
    </build>
</project>
```

Untuk Java 9+, `javac --release` memastikan source rules, target classes, dan public API sesuai release yang dituju; Maven Compiler Plugin juga menyediakan properti `maven.compiler.release` untuk konfigurasi ini. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/plugins/maven-compiler-plugin/examples/set-compiler-release.html))

---

# 9. Child POM Praktis

## 9.1 `payment-domain/pom.xml`

```xml id="h9wqet"
<project>
    <modelVersion>4.0.0</modelVersion>

    <parent>
        <groupId>com.company.payment</groupId>
        <artifactId>payment-service-parent</artifactId>
        <version>1.0.0-SNAPSHOT</version>
    </parent>

    <artifactId>payment-domain</artifactId>
    <packaging>jar</packaging>
</project>
```

Domain idealnya tidak punya dependency framework.

`module-info.java`:

```java id="tkqxfi"
module com.company.payment.domain {
    exports com.company.payment.domain.model;
    exports com.company.payment.domain.valueobject;
    exports com.company.payment.domain.port;
    exports com.company.payment.domain.exception;
}
```

---

## 9.2 `payment-application/pom.xml`

```xml id="u9eytr"
<project>
    <modelVersion>4.0.0</modelVersion>

    <parent>
        <groupId>com.company.payment</groupId>
        <artifactId>payment-service-parent</artifactId>
        <version>1.0.0-SNAPSHOT</version>
    </parent>

    <artifactId>payment-application</artifactId>
    <packaging>jar</packaging>

    <dependencies>
        <dependency>
            <groupId>com.company.payment</groupId>
            <artifactId>payment-domain</artifactId>
            <version>${project.version}</version>
        </dependency>
    </dependencies>
</project>
```

`module-info.java`:

```java id="umv0fw"
module com.company.payment.application {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    exports com.company.payment.application.usecase;
}
```

---

## 9.3 `payment-api/pom.xml`

```xml id="f1372p"
<project>
    <modelVersion>4.0.0</modelVersion>

    <parent>
        <groupId>com.company.payment</groupId>
        <artifactId>payment-service-parent</artifactId>
        <version>1.0.0-SNAPSHOT</version>
    </parent>

    <artifactId>payment-api</artifactId>
    <packaging>jar</packaging>
</project>
```

`module-info.java`:

```java id="vxrd9k"
module com.company.payment.api {
    exports com.company.payment.api.request;
    exports com.company.payment.api.response;
    exports com.company.payment.api.error;

    opens com.company.payment.api.request;
    opens com.company.payment.api.response;
}
```

---

## 9.4 `payment-rest/pom.xml`

```xml id="tsk9df"
<project>
    <modelVersion>4.0.0</modelVersion>

    <parent>
        <groupId>com.company.payment</groupId>
        <artifactId>payment-service-parent</artifactId>
        <version>1.0.0-SNAPSHOT</version>
    </parent>

    <artifactId>payment-rest</artifactId>
    <packaging>jar</packaging>

    <dependencies>
        <dependency>
            <groupId>com.company.payment</groupId>
            <artifactId>payment-api</artifactId>
            <version>${project.version}</version>
        </dependency>

        <dependency>
            <groupId>com.company.payment</groupId>
            <artifactId>payment-application</artifactId>
            <version>${project.version}</version>
        </dependency>

        <dependency>
            <groupId>jakarta.ws.rs</groupId>
            <artifactId>jakarta.ws.rs-api</artifactId>
        </dependency>
    </dependencies>
</project>
```

`module-info.java`:

```java id="m33v5u"
module com.company.payment.rest {
    requires jakarta.ws.rs;
    requires com.company.payment.api;
    requires com.company.payment.application;

    exports com.company.payment.rest.resource;
    exports com.company.payment.rest.exception;

    opens com.company.payment.rest.resource;
    opens com.company.payment.rest.exception;
}
```

Catatan: dependency API Jakarta REST/JAX-RS di Maven dan module name di `module-info.java` tidak harus identik. Artifact Maven bisa bernama `jakarta.ws.rs-api`, sedangkan JPMS module yang dipakai oleh API Jakarta REST adalah `jakarta.ws.rs`.

---

# 10. Dependency Direction yang Sehat

Target dependency graph:

```text id="vzfmhf"
payment-bootstrap
│
├── payment-rest
│   ├── payment-api
│   └── payment-application
│       └── payment-domain
│
├── payment-persistence-mybatis
│   └── payment-domain
│
└── payment-integration
    └── payment-domain / payment-application-spi
```

Jangan seperti ini:

```text id="mqxwdu"
payment-domain
└── payment-rest

payment-application
└── payment-persistence-mybatis

payment-rest
└── payment-persistence-mybatis
```

Kenapa buruk:

| Dependency salah | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Domain → REST | Business model tahu transport/framework |
| Application → MyBatis | Use case terkunci ke persistence implementation |
| REST → Persistence | Resource bisa bypass business rule |
| API DTO → Entity MyBatis | Contract publik bocor ke database model |
| Common → Semua module | Common menjadi god module |

---

# 11. Maven Reactor dan JPMS Graph Tidak Sama

Maven reactor graph dibentuk dari dependency antar Maven project. JPMS graph dibentuk dari `requires` antar Java module.

Contoh:

```xml id="x3eigq"
<dependency>
    <groupId>com.company.payment</groupId>
    <artifactId>payment-domain</artifactId>
    <version>${project.version}</version>
</dependency>
```

Maven tahu `payment-application` tergantung `payment-domain`.

Tapi JPMS tetap butuh:

```java id="eh0b1n"
requires com.company.payment.domain;
```

Sebaliknya, `dependencyManagement` hanya mengunci versi dependency. Maven guide menjelaskan bahwa `dependencyManagement` dan `pluginManagement` tidak mengubah reactor sort order karena bukan instantiated reference. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/guides/mini/guide-multiple-modules.html))

Makna praktis:

```text id="dy36jz"
dependencyManagement bukan dependency.
pluginManagement bukan plugin execution.
pom dependency bukan module readability.
requires bukan artifact download.
```

---

# 12. Maven `dependencyManagement` vs JPMS `requires`

## 12.1 `dependencyManagement`

Di parent POM:

```xml id="cjcu2t"
<dependencyManagement>
    <dependencies>
        <dependency>
            <groupId>jakarta.ws.rs</groupId>
            <artifactId>jakarta.ws.rs-api</artifactId>
            <version>3.1.0</version>
        </dependency>
    </dependencies>
</dependencyManagement>
```

Ini hanya mengatur versi.

Child tetap harus declare dependency:

```xml id="yvmhq7"
<dependency>
    <groupId>jakarta.ws.rs</groupId>
    <artifactId>jakarta.ws.rs-api</artifactId>
</dependency>
```

Dan JPMS tetap perlu:

```java id="fdff0i"
requires jakarta.ws.rs;
```

---

## 12.2 Rule Praktis

```text id="b9zu82"
dependencyManagement = version catalog
dependencies = artifact usage
module-info.java requires = Java module usage
```

---

# 13. Maven `pluginManagement` vs Plugin Execution

Di parent:

```xml id="ig56m2"
<pluginManagement>
    <plugins>
        <plugin>
            <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
            <artifactId>maven-compiler-plugin</artifactId>
            <version>3.15.0</version>
        </plugin>
    </plugins>
</pluginManagement>
```

Ini mengatur default konfigurasi plugin.

Namun kalau ingin execution spesifik, misalnya generate report atau run tool tertentu, child atau parent build perlu plugin declaration/execution sesuai kebutuhan.

JPMS tidak menggantikan Maven plugin lifecycle.

---

# 14. Artifact Name vs Module Name

Ini juga sering membingungkan.

| Maven | JPMS |
|---|---|
| `artifactId` | `module com.company.payment.domain` |
| `groupId` | Tidak otomatis menjadi module name |
| JAR filename | Tidak selalu sama dengan module name |
| Dependency coordinate | Beda dari module descriptor |

Contoh:

```xml id="htft3x"
<groupId>com.company.payment</groupId>
<artifactId>payment-domain</artifactId>
```

Module:

```java id="o6vvoj"
module com.company.payment.domain {
}
```

JAR:

```text id="wnzmuu"
payment-domain-1.0.0-SNAPSHOT.jar
```

Module name:

```text id="ejn9ij"
com.company.payment.domain
```

Rule:

```text id="h3dto9"
Gunakan nama module yang stabil, fully qualified, dan tidak bergantung pada version.
```

---

# 15. Automatic Module Risk

Jika dependency JAR belum punya `module-info.class`, saat diletakkan di module path bisa menjadi **automatic module**. Nama automatic module bisa berasal dari metadata atau nama JAR, sehingga jangan menebak.

Command:

```bash id="tgzpm5"
jar --describe-module --file target/dependency/some-library.jar
```

Untuk seluruh dependency:

```bash id="didtdd"
mkdir -p target/dependency
mvn dependency:copy-dependencies -DincludeScope=runtime

for j in target/dependency/*.jar; do
  echo "==== $j"
  jar --describe-module --file "$j" 2>/dev/null | head -30
done
```

Rule praktis:

```text id="ep8ue4"
Jangan menulis requires untuk third-party library sebelum tahu module name aktual dari JAR yang dipakai.
```

---

# 16. Split Package Risk

Split package terjadi ketika package yang sama ada di lebih dari satu module/JAR.

Contoh buruk:

```text id="ru7m9d"
payment-domain.jar
└── com.company.payment.common

payment-api.jar
└── com.company.payment.common
```

Di classpath ini mungkin masih lolos, tapi di module path bisa bermasalah.

Solusi:

```text id="rpvoy5"
1. Satu package hanya dimiliki satu module.
2. Hindari package terlalu generik seperti com.company.common.
3. Gunakan package ownership jelas:
   - com.company.payment.domain.*
   - com.company.payment.api.*
   - com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.*
```

---

# 17. JPMS-Friendly Maven Module Boundary

## 17.1 Domain Module

Ciri sehat:

```text id="ngyrxt"
- Tidak bergantung ke framework
- Tidak tahu REST
- Tidak tahu MyBatis
- Tidak tahu PostgreSQL
- Tidak tahu Kubernetes/cloud SDK
- Mengekspor model, value object, port, exception
- Menyembunyikan internal policy/rule/helper
```

---

## 17.2 Application Module

Ciri sehat:

```text id="zhrp91"
- Berisi use case orchestration
- Bergantung pada domain
- Tidak tahu transport detail
- Tidak tahu SQL mapper
- Boleh expose use case service
- Boleh pakai SPI/port
```

---

## 17.3 API Module

Ciri sehat:

```text id="qxrf7g"
- Berisi request/response DTO
- Berisi error contract
- Bisa dipakai REST resource dan client test
- Package DTO bisa export + opens
- Tidak berisi business logic berat
```

---

## 17.4 REST Module

Ciri sehat:

```text id="tcuzp2"
- Berisi JAX-RS resource
- Berisi exception mapper
- Berisi filter/interceptor/provider
- Bergantung ke application dan api
- Tidak bergantung ke persistence implementation
```

---

## 17.5 Persistence Module

Ciri sehat:

```text id="g0m7tv"
- Berisi repository implementation
- Berisi MyBatis mapper/type handler/entity
- Bergantung ke domain port/model
- Tidak diekspor sembarangan
- Di-wiring oleh bootstrap/composition root
```

---

## 17.6 Bootstrap Module

Ciri sehat:

```text id="l0xhg3"
- Main/composition root
- Boleh tahu semua adapter
- Membuat DataSource, SqlSessionFactory, repository, service, resource
- Register Jersey ResourceConfig
- Menjadi boundary runtime startup
```

---

# 18. Practical Project Layout: Recommended Baseline

```text id="h47lst"
payment-service/
├── pom.xml
├── README.md
├── docker/
│   └── Dockerfile
├── k8s/
│   ├── deployment.yaml
│   └── service.yaml
│
├── payment-domain/
│   └── src/main/java/
│       ├── module-info.java
│       └── com/company/payment/domain/...
│
├── payment-api/
│   └── src/main/java/
│       ├── module-info.java
│       └── com/company/payment/api/...
│
├── payment-application/
│   └── src/main/java/
│       ├── module-info.java
│       └── com/company/payment/application/...
│
├── payment-rest/
│   └── src/main/java/
│       ├── module-info.java
│       └── com/company/payment/rest/...
│
├── payment-persistence-mybatis/
│   └── src/main/
│       ├── java/module-info.java
│       └── resources/mappers/PaymentMapper.xml
│
└── payment-bootstrap/
    └── src/main/java/
        ├── module-info.java
        └── com/company/payment/bootstrap/Main.java
```

# 19. Build Commands Praktis

## 19.1 Full Build

```bash id="n68qk3"
mvn clean verify
```

## 19.2 Build Satu Module dan Dependency-nya

```bash id="kq52cr"
mvn -pl payment-rest -am clean verify
```

Makna:

| Option | Arti |
|---|---|
| `-pl payment-rest` | Build project/module Maven tertentu |
| `-am` | Also make required Maven modules |

---

## 19.3 Build dari Module Tertentu ke Dependent-nya

```bash id="fmh99l"
mvn -pl payment-domain -amd clean verify
```

Makna:

| Option | Arti |
|---|---|
| `-amd` | Also make dependents |

Cocok untuk melihat impact perubahan domain.

---

## 19.4 Resume Build Setelah Gagal

```bash id="e3ja45"
mvn -rf :payment-rest verify
```

Useful saat build multi-module panjang.

---

# 20. Dependency Inspection Commands

## 20.1 Dependency Tree

```bash id="kh8kwf"
mvn dependency:tree
```

Per module:

```bash id="kkkw8d"
mvn -pl payment-rest dependency:tree
```

Filter:

```bash id="zgfhu9"
mvn -pl payment-rest dependency:tree -Dincludes=jakarta.ws.rs
mvn -pl payment-persistence-mybatis dependency:tree -Dincludes=org.mybatis
mvn -pl payment-bootstrap dependency:tree -Dincludes=org.glassfish.jersey
```

---

## 20.2 Copy Dependencies untuk Audit JPMS

```bash id="sahqmi"
mvn -pl payment-bootstrap dependency:copy-dependencies \
  -DincludeScope=runtime \
  -DoutputDirectory=target/dependency
```

Lalu:

```bash id="sh7vwq"
for j in payment-bootstrap/target/dependency/*.jar; do
  echo "==== $j"
  jar --describe-module --file "$j" 2>/dev/null | head -20
done
```

---

## 20.3 jdeps

```bash id="nw0kf0"
jdeps \
  --multi-release 17 \
  --print-module-deps \
  payment-bootstrap/target/payment-bootstrap-1.0.0-SNAPSHOT.jar
```

Untuk dependency module path:

```bash id="l60jxx"
jdeps \
  --module-path payment-bootstrap/target/dependency \
  --module com.company.payment.bootstrap
```

---

# 21. Runtime: Classpath vs Module Path

## 21.1 Classpath Runtime

```bash id="dbio52"
java -cp "target/classes:target/dependency/*" \
  com.company.payment.bootstrap.Main
```

Karakteristik:

```text id="vdy1x9"
- Lebih kompatibel
- Cocok untuk framework-heavy runtime
- module-info.java tidak menjadi boundary runtime penuh
- Banyak enterprise app masih memakai ini
```

---

## 21.2 Module Path Runtime

```bash id="vf03lh"
java \
  --module-path "mods:target/dependency" \
  --module com.company.payment.bootstrap/com.company.payment.bootstrap.Main
```

Karakteristik:

```text id="ch2e1f"
- Lebih strict
- Dependency graph eksplisit
- Reflection perlu opens
- Split package lebih cepat ketahuan
- Cocok setelah dependency graph bersih
```

Maven Compiler Plugin documentation untuk JPMS args menekankan bahwa selain module path, argumen tambahan seperti `--add-exports`, `--add-reads`, `--add-modules`, dan `--patch-module` semestinya tidak dibutuhkan untuk main code jika `module-info.java` sudah lengkap; argumen seperti itu lebih sering diperlukan untuk test. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/plugins-archives/maven-compiler-plugin-4.0.0-beta-4/examples/jpms_args.html))

---

# 22. Practical Migration Strategy

## 22.1 Jangan Big Bang

Jangan langsung ubah semua module menjadi JPMS full runtime.

Urutan aman:

```text id="l02luv"
1. Rapikan Maven multi-module dulu.
2. Pastikan dependency direction benar.
3. Tambahkan module-info.java pada domain.
4. Tambahkan module-info.java pada application.
5. Tambahkan module-info.java pada API DTO.
6. Baru evaluasi REST/persistence yang reflection-heavy.
7. Jalankan jdeps.
8. Baru eksperimen module-path runtime.
```

---

## 22.2 Mulai dari Module Minim Reflection

Urutan paling aman:

| Urutan | Module | Alasan |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | `payment-domain` | Minim framework, minim reflection |
| 2 | `payment-application` | Dependency sederhana |
| 3 | `payment-api` | DTO butuh `opens`, tapi manageable |
| 4 | `payment-integration-spi` | Cocok untuk SPI |
| 5 | `payment-rest` | Mulai masuk Jersey reflection |
| 6 | `payment-persistence-mybatis` | MyBatis/resource/reflection lebih tricky |
| 7 | `payment-bootstrap` | Runtime assembly paling kompleks |

---

# 23. Anti-Pattern Maven + JPMS

## 23.1 God Parent Dependency

Salah:

```xml id="jjb0rz"
<dependencies>
    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey.core</groupId>
        <artifactId>jersey-server</artifactId>
    </dependency>
    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.mybatis</groupId>
        <artifactId>mybatis</artifactId>
    </dependency>
    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.postgresql</groupId>
        <artifactId>postgresql</artifactId>
    </dependency>
</dependencies>
```

Jika diletakkan di parent POM sebagai inherited dependency, semua child module bisa ikut mendapat framework dependency.

Lebih baik:

```text id="fnxn63"
dependencyManagement di parent,
dependencies di child sesuai kebutuhan.
```

---

## 23.2 Common Module Menjadi Tempat Sampah

Salah:

```text id="u9qzrg"
payment-common
├── StringUtil
├── DateUtil
├── JsonUtil
├── DbUtil
├── HttpClientFactory
├── SecurityContext
├── MyBatisHelper
├── JerseyExceptionMapper
└── PaymentRuleHelper
```

Dampak:

```text id="vcycri"
Semua module tergoda requires common.
Akhirnya common depends on everything.
JPMS boundary jadi tidak berguna.
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="rvpuxj"
payment-domain-common
payment-api-common
payment-observability
payment-test-fixture
payment-infra-common
```

Pisahkan berdasarkan ownership dan dependency direction.

---

## 23.3 Parent POM Memaksa Semua Plugin Execution

Salah jika semua child dipaksa menjalankan plugin yang hanya relevan untuk app runtime.

Contoh:

```text id="m3vvuk"
domain module ikut build Docker image
api module ikut package fat jar
test fixture module ikut publish deployment artifact
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="oykb4q"
pluginManagement di parent,
execution hanya di module yang relevan.
```

---

## 23.4 Module Name Mengikuti Artifact ID Mentah

Kurang ideal:

```java id="pzt99b"
module payment.domain {
}
```

Lebih stabil:

```java id="twjsu0"
module com.company.payment.domain {
}
```

Kenapa:

```text id="lxea54"
1. Menghindari collision.
2. Lebih jelas ownership.
3. Lebih cocok untuk enterprise multi-team.
4. Konsisten dengan package namespace.
```

---

# 24. Test Structure Note

Testing modular app punya tantangan sendiri, terutama white-box test untuk internal package.

Maven Compiler Plugin 4.x menyediakan konsep `module-info-patch.maven` untuk testing, agar developer tidak perlu menduplikasi seluruh `module-info.java` test hanya untuk menambahkan opsi seperti `add-reads`, `add-exports`, atau `add-opens`. Dokumentasi Maven menjelaskan file ini menerjemahkan deklarasi test-specific menjadi opsi compiler/launcher seperti `--add-reads`, `--add-exports`, dan `--add-opens`. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/plugins/maven-compiler-plugin-4.x/module-info-patch.html))

Untuk sekarang, rule praktisnya:

```text id="kq5lqy"
Jangan membuka production module hanya karena test butuh akses.
Gunakan konfigurasi test-specific.
```

Detail testing akan dibahas khusus di **Seri 10/12**.

---

# 25. Practical Example: REST Tidak Boleh Akses MyBatis

## 25.1 Maven Dependency Salah

`payment-rest/pom.xml`:

```xml id="ar7jpl"
<dependency>
    <groupId>com.company.payment</groupId>
    <artifactId>payment-persistence-mybatis</artifactId>
    <version>${project.version}</version>
</dependency>
```

`payment-rest/module-info.java`:

```java id="d8msy0"
module com.company.payment.rest {
    requires com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis;
}
```

Ini membuat REST bisa akses persistence.

---

## 25.2 Maven Dependency Benar

`payment-rest/pom.xml`:

```xml id="xo0tam"
<dependency>
    <groupId>com.company.payment</groupId>
    <artifactId>payment-application</artifactId>
    <version>${project.version}</version>
</dependency>
```

`payment-rest/module-info.java`:

```java id="f9zxcj"
module com.company.payment.rest {
    requires com.company.payment.application;
}
```

Persistence hanya dipakai di bootstrap:

```xml id="cpkl1k"
<dependency>
    <groupId>com.company.payment</groupId>
    <artifactId>payment-persistence-mybatis</artifactId>
    <version>${project.version}</version>
</dependency>
```

`payment-bootstrap/module-info.java`:

```java id="uwcfb0"
module com.company.payment.bootstrap {
    requires com.company.payment.rest;
    requires com.company.payment.application;
    requires com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis;
}
```

---

# 26. Maven Build vs Runtime Packaging

## 26.1 Build Artifact

Setiap module menghasilkan JAR:

```text id="w2o0a1"
payment-domain-1.0.0-SNAPSHOT.jar
payment-application-1.0.0-SNAPSHOT.jar
payment-api-1.0.0-SNAPSHOT.jar
payment-rest-1.0.0-SNAPSHOT.jar
payment-persistence-mybatis-1.0.0-SNAPSHOT.jar
payment-bootstrap-1.0.0-SNAPSHOT.jar
```

## 26.2 Deployment Artifact

Untuk deployment, ada beberapa pola:

| Pola | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|
| Fat JAR / shaded JAR | Sederhana, umum di microservice |
| Thin JAR + lib folder | Lebih eksplisit dependency runtime |
| Exploded app | App server/container tertentu |
| Module path runtime | JPMS strict runtime |
| jlink image | Runtime lebih kecil/terkontrol, tahap advanced |

Untuk Jersey/JAX-RS microservice, jangan asumsikan fat JAR otomatis modular runtime. Fat JAR sering berjalan sebagai classpath-style application, kecuali packaging dan startup command memang didesain untuk module path.

---

# 27. Docker Implication

Classpath style:

```dockerfile id="x59vre"
FROM eclipse-temurin:17-jre
WORKDIR /app
COPY target/payment-service.jar app.jar
ENTRYPOINT ["java", "-jar", "app.jar"]
```

Module path style:

```dockerfile id="bsjslw"
FROM eclipse-temurin:17-jre
WORKDIR /app
COPY mods/ mods/
COPY lib/ lib/
ENTRYPOINT [
  "java",
  "--module-path", "mods:lib",
  "--module", "com.company.payment.bootstrap/com.company.payment.bootstrap.Main"
]
```

Rule praktis:

```text id="vl8b2s"
Kalau Docker masih java -jar fat jar,
jangan klaim service sudah berjalan sebagai strict JPMS module-path runtime.
```

Tetap mungkin `module-info.java` berguna untuk compile-time boundary, tapi runtime enforcement berbeda jika jalan di classpath/unnamed module.

---

# 28. CI/CD Guardrail

Tambahkan stage seperti ini:

```bash id="gcezo4"
mvn clean verify
mvn dependency:tree
```

Untuk audit modular:

```bash id="itdn9a"
mvn -pl payment-bootstrap dependency:copy-dependencies \
  -DincludeScope=runtime \
  -DoutputDirectory=target/dependency

jdeps \
  --multi-release 17 \
  --print-module-deps \
  payment-bootstrap/target/payment-bootstrap-1.0.0-SNAPSHOT.jar
```

Untuk cek nama module dependency:

```bash id="zop93j"
for j in payment-bootstrap/target/dependency/*.jar; do
  echo "==== $j"
  jar --describe-module --file "$j" 2>/dev/null | head -20
done
```

# 29. Practical Lab

## Lab 1 - Buat Maven Multi-Module Skeleton

Buat struktur:

```text id="nni9vo"
payment-service/
├── pom.xml
├── payment-domain/pom.xml
├── payment-application/pom.xml
├── payment-api/pom.xml
├── payment-rest/pom.xml
├── payment-persistence-mybatis/pom.xml
└── payment-bootstrap/pom.xml
```

Command:

```bash id="a2cyaf"
mvn clean verify
```

Expected:

```text id="f9moe1"
Semua module dibangun oleh reactor.
```

---

## Lab 2 - Tambahkan `module-info.java` Domain

```java id="p3m3dl"
module com.company.payment.domain {
    exports com.company.payment.domain.model;
    exports com.company.payment.domain.port;
}
```

Expected:

```text id="cdd3j3"
Domain compile sebagai explicit JPMS module.
```

---

## Lab 3 - Application Requires Domain

`payment-application/pom.xml`:

```xml id="ce6s2y"
<dependency>
    <groupId>com.company.payment</groupId>
    <artifactId>payment-domain</artifactId>
    <version>${project.version}</version>
</dependency>
```

`payment-application/module-info.java`:

```java id="jc31lq"
module com.company.payment.application {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    exports com.company.payment.application.usecase;
}
```

Expected:

```text id="is5fla"
Application bisa import domain model/port yang diekspor.
```

---

## Lab 4 - Buktikan Maven Dependency Tidak Cukup

Hapus:

```java id="yof2yu"
requires com.company.payment.domain;
```

Tetapi biarkan dependency Maven.

Expected:

```text id="by9s9s"
Compile modular gagal ketika application mengakses package dari domain.
```

Lesson:

```text id="y3qm5a"
Maven dependency menyediakan artifact.
JPMS requires memberi readability.
```

---

## Lab 5 - Buktikan `dependencyManagement` Bukan Dependency

Pindahkan dependency domain ke `dependencyManagement` parent saja, lalu hapus dependency dari child.

Expected:

```text id="kaqkkf"
Application tidak lagi punya artifact dependency langsung.
Build gagal jika class application memakai domain.
```

Lesson:

```text id="hjz1je"
dependencyManagement hanya mengatur versi, bukan menambahkan dependency.
```

---

## Lab 6 - Cegah REST Akses Persistence

Jangan tambahkan dependency `payment-persistence-mybatis` ke `payment-rest`.

Coba di `PaymentResource`:

```java id="uvrm80"
import com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.PaymentMapper;
```

Expected:

```text id="eijn4d"
Compile gagal.
```

Lesson:

```text id="wmuuhj"
Boundary architecture berhasil ditegakkan oleh kombinasi Maven dependency + JPMS requires.
```

---

# 30. Review Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Maven structure | Apakah setiap Maven module punya responsibility jelas? |
| Parent POM | Apakah parent hanya mengelola version/config, bukan memaksa semua dependency? |
| Dependency management | Apakah dependencyManagement tidak disalahpahami sebagai dependency? |
| Plugin management | Apakah pluginManagement tidak disalahpahami sebagai execution? |
| JPMS descriptor | Apakah setiap `module-info.java` sesuai arah layer? |
| Domain module | Apakah bebas dari Jersey, MyBatis, PostgreSQL, cloud SDK? |
| REST module | Apakah tidak depends langsung ke persistence? |
| Persistence module | Apakah tidak diekspor sembarangan? |
| API module | Apakah DTO export/opens jelas? |
| Bootstrap module | Apakah composition root menjadi tempat wiring implementation? |
| Split package | Apakah tidak ada package sama di banyak module? |
| Runtime mode | Apakah jelas app jalan via classpath atau module path? |

---

# 31. Key Takeaway

Pegangan utama:

```text id="ibpzyv"
Maven mengatur artifact.
JPMS mengatur readability dan encapsulation.
```

```text id="st82hg"
pom.xml:
- dependency tersedia
- version terkunci
- build order
- plugin lifecycle

module-info.java:
- module boleh membaca module lain
- package boleh diekspor
- package boleh dibuka reflection
- service provider bisa ditemukan
```

Untuk Jersey/JAX-RS microservice, target praktisnya bukan langsung “semua full modular runtime”, tetapi:

```text id="svr1az"
1. Maven module rapi
2. dependency direction sehat
3. domain bebas framework
4. REST tidak bypass application layer
5. persistence implementation tidak bocor
6. module-info.java memperkuat boundary
7. runtime module-path dievaluasi setelah dependency graph bersih
```

**Seri 3/12 selesai - JPMS vs Maven Multi-Module.**

- [JEP 261: Module System](https://openjdk.org/jeps/261)

## Response:
7/15/2026, 1:18:45 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 4/12**. Fokusnya desain boundary modular yang bisa langsung dipakai untuk service nyata: layer ownership, dependency direction, package/module layout, composition root, dan failure mode saat boundary bocor.

# Seri 4/12 - Designing Modular Microservice Boundaries with JPMS

## 1. Introduction

Di seri ini kita masuk ke desain boundary praktis: **bagaimana membagi Java Jersey/JAX-RS microservice menjadi module yang jelas, aman, dan tidak saling bocor**.

JPMS berguna karena Java module memberi dependency graph eksplisit, module path, `requires`, `exports`, `opens`, dan encapsulation yang lebih ketat dibanding classpath tradisional. Ini relevan untuk microservice enterprise karena dependency antar layer sering memburuk secara perlahan: REST layer mulai import mapper, domain mulai tahu framework, persistence model dipakai sebagai API response, dan bootstrap logic tersebar di mana-mana. JPMS dapat membantu menjadikan boundary arsitektur sebagai aturan compile-time, bukan sekadar konvensi. ([OpenJDK](https://openjdk.org/jeps/261?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Untuk Jersey/JAX-RS, boundary harus tetap realistis. Jakarta REST menggunakan annotation seperti `@Path`, `@GET`, `@POST`, `@PUT`, `@DELETE`, dan konsep resource class untuk membangun endpoint REST. Jersey sebagai implementation menyediakan `ResourceConfig` untuk konfigurasi aplikasi/resource/provider. Jadi desain JPMS harus mempertimbangkan bahwa REST/resource/provider/serializer sering melibatkan reflection atau runtime discovery. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/websvcs/rest/rest.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Target Mental Model

Tujuan desain boundary:

```text
REST layer boleh tahu application use case.
Application boleh tahu domain.
Persistence boleh tahu domain port/model.
Integration boleh tahu domain/application SPI.
Bootstrap boleh tahu semua implementation untuk wiring.
Domain tidak boleh tahu REST, Jersey, MyBatis, PostgreSQL, Docker, Kubernetes, AWS, Azure.
```

Dengan JPMS, ini diterjemahkan menjadi:

```text
requires = arah dependency yang diizinkan
exports  = package yang resmi menjadi contract
opens    = package yang boleh diakses reflection runtime
```

---

# 3. Diagram Modular Boundary

```text
Java Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice
│
├── payment-api
│   ├── request DTO
│   ├── response DTO
│   ├── error contract
│   └── validation contract
│
├── payment-domain
│   ├── aggregate/model
│   ├── value object
│   ├── domain service
│   ├── domain event
│   ├── port/interface
│   └── internal policy
│
├── payment-application
│   ├── use case
│   ├── command/query handler
│   ├── transaction boundary
│   ├── orchestration
│   └── application port
│
├── payment-rest
│   ├── JAX-RS resource
│   ├── exception mapper
│   ├── request/response mapper
│   ├── filter/interceptor
│   └── Jersey provider
│
├── payment-persistence-mybatis
│   ├── repository implementation
│   ├── MyBatis mapper interface
│   ├── MyBatis XML mapper
│   ├── DB entity / persistence record
│   └── type handler
│
├── payment-integration
│   ├── external client
│   ├── adapter implementation
│   ├── retry/idempotency adapter
│   └── provider implementation
│
└── payment-bootstrap
    ├── Main
    ├── ResourceConfig
    ├── dependency wiring
    ├── DataSource setup
    ├── MyBatis setup
    ├── Jersey server setup
    └── runtime configuration
```

---

# 4. Decomposition Map

| Module | Responsibility | Boleh depends ke | Tidak boleh depends ke | Export package | Opens package |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `payment-domain` | Business model, invariant, port | Java SE only, maybe small annotation-only dependency | Jersey, MyBatis, PostgreSQL, cloud SDK | `model`, `valueobject`, `port`, `event`, `exception` | Hampir tidak perlu |
| `payment-api` | REST contract DTO/error | Java SE, validation annotation jika dipakai | MyBatis, DB entity, Jersey implementation | `request`, `response`, `error` | DTO package untuk serializer/validator |
| `payment-application` | Use case orchestration | `domain` | Jersey, MyBatis mapper, PostgreSQL driver | `usecase`, `command`, `query` | Jarang |
| `payment-rest` | JAX-RS adapter | `api`, `application`, `jakarta.ws.rs` | Persistence implementation | `resource`, mungkin `provider` | Resource/provider/mapper jika Jersey butuh reflection |
| `payment-persistence-mybatis` | DB adapter | `domain`, MyBatis, PostgreSQL driver | REST/Jersey | Biasanya tidak export implementation | Mapper/entity/typehandler |
| `payment-integration-*` | External system adapter | `domain` atau application SPI | REST resource, DB mapper | Biasanya tidak export implementation | Client DTO jika serializer butuh |
| `payment-bootstrap` | Composition root/runtime assembly | Semua module yang perlu di-wiring | - | Biasanya tidak perlu | Biasanya tidak perlu kecuali framework |

---

# 5. Recommended Module Graph

```text
payment-bootstrap
│
├── requires payment-rest
├── requires payment-application
├── requires payment-persistence-mybatis
├── requires payment-integration-gateway
└── requires Jersey runtime dependencies
    │
    └── composition root / startup

payment-rest
│
├── requires jakarta.ws.rs
├── requires payment-api
└── requires payment-application

payment-application
│
└── requires payment-domain

payment-persistence-mybatis
│
└── requires payment-domain

payment-integration-gateway
│
└── requires payment-domain / payment-application-spi

payment-api
│
└── mostly DTO contract

payment-domain
│
└── no framework dependency
```

Prinsip penting: **bootstrap boleh tahu banyak, domain tidak boleh tahu banyak**. Bootstrap adalah tempat wiring concrete implementation, sedangkan domain/application harus tetap framework-agnostic.

---

# 6. Layer Ownership Rules

## 6.1 Domain Module

Domain adalah pusat business rule. Isi ideal:

```text
payment-domain
│
├── model
│   └── Payment, Invoice, Order, CustomerAccount
│
├── valueobject
│   └── Money, CurrencyCode, PaymentId
│
├── port
│   └── PaymentRepository, PaymentGateway
│
├── event
│   └── PaymentAuthorized, PaymentFailed
│
├── exception
│   └── PaymentRuleViolationException
│
└── internal
    └── PaymentStatePolicy, FraudPolicy
```

`module-info.java`:

```java
module com.company.payment.domain {
    exports com.company.payment.domain.model;
    exports com.company.payment.domain.valueobject;
    exports com.company.payment.domain.port;
    exports com.company.payment.domain.event;
    exports com.company.payment.domain.exception;
}
```

Tidak diekspor:

```text
com.company.payment.domain.internal
com.company.payment.domain.policy.internal
com.company.payment.domain.rule.internal
```

Rule:

```text
Jika class adalah business contract, export.
Jika class adalah implementation detail, jangan export.
```

---

## 6.2 API Module

API module berisi contract transport, bukan business logic.

```text
payment-api
│
├── request
│   └── CreatePaymentRequest
│
├── response
│   └── PaymentResponse
│
├── error
│   └── ErrorResponse
│
└── validation
    └── custom validation annotation, jika ada
```

`module-info.java`:

```java
module com.company.payment.api {
    exports com.company.payment.api.request;
    exports com.company.payment.api.response;
    exports com.company.payment.api.error;

    opens com.company.payment.api.request;
    opens com.company.payment.api.response;
    opens com.company.payment.api.error;
}
```

Kenapa DTO perlu `opens`? Karena serializer/deserializer dan validator sering membaca constructor, field, method, atau annotation lewat reflection. JPMS membedakan akses normal via `exports` dan akses reflection via `opens`. ([OpenJDK](https://openjdk.org/jeps/261?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 6.3 Application Module

Application module menjalankan use case.

```text
payment-application
│
├── usecase
│   ├── CreatePaymentUseCase
│   ├── GetPaymentUseCase
│   └── CancelPaymentUseCase
│
├── command
│   └── CreatePaymentCommand
│
├── query
│   └── GetPaymentQuery
│
└── mapper
    └── DomainToApplicationMapper
```

`module-info.java`:

```java
module com.company.payment.application {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    exports com.company.payment.application.usecase;
    exports com.company.payment.application.command;
    exports com.company.payment.application.query;
}
```

Rule:

```text
Application boleh orchestrate.
Application tidak boleh tahu JAX-RS resource.
Application tidak boleh tahu MyBatis mapper.
Application tidak boleh tahu PostgreSQL driver.
```

---

## 6.4 REST Module

REST module adalah adapter HTTP/JAX-RS.

Jakarta REST mendefinisikan resource class dan HTTP method designator seperti `@GET`, `@POST`, `@PUT`, `@DELETE`, dan `@HEAD`, sehingga package resource adalah boundary transport, bukan domain. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/websvcs/rest/rest.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```text
payment-rest
│
├── resource
│   └── PaymentResource
│
├── mapper
│   └── PaymentRestMapper
│
├── exception
│   └── PaymentExceptionMapper
│
├── filter
│   └── CorrelationIdFilter
│
└── provider
    └── ObjectMapperProvider / JsonbProvider
```

`module-info.java`:

```java
module com.company.payment.rest {
    requires jakarta.ws.rs;

    requires com.company.payment.api;
    requires com.company.payment.application;
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    exports com.company.payment.rest.resource;
    exports com.company.payment.rest.exception;

    opens com.company.payment.rest.resource;
    opens com.company.payment.rest.exception;
    opens com.company.payment.rest.provider;
}
```

Rule:

```text
REST boleh mapping HTTP request → application command.
REST tidak boleh call MyBatis mapper.
REST tidak boleh menjalankan business rule langsung.
REST tidak boleh membangun SQL.
```

---

## 6.5 Persistence MyBatis Module

Persistence module adalah adapter database.

```text
payment-persistence-mybatis
│
├── repository
│   └── MyBatisPaymentRepository
│
├── mapper
│   └── PaymentMapper
│
├── entity
│   └── PaymentRecord
│
├── typehandler
│   └── MoneyTypeHandler
│
└── resources
    └── mappers/PaymentMapper.xml
```

`module-info.java` konseptual:

```java
module com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    // requires org.mybatis;            // cek nama module aktual
    // requires org.postgresql.jdbc;    // cek nama module aktual

    opens com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.mapper;
    opens com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.entity;
    opens com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.typehandler;
}
```

Jangan export repository implementation jika tidak perlu:

```java
// Hindari jika tidak ada alasan kuat
exports com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.repository;
```

Lebih baik repository implementation diakses oleh bootstrap sebagai composition root, atau disediakan melalui SPI jika memang plugin-style.

---

## 6.6 Integration Module

Integration module menangani external system.

```text
payment-integration-gateway
│
├── client
│   └── PaymentGatewayHttpClient
│
├── dto
│   ├── GatewayChargeRequest
│   └── GatewayChargeResponse
│
├── adapter
│   └── PaymentGatewayAdapter
│
├── mapper
│   └── GatewayMapper
│
└── resilience
    └── RetryPolicy, TimeoutPolicy
```

Descriptor:

```java
module com.company.payment.integration.gateway {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    // requires java.net.http; jika pakai Java HttpClient

    opens com.company.payment.integration.gateway.dto;
}
```

Rule:

```text
Integration adapter boleh tahu external API.
Domain tidak boleh tahu external API.
Application cukup tahu port/interface.
```

---

## 6.7 Bootstrap Module

Bootstrap adalah composition root.

Jersey menyediakan `ResourceConfig` sebagai utility/configuration class untuk mengatur resource/provider dalam aplikasi Jersey. Ini membuat bootstrap module cocok menjadi tempat register resource, provider, filter, exception mapper, dan wiring dependency. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/apidocs/3.1.7/jersey/org/glassfish/jersey/server/ResourceConfig.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```text
payment-bootstrap
│
├── Main
├── PaymentApplicationConfig
├── DataSourceFactory
├── MyBatisFactory
├── RepositoryFactory
├── UseCaseFactory
└── JerseyServerFactory
```

Descriptor:

```java
module com.company.payment.bootstrap {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;
    requires com.company.payment.api;
    requires com.company.payment.application;
    requires com.company.payment.rest;
    requires com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis;
    requires com.company.payment.integration.gateway;

    // requires Jersey runtime modules; cek nama module aktual dari JAR
}
```

Rule:

```text
Bootstrap boleh tahu concrete implementation.
Bootstrap tidak berisi business rule.
Bootstrap tidak berisi SQL.
Bootstrap tidak berisi REST endpoint logic.
```

---

# 7. Example End-to-End Flow

## 7.1 Request Flow

```text
HTTP POST /payments
│
├── PaymentResource
│   └── maps CreatePaymentRequest → CreatePaymentCommand
│
├── CreatePaymentUseCase
│   ├── validates application-level rule
│   ├── calls domain model/factory
│   ├── calls PaymentRepository port
│   └── calls PaymentGateway port if needed
│
├── Domain
│   ├── enforces invariant
│   └── produces domain event
│
├── MyBatisPaymentRepository
│   └── persists to PostgreSQL
│
└── PaymentResponse
    └── returned by REST layer
```

## 7.2 Dependency Direction

```text
resource → usecase → domain port/model
repository implementation → domain port/model
bootstrap → resource/usecase/repository/integration
```

Yang dilarang:

```text
resource → MyBatis mapper
domain → resource
domain → JAX-RS Response
application → PostgreSQL driver
api DTO → persistence entity
```

---

# 8. Practical Code Skeleton

## 8.1 Domain Port

```java
package com.company.payment.domain.port;

import com.company.payment.domain.model.Payment;

import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.UUID;

public interface PaymentRepository {
    Optional<Payment> findById(UUID id);
    Payment save(Payment payment);
}
```

## 8.2 Domain Model

```java
package com.company.payment.domain.model;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.UUID;

public record Payment(
    UUID id,
    BigDecimal amount,
    String currency,
    PaymentStatus status
) {
    public Payment {
        if (amount == null || amount.signum() <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Payment amount must be positive");
        }
    }
}
```

## 8.3 Application Use Case

```java
package com.company.payment.application.usecase;

import com.company.payment.application.command.CreatePaymentCommand;
import com.company.payment.domain.model.Payment;
import com.company.payment.domain.model.PaymentStatus;
import com.company.payment.domain.port.PaymentRepository;

import java.util.UUID;

public class CreatePaymentUseCase {

    private final PaymentRepository repository;

    public CreatePaymentUseCase(PaymentRepository repository) {
        this.repository = repository;
    }

    public Payment execute(CreatePaymentCommand command) {
        var payment = new Payment(
            UUID.randomUUID(),
            command.amount(),
            command.currency(),
            PaymentStatus.CREATED
        );

        return repository.save(payment);
    }
}
```

## 8.4 API Request DTO

```java
package com.company.payment.api.request;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

public class CreatePaymentRequest {
    private BigDecimal amount;
    private String currency;

    public CreatePaymentRequest() {
    }

    public BigDecimal getAmount() {
        return amount;
    }

    public String getCurrency() {
        return currency;
    }
}
```

## 8.5 REST Resource

```java
package com.company.payment.rest.resource;

import com.company.payment.api.request.CreatePaymentRequest;
import com.company.payment.application.command.CreatePaymentCommand;
import com.company.payment.application.usecase.CreatePaymentUseCase;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Consumes;
import jakarta.ws.rs.POST;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Path;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Produces;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.MediaType;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;

@Path("/payments")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class PaymentResource {

    private final CreatePaymentUseCase createPaymentUseCase;

    public PaymentResource(CreatePaymentUseCase createPaymentUseCase) {
        this.createPaymentUseCase = createPaymentUseCase;
    }

    @POST
    public Response create(CreatePaymentRequest request) {
        var command = new CreatePaymentCommand(
            request.getAmount(),
            request.getCurrency()
        );

        var payment = createPaymentUseCase.execute(command);

        return Response
            .status(Response.Status.CREATED)
            .entity(payment)
            .build();
    }
}
```

Catatan praktis: mengembalikan domain model langsung sebagai response bisa diterima di contoh kecil, tetapi untuk production lebih baik mapping domain model ke response DTO agar contract API tidak terikat langsung ke internal domain shape.

---

# 9. Composition Root Example

## 9.1 Manual Wiring

```java
package com.company.payment.bootstrap;

import com.company.payment.application.usecase.CreatePaymentUseCase;
import com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.repository.MyBatisPaymentRepository;
import com.company.payment.rest.resource.PaymentResource;
import org.glassfish.jersey.server.ResourceConfig;

public class PaymentApplicationConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public PaymentApplicationConfig() {
        var repository = new MyBatisPaymentRepository();
        var createPaymentUseCase = new CreatePaymentUseCase(repository);
        var paymentResource = new PaymentResource(createPaymentUseCase);

        register(paymentResource);
    }
}
```

Jersey `ResourceConfig` memang digunakan untuk konfigurasi resource/provider aplikasi Jersey, sehingga wajar jika bootstrap module menjadi tempat konfigurasi ini. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/apidocs/3.1.7/jersey/org/glassfish/jersey/server/ResourceConfig.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 9.2 Kenapa Manual Wiring Berguna Saat Onboarding?

Manual wiring membantu melihat dependency nyata:

```text
Resource butuh use case.
Use case butuh repository port.
Repository implementation butuh MyBatis.
Bootstrap menyatukan semuanya.
```

Setelah boundary jelas, tim bisa memakai DI container/framework jika diperlukan. Tapi jangan sampai DI menyembunyikan dependency direction yang buruk.

---

# 10. Maven Layout Praktis

Maven multi-module mengatur aggregator/reactor build, sedangkan JPMS mengatur module readability dan encapsulation. Maven reactor menentukan build order berdasarkan hubungan antar project yang benar-benar dipakai sebagai dependency/plugin/extension, bukan sekadar daftar dependency management. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/guides/mini/guide-multiple-modules.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```text
payment-service/
├── pom.xml
├── payment-domain/
│   ├── pom.xml
│   └── src/main/java/module-info.java
├── payment-api/
│   ├── pom.xml
│   └── src/main/java/module-info.java
├── payment-application/
│   ├── pom.xml
│   └── src/main/java/module-info.java
├── payment-rest/
│   ├── pom.xml
│   └── src/main/java/module-info.java
├── payment-persistence-mybatis/
│   ├── pom.xml
│   └── src/main/java/module-info.java
└── payment-bootstrap/
    ├── pom.xml
    └── src/main/java/module-info.java
```

Root `pom.xml`:

```xml
<project>
    <modelVersion>4.0.0</modelVersion>

    <groupId>com.company.payment</groupId>
    <artifactId>payment-service-parent</artifactId>
    <version>1.0.0-SNAPSHOT</version>
    <packaging>pom</packaging>

    <modules>
        <module>payment-domain</module>
        <module>payment-api</module>
        <module>payment-application</module>
        <module>payment-rest</module>
        <module>payment-persistence-mybatis</module>
        <module>payment-bootstrap</module>
    </modules>

    <properties>
        <maven.compiler.release>17</maven.compiler.release>
        <project.build.sourceEncoding>UTF-8</project.build.sourceEncoding>
    </properties>
</project>
```

---

# 11. Boundary Rules as Code

## 11.1 REST Module

```java
module com.company.payment.rest {
    requires jakarta.ws.rs;
    requires com.company.payment.api;
    requires com.company.payment.application;

    exports com.company.payment.rest.resource;
    exports com.company.payment.rest.exception;

    opens com.company.payment.rest.resource;
    opens com.company.payment.rest.exception;
}
```

Tidak ada:

```java
requires com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis;
```

Artinya REST tidak bisa import MyBatis repository/mapper.

---

## 11.2 Application Module

```java
module com.company.payment.application {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    exports com.company.payment.application.usecase;
    exports com.company.payment.application.command;
}
```

Tidak ada:

```java
requires jakarta.ws.rs;
requires org.mybatis;
requires org.postgresql.jdbc;
```

Artinya use case tidak bergantung pada HTTP atau DB implementation.

---

## 11.3 Domain Module

```java
module com.company.payment.domain {
    exports com.company.payment.domain.model;
    exports com.company.payment.domain.valueobject;
    exports com.company.payment.domain.port;
    exports com.company.payment.domain.exception;
}
```

Tidak ada framework dependency.

---

## 11.4 Persistence Module

```java
module com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    opens com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.mapper;
    opens com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.entity;
}
```

Tidak perlu export mapper/entity jika tidak menjadi public contract.

---

# 12. Practical Failure Modes

| Failure mode | Gejala | Penyebab | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|---|
| REST import MyBatis mapper | Compile berhasil di classpath, boundary bocor | Maven dependency terlalu longgar | Hapus dependency persistence dari REST; wiring di bootstrap |
| Domain import `jakarta.ws.rs.*` | Domain tahu HTTP | Error model memakai `Response`, `Status`, annotation REST | Pindahkan mapping ke REST/application |
| API DTO reuse DB entity | API contract berubah saat schema DB berubah | Persistence entity dipakai sebagai response | Buat DTO khusus API |
| Semua package di-export | JPMS tidak memberi encapsulation nyata | Takut compile error | Export hanya package contract |
| Semua module dibuat `open module` | Reflection terlalu bebas | Framework issue ditambal kasar | Pakai `opens package` spesifik |
| Bootstrap logic tersebar | Object graph sulit dipahami | Resource membuat repository sendiri | Centralize di composition root |
| Common module jadi god module | Semua module depends common | Util campur domain/api/infra | Pecah common berdasarkan ownership |

---

# 13. Boundary Decision Matrix

| Pertanyaan | Keputusan |
|---|---|
| Apakah class ini bagian dari business invariant? | Taruh di `domain` |
| Apakah class ini request/response HTTP contract? | Taruh di `api` |
| Apakah class ini menjalankan use case? | Taruh di `application` |
| Apakah class ini JAX-RS endpoint/filter/provider? | Taruh di `rest` |
| Apakah class ini mapper SQL/repository implementation? | Taruh di `persistence-mybatis` |
| Apakah class ini call external system? | Taruh di `integration-*` |
| Apakah class ini menyusun object graph/runtime? | Taruh di `bootstrap` |
| Apakah package ini perlu dipakai module lain? | `exports` |
| Apakah package ini hanya perlu reflection? | `opens` |
| Apakah dependency hanya untuk composition root? | Taruh di `bootstrap`, bukan semua layer |

---

# 14. Practical Lab

## Lab 1 - Buat Boundary Skeleton

Buat module:

```text
payment-domain
payment-api
payment-application
payment-rest
payment-persistence-mybatis
payment-bootstrap
```

Pastikan setiap module punya `module-info.java`.

---

## Lab 2 - Cegah REST ke Persistence

Di `payment-rest`, jangan tambahkan dependency Maven ke `payment-persistence-mybatis`.

Lalu coba:

```java
import com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.repository.MyBatisPaymentRepository;
```

Expected:

```text
Compile gagal.
```

Lesson:

```text
REST harus lewat application use case, bukan repository implementation.
```

---

## Lab 3 - Cegah Domain ke JAX-RS

Di `payment-domain`, coba import:

```java
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
```

Expected:

```text
Compile gagal karena domain module tidak requires jakarta.ws.rs.
```

Lesson:

```text
Domain tidak boleh tahu transport protocol.
```

---

## Lab 4 - Pisahkan Domain Model dan API DTO

Buat:

```text
domain.model.Payment
api.response.PaymentResponse
rest.mapper.PaymentRestMapper
```

Jangan expose domain model langsung sebagai API response.

Contoh mapper:

```java
package com.company.payment.rest.mapper;

import com.company.payment.api.response.PaymentResponse;
import com.company.payment.domain.model.Payment;

public class PaymentRestMapper {

    public PaymentResponse toResponse(Payment payment) {
        return new PaymentResponse(
            payment.id().toString(),
            payment.amount(),
            payment.currency(),
            payment.status().name()
        );
    }
}
```

Lesson:

```text
API contract stabil meskipun domain model berubah.
```

---

## Lab 5 - Centralize Wiring di Bootstrap

Pindahkan semua `new MyBatisPaymentRepository()`, `new CreatePaymentUseCase()`, dan `new PaymentResource()` ke bootstrap/config class.

Expected:

```text
Resource tidak membuat repository sendiri.
Use case tidak tahu concrete repository.
Bootstrap menjadi satu-satunya tempat wiring.
```

---

# 15. Review Checklist untuk PR

| Check | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Dependency direction | Apakah dependency mengalir REST → application → domain? |
| Domain purity | Apakah domain bebas dari JAX-RS, Jersey, MyBatis, PostgreSQL, cloud SDK? |
| API contract | Apakah API DTO tidak memakai DB entity? |
| Persistence isolation | Apakah mapper/entity tidak diekspor sembarangan? |
| Bootstrap ownership | Apakah wiring concrete implementation ada di bootstrap? |
| Reflection control | Apakah `opens` hanya untuk package yang butuh reflection? |
| Export minimality | Apakah `exports` hanya package contract? |
| Testability | Apakah use case bisa dites dengan fake repository? |
| Failure isolation | Jika DB berubah, apakah REST contract tetap aman? |
| Runtime clarity | Apakah startup mode classpath/module-path jelas? |

---

# 16. Recommended Boundary Standard

Untuk tim enterprise, saya sarankan standard minimum berikut:

```text
1. Domain module tidak boleh depends ke framework.
2. REST module tidak boleh depends ke persistence module.
3. Application module tidak boleh depends ke MyBatis/PostgreSQL/Jersey.
4. API DTO tidak boleh reuse persistence entity.
5. Persistence implementation tidak boleh diexport sebagai public API.
6. Bootstrap adalah satu-satunya layer yang boleh tahu semua concrete implementation.
7. Setiap `exports` harus punya alasan contract.
8. Setiap `opens` harus punya alasan reflection.
9. Setiap `requires` harus merepresentasikan dependency direction yang sah.
```

---

# 17. Roadmap Alur Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Output praktis |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Boundary mental model | Bisa menjelaskan ownership tiap module |
| 2 | Module graph | Bisa menggambar dependency antar module |
| 3 | Package ownership | Bisa menentukan package mana export/open/internal |
| 4 | REST/application/domain split | Resource tidak bypass use case |
| 5 | Persistence isolation | MyBatis tidak bocor ke REST/domain |
| 6 | Composition root | Bootstrap menyusun object graph |
| 7 | PR guardrail | Review dependency direction secara konsisten |
