## Response:
7/15/2026, 1:20:41 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 5/12**. Fokusnya praktik JPMS saat bertemu Jersey/JAX-RS: resource registration, provider/filter/exception mapper, reflection, `opens`, dan keputusan classpath vs module path.

# Seri 5/12 - JPMS with Jersey/JAX-RS

## 1. Introduction

Seri ini membahas bagaimana **JPMS dipakai secara praktis pada module REST** dalam Java microservice berbasis **Jersey/JAX-RS**.

Di seri sebelumnya kita sudah punya boundary seperti ini:

```text id="yq7ui2"
payment-rest
│
├── JAX-RS resource
├── exception mapper
├── filter
├── provider
├── request/response mapper
└── depends on application + api DTO
```

Masalahnya, Jersey/JAX-RS bukan sekadar compile-time API. Resource, provider, filter, exception mapper, serializer, validator, dan dependency injection sering melibatkan **runtime discovery** dan **reflection**. Karena itu JPMS harus dipakai dengan disiplin: `exports` untuk contract compile-time, `opens` untuk reflective access, dan jangan asal menjadikan semua module `open module`.

Jakarta RESTful Web Services adalah API standar Jakarta untuk membangun web service bergaya REST. Versi 4.0 tersedia sebagai bagian dari ekosistem Jakarta EE 11, dan artifact `jakarta.ws.rs-api` versi 4.0.0 sudah tersedia di Maven Central; tetapi banyak aplikasi Jersey enterprise masih berada di jalur Jersey/Jakarta REST 3.x, jadi versi aktual project tetap harus dicek dari `pom.xml` dan dependency tree. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Core Problem: JPMS vs Framework Runtime Discovery

JPMS ingin dependency eksplisit:

```text id="2gjtl1"
module A reads module B only if A requires B.
package P is visible only if module B exports P.
reflection works only if package is opened.
```

Jersey/JAX-RS biasanya bekerja dengan pola:

```text id="9okuld"
1. Scan resource class
2. Detect @Path, @GET, @POST, @Provider
3. Instantiate resource/provider/filter
4. Bind dependency or context
5. Serialize/deserialize request/response
6. Map exception to HTTP response
```

Itulah kenapa module REST perlu desain khusus:

```text id="84n6l5"
payment-rest
│
├── requires jakarta.ws.rs          → supaya annotation/API JAX-RS bisa dipakai
├── requires payment-api            → request/response DTO
├── requires payment-application    → use case
├── exports resource/provider       → jika perlu dilihat compile-time
└── opens resource/provider/dto     → jika Jersey/serializer butuh reflection
```

---

# 3. Diagram

```text id="rwn1b7"
HTTP Request
│
▼
Jersey Runtime
│
├── Finds resource class
│   └── @Path("/payments")
│
├── Invokes resource method
│   └── @GET / @POST / @PUT / @DELETE
│
├── Deserializes request body
│   └── CreatePaymentRequest
│
├── Calls application use case
│   └── CreatePaymentUseCase
│
├── Handles exception if thrown
│   └── ExceptionMapper
│
├── Serializes response
│   └── PaymentResponse
│
└── Sends HTTP response

JPMS impact points
│
├── requires jakarta.ws.rs
├── exports REST resource package if needed
├── opens REST resource package for Jersey reflection
├── opens DTO package for JSON binding
├── opens exception mapper package for provider discovery
└── avoid REST → persistence dependency
```

---

# 4. Decomposition Map

| Area | Practical skill | JPMS concern | Jersey/JAX-RS concern | Recommended practice |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Jakarta REST API dependency | Use annotations/API like `@Path`, `@GET`, `Response` | `requires jakarta.ws.rs` | Compile against Jakarta REST API | Keep API dependency in REST module, not domain |
| Jersey runtime dependency | Run resource/provider with Jersey implementation | Need actual Jersey module names if module-path runtime | Jersey server registers resources/providers | Validate names with `jar --describe-module` |
| Resource class | Endpoint class | May need `exports` and/or `opens` | Jersey discovers and invokes resource | Prefer explicit registration in JPMS-sensitive runtime |
| Resource method | HTTP operation | Needs `jakarta.ws.rs` readable | `@GET`, `@POST`, etc. | Keep method thin; delegate to use case |
| Request DTO | Input contract | `exports` for compile usage, `opens` for JSON reflection | Deserialization | Put DTO in API module |
| Response DTO | Output contract | `exports` + maybe `opens` | Serialization | Do not return DB entity |
| Exception mapper | Error mapping | Usually `opens` for provider discovery | `ExceptionMapper<T>` + `@Provider` | Keep in REST module |
| Filter/interceptor | Cross-cutting HTTP concern | Usually `opens` if discovered reflectively | Auth, correlation ID, logging | Keep transport concern out of domain |
| Provider | JSON, context, feature provider | Usually `opens` | Runtime extension | Register explicitly where possible |
| Package scanning | Runtime classpath/module scanning | Can break under module boundaries | Jersey `packages(...)` scans package recursively | Use cautiously; explicit `register(...)` is easier to reason about |
| ResourceConfig | Jersey application config | Usually in bootstrap module | Registers resources/providers/properties | Use as composition root boundary |
| Classpath runtime | Compatibility mode | JPMS runtime encapsulation weaker | Usually easiest for legacy framework apps | Good migration baseline |
| Module-path runtime | Strict mode | Needs correct module graph and `opens` | More sensitive to discovery/reflection | Use after dependency audit |

---

# 5. Jakarta REST API vs Jersey Implementation

## 5.1 Jakarta REST API

This is the standard API layer:

```java id="ztpdqn"
import jakarta.ws.rs.GET;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Path;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Produces;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.MediaType;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
```

JPMS descriptor:

```java id="s7z5xj"
module com.company.payment.rest {
    requires jakarta.ws.rs;
}
```

`jakarta.ws.rs` is the module name used by the Jakarta REST API module in the official API documentation. ([JavaDoc](https://javadoc.io/doc/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta.ws.rs-api/latest/index.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 5.2 Jersey Implementation

Jersey is the runtime implementation that actually handles resource registration, request dispatching, providers, filters, and configuration. Jersey’s `ResourceConfig` is a central configuration class for Jersey server applications, and the Jersey 3.1.x API docs show methods for registering resources/classes/instances and scanning packages. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/apidocs/3.1.7/jersey/org/glassfish/jersey/server/ResourceConfig.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Important distinction:

```text id="f8wqmh"
jakarta.ws.rs-api
│
└── API only: annotations, interfaces, Response, Application, etc.

Jersey
│
└── Runtime implementation: ResourceConfig, server runtime, provider handling, request dispatch
```

Practical implication:

```text id="7lsizm"
REST module usually requires jakarta.ws.rs.
Bootstrap/runtime module usually requires Jersey implementation modules.
```

Do not guess Jersey module names. Check actual JARs:

```bash id="jb82qj"
jar --describe-module --file ~/.m2/repository/org/glassfish/jersey/core/jersey-server/*/jersey-server-*.jar
```

---

# 6. Resource Registration Strategies

## 6.1 Strategy A - Explicit Class Registration

```java id="xr1wiv"
package com.company.payment.bootstrap;

import com.company.payment.rest.resource.PaymentResource;
import org.glassfish.jersey.server.ResourceConfig;

public class PaymentApplicationConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public PaymentApplicationConfig() {
        register(PaymentResource.class);
    }
}
```

Pros:

```text id="zxbt5l"
1. Easy to reason about.
2. Less magic than package scanning.
3. Better for JPMS migration.
4. Fewer surprises from hidden resource discovery.
```

Cons:

```text id="66bpc3"
1. More manual registration.
2. Need to remember each resource/provider.
```

Recommended for JPMS-sensitive migration:

```text id="6rxxmu"
Prefer explicit registration first.
Use package scanning only when the team understands the module/reflection impact.
```

---

## 6.2 Strategy B - Explicit Instance Registration

```java id="7c30ka"
package com.company.payment.bootstrap;

import com.company.payment.application.usecase.CreatePaymentUseCase;
import com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.repository.MyBatisPaymentRepository;
import com.company.payment.rest.resource.PaymentResource;
import org.glassfish.jersey.server.ResourceConfig;

public class PaymentApplicationConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public PaymentApplicationConfig() {
        var repository = new MyBatisPaymentRepository();
        var useCase = new CreatePaymentUseCase(repository);
        var resource = new PaymentResource(useCase);

        register(resource);
    }
}
```

Pros:

```text id="p8hqcx"
1. Very explicit object graph.
2. Good for onboarding.
3. Avoids hidden DI assumptions.
4. Makes dependency direction visible.
```

Cons:

```text id="enkc3y"
1. Manual wiring grows quickly.
2. Needs factory/composition root structure for larger services.
```

This pattern is useful when you want to prove:

```text id="pd3wwr"
REST → Application → Domain Port
Bootstrap → Concrete Repository Implementation
```

---

## 6.3 Strategy C - Package Scanning

```java id="g4o39w"
package com.company.payment.bootstrap;

import org.glassfish.jersey.server.ResourceConfig;

public class PaymentApplicationConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public PaymentApplicationConfig() {
        packages("com.company.payment.rest");
    }
}
```

Jersey `ResourceConfig` has package scanning support, and the 3.1.9 API docs note that package scanning is recursive and ignores inherited `@Path` annotations on parent classes/interfaces. This means package scanning behavior is not just “find everything magically”; its rules matter. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/apidocs/3.1.9/jersey/org/glassfish/jersey/server/ResourceConfig.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Pros:

```text id="gct1zh"
1. Less boilerplate.
2. Familiar to many Jersey projects.
3. Easy for small apps.
```

Cons in JPMS context:

```text id="dq1os1"
1. More reflection/discovery sensitivity.
2. Can hide missing registration.
3. Package visibility/opening issues become harder to debug.
4. Can accidentally register classes you did not intend.
```

Practical recommendation:

```text id="8fna9x"
For strict JPMS migration, start with explicit register().
After stable, decide whether package scanning is worth the convenience.
```

---

# 7. `exports` vs `opens` for Jersey

## 7.1 Resource Package

Resource:

```java id="cmi6c2"
package com.company.payment.rest.resource;

import jakarta.ws.rs.GET;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Path;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;

@Path("/payments")
public class PaymentResource {

    @GET
    public Response health() {
        return Response.ok("OK").build();
    }
}
```

Descriptor option:

```java id="5dtxbf"
module com.company.payment.rest {
    requires jakarta.ws.rs;
    requires com.company.payment.application;
    requires com.company.payment.api;

    exports com.company.payment.rest.resource;

    opens com.company.payment.rest.resource;
}
```

Meaning:

| Directive | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `exports com.company.payment.rest.resource` | Other modules can access public resource class normally |
| `opens com.company.payment.rest.resource` | Jersey can use reflection if needed |

If only bootstrap needs to instantiate the resource, you can narrow the export:

```java id="05oiud"
module com.company.payment.rest {
    requires jakarta.ws.rs;
    requires com.company.payment.application;
    requires com.company.payment.api;

    exports com.company.payment.rest.resource
        to com.company.payment.bootstrap;

    opens com.company.payment.rest.resource;
}
```

---

## 7.2 DTO Package

DTO package should usually live in `payment-api`.

```java id="5f5g6p"
module com.company.payment.api {
    exports com.company.payment.api.request;
    exports com.company.payment.api.response;
    exports com.company.payment.api.error;

    opens com.company.payment.api.request;
    opens com.company.payment.api.response;
    opens com.company.payment.api.error;
}
```

Why both?

```text id="72z8fe"
exports → REST module can compile against DTO types.
opens   → JSON serializer/deserializer can reflect into DTOs.
```

More defensive form if target module names are known:

```java id="ui2lsn"
module com.company.payment.api {
    exports com.company.payment.api.request;
    exports com.company.payment.api.response;
    exports com.company.payment.api.error;

    opens com.company.payment.api.request to com.fasterxml.jackson.databind;
    opens com.company.payment.api.response to com.fasterxml.jackson.databind;
}
```

But do not guess `com.fasterxml.jackson.databind`. Validate actual module name from the JAR used by the project.

---

## 7.3 Exception Mapper Package

Exception mapper:

```java id="o6qt3i"
package com.company.payment.rest.exception;

import com.company.payment.domain.exception.PaymentNotFoundException;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.ExceptionMapper;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

@Provider
public class PaymentNotFoundExceptionMapper
        implements ExceptionMapper<PaymentNotFoundException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(PaymentNotFoundException exception) {
        return Response.status(Response.Status.NOT_FOUND)
            .entity(new ErrorResponse("PAYMENT_NOT_FOUND", exception.getMessage()))
            .build();
    }
}
```

Descriptor:

```java id="sp72gr"
module com.company.payment.rest {
    requires jakarta.ws.rs;
    requires com.company.payment.domain;
    requires com.company.payment.api;

    exports com.company.payment.rest.exception
        to com.company.payment.bootstrap;

    opens com.company.payment.rest.exception;
}
```

If registered explicitly:

```java id="x8ykpj"
register(PaymentNotFoundExceptionMapper.class);
```

you may still need `opens` depending on Jersey’s instantiation/reflection path.

---

## 7.4 Filter Package

Filter:

```java id="xf6c6j"
package com.company.payment.rest.filter;

import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

import java.io.IOException;

@Provider
public class CorrelationIdFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext) throws IOException {
        var correlationId = requestContext.getHeaderString("X-Correlation-Id");
        // attach to logging context
    }
}
```

Descriptor:

```java id="rup2ui"
module com.company.payment.rest {
    requires jakarta.ws.rs;

    exports com.company.payment.rest.filter
        to com.company.payment.bootstrap;

    opens com.company.payment.rest.filter;
}
```

Rule:

```text id="gjicwn"
HTTP filter = REST module concern.
Do not place it in domain/application.
```

---

# 8. Recommended `payment-rest` Module Layout

```text id="mtxod5"
payment-rest/
└── src/main/java/
    ├── module-info.java
    └── com/company/payment/rest/
        ├── resource/
        │   └── PaymentResource.java
        │
        ├── mapper/
        │   └── PaymentRestMapper.java
        │
        ├── exception/
        │   ├── PaymentExceptionMapper.java
        │   └── GenericExceptionMapper.java
        │
        ├── filter/
        │   ├── CorrelationIdFilter.java
        │   └── AuthenticationFilter.java
        │
        └── provider/
            └── JsonProvider.java
```

Descriptor baseline:

```java id="mtzcu4"
module com.company.payment.rest {
    requires jakarta.ws.rs;

    requires com.company.payment.api;
    requires com.company.payment.application;
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    exports com.company.payment.rest.resource
        to com.company.payment.bootstrap;

    exports com.company.payment.rest.exception
        to com.company.payment.bootstrap;

    exports com.company.payment.rest.filter
        to com.company.payment.bootstrap;

    opens com.company.payment.rest.resource;
    opens com.company.payment.rest.exception;
    opens com.company.payment.rest.filter;
    opens com.company.payment.rest.provider;
}
```

Production note:

```text id="7vm4mw"
If qualified opens target module names are known and stable,
prefer opens package to specific.module instead of unqualified opens.
```

---

# 9. Recommended `payment-bootstrap` Layout

```text id="q4x67f"
payment-bootstrap/
└── src/main/java/
    └── com/company/payment/bootstrap/
        ├── Main.java
        ├── PaymentApplicationConfig.java
        ├── PaymentObjectGraph.java
        ├── DataSourceFactory.java
        ├── MyBatisFactory.java
        └── ServerFactory.java
```

`PaymentObjectGraph`:

```java id="83qdzb"
package com.company.payment.bootstrap;

import com.company.payment.application.usecase.CreatePaymentUseCase;
import com.company.payment.domain.port.PaymentRepository;
import com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.repository.MyBatisPaymentRepository;
import com.company.payment.rest.mapper.PaymentRestMapper;
import com.company.payment.rest.resource.PaymentResource;

public class PaymentObjectGraph {

    public PaymentResource paymentResource() {
        PaymentRepository repository = new MyBatisPaymentRepository();
        var useCase = new CreatePaymentUseCase(repository);
        var mapper = new PaymentRestMapper();

        return new PaymentResource(useCase, mapper);
    }
}
```

`PaymentApplicationConfig`:

```java id="ntegbl"
package com.company.payment.bootstrap;

import com.company.payment.rest.exception.GenericExceptionMapper;
import com.company.payment.rest.exception.PaymentNotFoundExceptionMapper;
import com.company.payment.rest.filter.CorrelationIdFilter;
import org.glassfish.jersey.server.ResourceConfig;

public class PaymentApplicationConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public PaymentApplicationConfig() {
        var graph = new PaymentObjectGraph();

        register(graph.paymentResource());
        register(PaymentNotFoundExceptionMapper.class);
        register(GenericExceptionMapper.class);
        register(CorrelationIdFilter.class);
    }
}
```

This keeps Jersey runtime configuration inside bootstrap, while resource logic remains in `payment-rest`.

---

# 10. `payment-bootstrap` Descriptor

Conceptual:

```java id="0915gb"
module com.company.payment.bootstrap {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;
    requires com.company.payment.api;
    requires com.company.payment.application;
    requires com.company.payment.rest;
    requires com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis;

    // Jersey module names must be checked from actual JARs.
    // requires org.glassfish.jersey.server;
}
```

Because Jersey module names can depend on the actual artifact/version and whether JARs are explicit or automatic modules, validate first:

```bash id="t3ebx5"
mvn -pl payment-bootstrap dependency:copy-dependencies \
  -DincludeScope=runtime \
  -DoutputDirectory=target/dependency

for j in payment-bootstrap/target/dependency/*.jar; do
  echo "==== $j"
  jar --describe-module --file "$j" 2>/dev/null | head -30
done
```

---

# 11. Resource Class Design Rules

## 11.1 Keep Resource Thin

Bad:

```java id="9r6res"
@Path("/payments")
public class PaymentResource {

    @POST
    public Response create(CreatePaymentRequest request) {
        // validate domain invariant
        // open SQL session
        // call MyBatis mapper
        // build payment status transition
        // call external gateway
        // handle retry
        // return response
    }
}
```

Good:

```java id="maz9gm"
@Path("/payments")
public class PaymentResource {

    private final CreatePaymentUseCase createPaymentUseCase;
    private final PaymentRestMapper mapper;

    public PaymentResource(
            CreatePaymentUseCase createPaymentUseCase,
            PaymentRestMapper mapper
    ) {
        this.createPaymentUseCase = createPaymentUseCase;
        this.mapper = mapper;
    }

    @POST
    public Response create(CreatePaymentRequest request) {
        var command = mapper.toCommand(request);
        var result = createPaymentUseCase.execute(command);
        var response = mapper.toResponse(result);

        return Response.status(Response.Status.CREATED)
            .entity(response)
            .build();
    }
}
```

REST resource responsibilities:

```text id="pyq804"
1. HTTP input mapping
2. HTTP response mapping
3. Status code choice
4. Header handling
5. Delegation to use case
```

Not resource responsibilities:

```text id="zt7mc8"
1. SQL
2. transaction internals
3. domain invariant implementation
4. external gateway retry orchestration
5. persistence entity mapping
```

---

# 12. DTO Design with JPMS

## 12.1 Request DTO

```java id="3xchuf"
package com.company.payment.api.request;

import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotNull;
import java.math.BigDecimal;

public class CreatePaymentRequest {

    @NotNull
    private BigDecimal amount;

    @NotBlank
    private String currency;

    public CreatePaymentRequest() {
    }

    public BigDecimal getAmount() {
        return amount;
    }

    public String getCurrency() {
        return currency;
    }
}
```

If validation annotations are used in DTO, the API module may need the Jakarta Validation API dependency at compile time. Jakarta EE 11 includes Validation 3.1 in the platform profile, but project dependency versions still need to match the runtime stack used by the service. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/release/11/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Descriptor:

```java id="yz3j3f"
module com.company.payment.api {
    requires jakarta.validation;

    exports com.company.payment.api.request;
    exports com.company.payment.api.response;

    opens com.company.payment.api.request;
    opens com.company.payment.api.response;
}
```

If your project does not use Jakarta Validation in DTOs, do not add `requires jakarta.validation`.

---

## 12.2 Response DTO

```java id="3a2imo"
package com.company.payment.api.response;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

public record PaymentResponse(
    String id,
    BigDecimal amount,
    String currency,
    String status
) {
}
```

Records are often easier for immutable response DTOs, but serializer support depends on the JSON provider/version configured in the project. Validate with an integration test rather than assuming.

---

# 13. Exception Mapping Pattern

## 13.1 Domain Exception

```java id="09cmpw"
package com.company.payment.domain.exception;

public class PaymentNotFoundException extends RuntimeException {

    public PaymentNotFoundException(String paymentId) {
        super("Payment not found: " + paymentId);
    }
}
```

Domain descriptor:

```java id="8t8qpl"
module com.company.payment.domain {
    exports com.company.payment.domain.exception;
}
```

## 13.2 API Error Response

```java id="i5iyom"
package com.company.payment.api.error;

public record ErrorResponse(
    String code,
    String message
) {
}
```

API descriptor:

```java id="ew3499"
module com.company.payment.api {
    exports com.company.payment.api.error;

    opens com.company.payment.api.error;
}
```

## 13.3 REST Exception Mapper

```java id="v09369"
package com.company.payment.rest.exception;

import com.company.payment.api.error.ErrorResponse;
import com.company.payment.domain.exception.PaymentNotFoundException;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.ExceptionMapper;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

@Provider
public class PaymentNotFoundExceptionMapper
        implements ExceptionMapper<PaymentNotFoundException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(PaymentNotFoundException exception) {
        return Response.status(Response.Status.NOT_FOUND)
            .entity(new ErrorResponse("PAYMENT_NOT_FOUND", exception.getMessage()))
            .build();
    }
}
```

REST descriptor:

```java id="7he134"
module com.company.payment.rest {
    requires jakarta.ws.rs;
    requires com.company.payment.api;
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    exports com.company.payment.rest.exception
        to com.company.payment.bootstrap;

    opens com.company.payment.rest.exception;
}
```

Rule:

```text id="fuv03s"
Domain decides what went wrong.
REST decides how it maps to HTTP.
API module defines error response contract.
```

---

# 14. Provider and Feature Registration

Provider classes are commonly used for:

```text id="jgapc8"
1. JSON provider customization
2. Exception mapping
3. Request/response filters
4. Entity providers
5. Custom context injection
6. Features
```

Explicit registration pattern:

```java id="qg3bi5"
public class PaymentApplicationConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public PaymentApplicationConfig() {
        register(PaymentResource.class);
        register(PaymentNotFoundExceptionMapper.class);
        register(CorrelationIdFilter.class);
        register(JsonProvider.class);
    }
}
```

Package scanning pattern:

```java id="0lcpel"
public class PaymentApplicationConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public PaymentApplicationConfig() {
        packages("com.company.payment.rest");
    }
}
```

Because Jersey supports both registration and package scanning through `ResourceConfig`, the choice is architectural, not just technical. For JPMS, explicit registration usually produces clearer failure modes. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/apidocs/3.1.7/jersey/org/glassfish/jersey/server/ResourceConfig.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 15. Classpath vs Module Path Strategy for Jersey

## 15.1 Classpath Runtime

```bash id="ept2q7"
java -jar payment-service.jar
```

or:

```bash id="as1vcm"
java -cp "app.jar:lib/*" com.company.payment.bootstrap.Main
```

Characteristics:

```text id="9j9mhe"
1. Highest compatibility.
2. Common for enterprise Jersey apps.
3. JPMS descriptors may still help compile-time structure.
4. Runtime strong encapsulation is not fully exercised if everything is classpath/unnamed module.
```

Good when:

```text id="uhj7sn"
1. Existing app uses many reflection-heavy libraries.
2. Team is still cleaning dependency graph.
3. Docker/Kubernetes deployment is already classpath/fat-jar based.
4. Goal is first to enforce source/build boundary, not strict runtime modularity.
```

---

## 15.2 Module Path Runtime

```bash id="9rs4eq"
java \
  --module-path "mods:lib" \
  --module com.company.payment.bootstrap/com.company.payment.bootstrap.Main
```

Characteristics:

```text id="4aap2u"
1. Stricter module graph.
2. Better encapsulation.
3. Requires correct module names.
4. Requires opens for reflection-heavy packages.
5. Exposes split package and automatic module issues earlier.
```

Good when:

```text id="xj43qi"
1. Internal modules are already clean.
2. Dependency graph is audited.
3. Framework packages needing reflection are known.
4. Integration tests pass on module-path runtime.
```

---

# 16. Recommended Migration Path for Jersey + JPMS

```text id="o8ol4k"
Stage 1: Keep runtime classpath, add module-info to clean internal modules.
Stage 2: Modularize domain/application/api.
Stage 3: Add REST module descriptor with minimal exports/opens.
Stage 4: Replace package scanning with explicit registration where possible.
Stage 5: Audit Jersey/Jackson/JSON-B/MyBatis module names.
Stage 6: Run integration tests with module path.
Stage 7: Reduce unqualified opens into qualified opens.
Stage 8: Consider strict module-path runtime only after stability.
```

Do not start with:

```text id="t3es17"
"Move entire Jersey service to module path in one PR."
```

Better:

```text id="agz3n8"
"Make boundaries correct first, then tighten runtime."
```

---

# 17. Practical Failure Modes

| Failure mode | Symptom | Likely cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|
| Resource not found | 404 for expected endpoint | Resource not registered or package scan missed it | Explicitly `register(PaymentResource.class)` |
| Provider not applied | Exception not mapped | Mapper not registered or not discovered | Explicitly register mapper |
| Reflection access error | Runtime `IllegalAccessException` / inaccessible object | Package not opened | Add targeted `opens` |
| DTO deserialization fails | Empty object/null fields/error | DTO package not opened, no constructor, serializer mismatch | Add `opens`, verify DTO shape |
| Resource constructor fails | Jersey cannot instantiate resource | Constructor injection not configured | Register instance or configure DI binding |
| REST imports mapper | Architecture leak | REST module depends on persistence | Remove Maven dependency and JPMS `requires` |
| Works in IDE, fails in Docker | Different classpath/module path | Runtime command differs | Align local/CI/container startup |
| Works in classpath, fails module path | Missing `requires`/`opens`, split package, automatic module issue | JPMS strictness exposed | Audit with `jdeps` and `jar --describe-module` |

---

# 18. Practical Checks and Commands

## 18.1 Check Dependency Tree

```bash id="q14g4y"
mvn -pl payment-rest dependency:tree
```

Expected:

```text id="f82v68"
payment-rest should include:
- payment-api
- payment-application
- jakarta.ws.rs-api
- maybe Jersey API/runtime dependency depending module layout

payment-rest should not include:
- payment-persistence-mybatis
- PostgreSQL driver directly
```

---

## 18.2 Check Jersey Dependencies

```bash id="8xlupy"
mvn dependency:tree -Dincludes=org.glassfish.jersey
```

---

## 18.3 Copy Runtime Dependencies

```bash id="qozolk"
mvn -pl payment-bootstrap dependency:copy-dependencies \
  -DincludeScope=runtime \
  -DoutputDirectory=target/dependency
```

---

## 18.4 Inspect Module Names

```bash id="8fhh1i"
for j in payment-bootstrap/target/dependency/*.jar; do
  echo "==== $j"
  jar --describe-module --file "$j" 2>/dev/null | head -30
done
```

Use this before writing:

```java id="r3nuae"
requires some.third.party.module;
opens some.package to some.third.party.module;
```

---

## 18.5 Analyze Module Dependencies

```bash id="tp4v6d"
jdeps \
  --multi-release 17 \
  --print-module-deps \
  payment-rest/target/payment-rest-1.0.0-SNAPSHOT.jar
```

JEP 261 defines options such as module path and `--add-opens`/`--add-exports` as part of the JPMS toolchain; use these as diagnostics or migration bridges, not as a substitute for proper `module-info.java` design. ([OpenJDK](https://openjdk.org/jeps/261?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 19. Practical Module Descriptor Set

## 19.1 `payment-api`

```java id="9ydotd"
module com.company.payment.api {
    exports com.company.payment.api.request;
    exports com.company.payment.api.response;
    exports com.company.payment.api.error;

    opens com.company.payment.api.request;
    opens com.company.payment.api.response;
    opens com.company.payment.api.error;
}
```

## 19.2 `payment-application`

```java id="y48pag"
module com.company.payment.application {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    exports com.company.payment.application.command;
    exports com.company.payment.application.usecase;
}
```

## 19.3 `payment-rest`

```java id="z1htds"
module com.company.payment.rest {
    requires jakarta.ws.rs;

    requires com.company.payment.api;
    requires com.company.payment.application;
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    exports com.company.payment.rest.resource
        to com.company.payment.bootstrap;

    exports com.company.payment.rest.exception
        to com.company.payment.bootstrap;

    exports com.company.payment.rest.filter
        to com.company.payment.bootstrap;

    opens com.company.payment.rest.resource;
    opens com.company.payment.rest.exception;
    opens com.company.payment.rest.filter;
    opens com.company.payment.rest.provider;
}
```

## 19.4 `payment-bootstrap`

```java id="008q31"
module com.company.payment.bootstrap {
    requires com.company.payment.api;
    requires com.company.payment.application;
    requires com.company.payment.domain;
    requires com.company.payment.rest;
    requires com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis;

    // Add actual Jersey runtime module names after checking JARs.
}
```

# 20. Practical Lab

## Lab 1 - Explicit Resource Registration

Create:

```text id="fr8p69"
payment-rest.resource.PaymentResource
payment-bootstrap.PaymentApplicationConfig
```

Register explicitly:

```java id="58gn9u"
register(PaymentResource.class);
```

Expected:

```text id="2njsvp"
Endpoint is available without relying on package scanning.
```

---

## Lab 2 - Prove Package Scanning Difference

Replace explicit registration:

```java id="gy8kpu"
register(PaymentResource.class);
```

with:

```java id="c6jqk6"
packages("com.company.payment.rest");
```

Expected:

```text id="898fjf"
Endpoint still works if package scanning sees the resource.
```

Then move resource to another package without updating scan path.

Expected:

```text id="3s533a"
Endpoint disappears or returns 404.
```

Lesson:

```text id="przpa9"
Package scanning is convenient but less explicit.
```

---

## Lab 3 - Remove `opens` from DTO

Remove:

```java id="21mpm0"
opens com.company.payment.api.request;
```

Run integration test that deserializes JSON request.

Possible expected result:

```text id="iqcq54"
If serializer needs reflection, request deserialization fails.
If serializer can work through public accessors/constructors, it may still pass.
```

Lesson:

```text id="7or23h"
Do not guess. Validate DTO reflection requirement with integration tests.
```

---

## Lab 4 - Remove REST → Persistence Access

Try to add this in `PaymentResource`:

```java id="qcvvmb"
import com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.repository.MyBatisPaymentRepository;
```

Expected:

```text id="7kiwwg"
Compile fails if payment-rest has no Maven dependency and no JPMS requires to persistence.
```

Lesson:

```text id="g2n2f8"
JPMS + Maven dependency discipline prevents REST from bypassing use cases.
```

---

## Lab 5 - Register Exception Mapper Explicitly

```java id="9l2prv"
register(PaymentNotFoundExceptionMapper.class);
```

Trigger `PaymentNotFoundException`.

Expected:

```text id="9t6e9k"
HTTP 404 with ErrorResponse.
```

Remove registration if not using package scanning.

Expected:

```text id="h0y7xz"
Mapper may not apply.
```

Lesson:

```text id="0rs9mf"
Provider registration is part of runtime contract.
```

# 21. Review Checklist

| Review area | Question |
|---|---|
| REST dependency | Does `payment-rest` avoid direct dependency on persistence? |
| Jakarta API | Is `jakarta.ws.rs` required only where REST API is used? |
| Resource registration | Are resources registered explicitly or package scanning intentionally configured? |
| DTO reflection | Are DTO packages opened only where needed? |
| Exception mapping | Are exception mappers registered and tested? |
| Filters/providers | Are provider packages opened if runtime discovery needs it? |
| Module names | Are Jersey/Jackson/MyBatis module names validated from actual JARs? |
| Runtime mode | Is deployment using classpath or module path? |
| CI parity | Does CI test the same runtime style as Docker/Kubernetes? |
| Opens hygiene | Are `opens` directives documented and minimal? |

---

# 22. Practical Recommendation

Untuk service Jersey/JAX-RS enterprise, rekomendasi saya:

```text id="4w4nfj"
1. Keep REST resource thin.
2. Put DTO in payment-api.
3. Put use case in payment-application.
4. Put Jersey ResourceConfig in payment-bootstrap.
5. Prefer explicit register(...) during JPMS migration.
6. Use opens only for resource/provider/DTO packages that need reflection.
7. Avoid open module unless it is a temporary migration bridge.
8. Do not force module-path runtime before dependency graph is clean.
```

Target akhirnya bukan “semua kelihatan modular di atas kertas”, tetapi:

```text id="r2slmr"
REST cannot bypass application.
Domain cannot depend on HTTP/Jersey.
DTO serialization works predictably.
Provider/filter/exception mapper registration is explicit.
Runtime startup mode is understood and tested.
```

---

## Status Seri

**Seri 5/12 selesai - JPMS with Jersey/JAX-RS.**

Rekomendasi materi berikutnya: **Seri 6/12 - JPMS with Reflection-heavy Frameworks: JSON-B/Jackson, MyBatis, Hibernate Validator, mapper scanning, DTO/entity access, dan strategi `opens` yang aman.**

- [Jakarta RESTful Web Services](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- [Jakarta RESTful Web Services 4.0 API Specification](https://javadoc.io/doc/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta.ws.rs-api/latest/index.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- [ResourceConfig (Jersey 3.1.7 API)](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/apidocs/3.1.7/jersey/org/glassfish/jersey/server/ResourceConfig.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- [JEP 261: Module System](https://openjdk.org/jeps/261?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

## Response:
7/15/2026, 1:22:44 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 6/12**. Fokusnya area yang paling sering bikin JPMS “pecah” di aplikasi enterprise: reflection dari JSON serializer, validator, MyBatis mapper/entity, dan cara memilih `opens` yang tidak terlalu longgar.

# Seri 6/12 - JPMS with Reflection-heavy Frameworks

## 1. Introduction

Di seri ini kita bahas bagian JPMS yang paling sering bermasalah di aplikasi enterprise: **framework yang memakai reflection**.

Dalam Java Jersey/JAX-RS microservice, area reflection-heavy biasanya muncul di:

```text id="glw8ov"
1. JSON serialization/deserialization
   - JSON-B / Yasson
   - Jackson

2. Jakarta Bean Validation / Hibernate Validator
   - @NotNull
   - @Valid
   - custom constraint
   - method/constructor validation

3. MyBatis
   - mapper interface
   - mapper XML
   - result mapping
   - type alias
   - type handler
   - object factory

4. Jersey runtime
   - resource discovery
   - provider/filter/exception mapper
   - request/response entity provider
```

JPMS secara default membuat module lebih tertutup. Package yang tidak di-`exports` tidak bisa dipakai module lain secara normal, dan package yang tidak di-`opens` bisa bermasalah jika framework perlu akses reflection. Dev.java menjelaskan bahwa `opens` dipakai untuk membuka package terhadap reflection tanpa membuat package tersebut menjadi accessible secara normal pada compile-time. ([Dev.java](https://dev.java/learn/modules/opening-for-reflection/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Core Mental Model

Pegangan utama:

```text id="3ow7ll"
exports = compile-time API access
opens   = runtime reflective access
```

Contoh:

```java id="hlptlo"
module com.company.payment.api {
    exports com.company.payment.api.request;
    opens com.company.payment.api.request;
}
```

Maknanya:

| Directive | Arti praktis |
|---|---|
| `exports com.company.payment.api.request` | Module lain boleh import `CreatePaymentRequest` secara normal |
| `opens com.company.payment.api.request` | Serializer/validator boleh mengakses field/constructor/method via reflection |

Jangan mengganti semua masalah reflection dengan:

```java id="933fy0"
open module com.company.payment.api {
}
```

Itu terlalu longgar karena membuka seluruh package di module untuk reflection.

---

# 3. Diagram

```text id="rjsdzl"
HTTP JSON Request
│
▼
Jersey Resource
│
├── Deserialize JSON body
│   ├── JSON-B / Jackson
│   └── needs access to DTO constructor/field/getter/setter
│
├── Validate request
│   ├── Jakarta Validation API
│   └── Hibernate Validator implementation
│
├── Call application use case
│
├── Persistence operation
│   ├── MyBatis mapper interface
│   ├── XML mapper
│   ├── resultMap / typeAlias / typeHandler
│   └── DB entity / persistence record
│
└── Serialize response DTO

JPMS pressure points
│
├── DTO package may need opens
├── validation model package may need opens
├── MyBatis entity/mapper/typehandler package may need opens
├── REST provider/filter/mapper package may need opens
└── domain package should stay mostly closed
```

---

# 4. Decomposition Map

| Area | Framework behavior | JPMS impact | Recommended action |
|---|---|---|---|
| Request DTO | JSON deserialization reads constructors/fields/properties | DTO package may need `opens` | Put DTO in `payment-api`, export + open only DTO packages |
| Response DTO | JSON serialization reads properties/records/getters | DTO package may need `opens` depending serializer | Prefer immutable DTO/record; test actual provider |
| Validation annotations | Validator introspects fields/methods/annotations | DTO/model package may need reflection access | Open request DTO package; avoid opening domain broadly |
| Custom validator | Validator instantiates validator class | Validator package may need access | Keep validators in API/rest validation package |
| MyBatis mapper interface | Mapper interface bound to SQL statements | Mapper package may need visibility/config registration | Keep mapper in persistence module |
| MyBatis XML mapper | XML maps SQL to Java type/result maps | Resource loading + type resolution | Keep XML under persistence resources; use fully qualified names if aliases fragile |
| MyBatis entity/record | MyBatis sets/reads properties/constructor args | Entity package may need `opens` | Open persistence entity package, not domain wholesale |
| TypeHandler | Converts Java type ↔ JDBC type | Type handler class must be visible/registered | Keep in persistence module; open/register package |
| Jersey resource | Runtime invokes resource methods | Resource package may need `opens` | Prefer explicit registration; open resource package if needed |
| Exception mapper/filter/provider | Discovered/instantiated by Jersey | Provider packages may need `opens` | Explicitly register and open package as needed |
| Module names | Third-party module names vary by artifact/version | Wrong `requires`/`opens to` breaks build/runtime | Always verify actual JAR with `jar --describe-module` |

---

# 5. JSON-B / Jakarta JSON Binding

## 5.1 Apa yang dilakukan JSON-B

Jakarta JSON Binding mendefinisikan binding framework untuk mengubah Java object ke/dari JSON document. Dokumentasi Jakarta menyebut JSON-B sebagai layer standar antara Java classes dan JSON documents; salah satu reference implementation-nya adalah Yasson. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/web/jsonb/jsonb.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Praktisnya, JSON-B sering perlu membaca:

```text id="i8le07"
1. no-arg constructor
2. field
3. getter/setter
4. annotation
5. generic type
6. record component, tergantung provider/version
```

## 5.2 DTO module dengan JSON-B

Contoh request DTO:

```java id="r31rx1"
package com.company.payment.api.request;

import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotNull;
import java.math.BigDecimal;

public class CreatePaymentRequest {

    @NotNull
    private BigDecimal amount;

    @NotBlank
    private String currency;

    public CreatePaymentRequest() {
    }

    public BigDecimal getAmount() {
        return amount;
    }

    public String getCurrency() {
        return currency;
    }
}
```

`module-info.java`:

```java id="y0f7t3"
module com.company.payment.api {
    requires jakarta.validation;

    exports com.company.payment.api.request;
    exports com.company.payment.api.response;
    exports com.company.payment.api.error;

    opens com.company.payment.api.request;
    opens com.company.payment.api.response;
    opens com.company.payment.api.error;
}
```

Kenapa `exports` dan `opens` sama-sama ada?

```text id="19c4pq"
exports → REST module bisa compile memakai DTO type.
opens   → JSON-B/validator bisa reflect ke DTO.
```

## 5.3 Qualified opens untuk JSON-B

Jika module name provider sudah diketahui dari JAR aktual, bisa dibuat lebih ketat:

```java id="jjo4ny"
module com.company.payment.api {
    requires jakarta.validation;

    exports com.company.payment.api.request;
    exports com.company.payment.api.response;

    opens com.company.payment.api.request to org.eclipse.yasson;
    opens com.company.payment.api.response to org.eclipse.yasson;
}
```

Catatan penting:

```text id="2di06y"
Jangan copy nama module dari internet tanpa validasi.
Cek JAR aktual dari dependency project.
```

Command:

```bash id="b5bp1x"
jar --describe-module --file ~/.m2/repository/org/eclipse/yasson/*/*.jar
```

---

# 6. Jackson

## 6.1 Apa yang dilakukan Jackson

Jackson `databind` adalah library data-binding dan tree-model yang dibangun di atas Jackson Streaming API dan Jackson Annotations. Ini berarti Jackson bisa membaca/menulis object graph, JSON tree, annotation, constructor, property, field, dan custom serializer/deserializer. ([GitHub](https://github.com/FasterXML/jackson-databind?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Dalam modular app, error umum Jackson biasanya berbentuk:

```text id="a0kry6"
module com.company.payment.api does not "opens com.company.payment.api.request"
to module com.fasterxml.jackson.databind
```

Ada issue di repository Jackson yang menunjukkan error serupa ketika module tidak membuka package ke `com.fasterxml.jackson.databind`. Ini bukan berarti semua versi/semua konfigurasi pasti gagal, tetapi ini failure mode yang realistis saat Jackson perlu deep reflection. ([GitHub](https://github.com/FasterXML/jackson-databind/issues/3352?timeline_page=1&utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 6.2 Descriptor baseline untuk Jackson

```java id="9iejiz"
module com.company.payment.api {
    exports com.company.payment.api.request;
    exports com.company.payment.api.response;

    opens com.company.payment.api.request;
    opens com.company.payment.api.response;
}
```

Lebih ketat jika nama module aktual valid:

```java id="1h0h0e"
module com.company.payment.api {
    exports com.company.payment.api.request;
    exports com.company.payment.api.response;

    opens com.company.payment.api.request to com.fasterxml.jackson.databind;
    opens com.company.payment.api.response to com.fasterxml.jackson.databind;
}
```

Tetap validasi:

```bash id="o3ubqj"
jar --describe-module --file ~/.m2/repository/com/fasterxml/jackson/core/jackson-databind/*/jackson-databind-*.jar
```

## 6.3 DTO style yang lebih JPMS-friendly

Lebih mudah untuk serializer:

```java id="pr5ax8"
package com.company.payment.api.response;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

public record PaymentResponse(
    String id,
    BigDecimal amount,
    String currency,
    String status
) {
}
```

Untuk request DTO mutable:

```java id="395bpo"
public class CreatePaymentRequest {
    private BigDecimal amount;
    private String currency;

    public CreatePaymentRequest() {
    }

    public BigDecimal getAmount() {
        return amount;
    }

    public String getCurrency() {
        return currency;
    }
}
```

Untuk constructor-based DTO:

```java id="qhp8ds"
public record CreatePaymentRequest(
    BigDecimal amount,
    String currency
) {
}
```

Tetap wajib dites dengan provider actual, karena dukungan terhadap record, parameter names, dan creator behavior bergantung pada versi dan konfigurasi ObjectMapper/provider.

---

# 7. Jakarta Validation / Hibernate Validator

## 7.1 Apa yang dilakukan validator

Jakarta Validation memungkinkan constraint annotation pada object model, custom constraint, validasi object graph, parameter method, return value method, dan pelaporan violation. ([Bean Validation](https://beanvalidation.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```java id="xznsgb"
package com.company.payment.api.request;

import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotNull;
import java.math.BigDecimal;

public class CreatePaymentRequest {

    @NotNull
    private BigDecimal amount;

    @NotBlank
    private String currency;

    public CreatePaymentRequest() {
    }
}
```

Module:

```java id="z1kdwr"
module com.company.payment.api {
    requires jakarta.validation;

    exports com.company.payment.api.request;
    opens com.company.payment.api.request;
}
```

## 7.2 Hibernate Validator di module path

Hibernate Validator documentation menyatakan bahwa mulai Hibernate Validator 9.0 tersedia JPMS module descriptors untuk penggunaan di module path, dengan module name antara lain `jakarta.validation` untuk API dan `org.hibernate.validator` untuk core. ([Hibernate Documentation](https://docs.hibernate.org/stable/validator/reference/en-US/html_single/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh conceptual bootstrap module:

```java id="rbx28j"
module com.company.payment.bootstrap {
    requires jakarta.validation;
    requires org.hibernate.validator;

    requires com.company.payment.api;
    requires com.company.payment.rest;
}
```

Jika project memakai Hibernate Validator 8.x atau versi lain, jangan asumsi descriptor/module name sama. Validasi dengan:

```bash id="fjwuau"
jar --describe-module --file ~/.m2/repository/org/hibernate/validator/hibernate-validator/*/hibernate-validator-*.jar
```

## 7.3 Custom validator package

Custom annotation:

```java id="x4wvd8"
package com.company.payment.api.validation;

import jakarta.validation.Constraint;
import jakarta.validation.Payload;

import java.lang.annotation.Retention;
import java.lang.annotation.Target;

import static java.lang.annotation.ElementType.FIELD;
import static java.lang.annotation.RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME;

@Target(FIELD)
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Constraint(validatedBy = CurrencyCodeValidator.class)
public @interface ValidCurrencyCode {
    String message() default "Invalid currency code";
    Class<?>[] groups() default {};
    Class<? extends Payload>[] payload() default {};
}
```

Validator:

```java id="c3rzkv"
package com.company.payment.api.validation;

import jakarta.validation.ConstraintValidator;
import jakarta.validation.ConstraintValidatorContext;

public class CurrencyCodeValidator
        implements ConstraintValidator<ValidCurrencyCode, String> {

    @Override
    public boolean isValid(String value, ConstraintValidatorContext context) {
        return value != null && value.matches("[A-Z]{3}");
    }
}
```

Descriptor:

```java id="gqxwzk"
module com.company.payment.api {
    requires jakarta.validation;

    exports com.company.payment.api.request;
    exports com.company.payment.api.response;
    exports com.company.payment.api.validation;

    opens com.company.payment.api.request;
    opens com.company.payment.api.validation;
}
```

Practical note:

```text id="9wvb7z"
Constraint annotation biasanya perlu diekspor jika dipakai di package lain.
Validator implementation tidak selalu perlu diekspor, tapi bisa perlu dibuka/terlihat oleh provider.
```

---

# 8. MyBatis

## 8.1 Apa yang dilakukan MyBatis

MyBatis configuration memiliki elemen seperti `properties`, `settings`, `typeAliases`, `typeHandlers`, `objectFactory`, `plugins`, `environments`, `databaseIdProvider`, dan `mappers`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Dokumentasi MyBatis juga menjelaskan bahwa elemen `mappers` berisi daftar mapper, baik XML files maupun annotated Java interface classes yang berisi SQL code dan mapping definition. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/getting-started.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Ini penting untuk JPMS karena MyBatis dapat menyentuh:

```text id="qywkkv"
1. mapper interface
2. mapper XML resource
3. result object / persistence entity
4. constructor / setter / field
5. type alias
6. type handler
7. plugin/interceptor
8. object factory
```

## 8.2 Persistence module layout

```text id="8pz6ao"
payment-persistence-mybatis/
└── src/main/
    ├── java/
    │   ├── module-info.java
    │   └── com/company/payment/persistence/mybatis/
    │       ├── repository/
    │       │   └── MyBatisPaymentRepository.java
    │       ├── mapper/
    │       │   └── PaymentMapper.java
    │       ├── entity/
    │       │   └── PaymentRecord.java
    │       └── typehandler/
    │           └── MoneyTypeHandler.java
    │
    └── resources/
        └── mappers/
            └── PaymentMapper.xml
```

## 8.3 Mapper interface

```java id="6wxym9"
package com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.mapper;

import com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.entity.PaymentRecord;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.UUID;

public interface PaymentMapper {
    Optional<PaymentRecord> findById(UUID id);
    int insert(PaymentRecord record);
}
```

## 8.4 Persistence entity / record

```java id="pdrp1d"
package com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.entity;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.UUID;

public class PaymentRecord {
    private UUID id;
    private BigDecimal amount;
    private String currency;
    private String status;

    public PaymentRecord() {
    }

    public UUID getId() {
        return id;
    }

    public BigDecimal getAmount() {
        return amount;
    }

    public String getCurrency() {
        return currency;
    }

    public String getStatus() {
        return status;
    }
}
```

## 8.5 MyBatis module descriptor

Conceptual:

```java id="hq4p8a"
module com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    // requires org.mybatis;          // validate actual module name
    // requires java.sql;

    exports com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.repository
        to com.company.payment.bootstrap;

    opens com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.mapper;
    opens com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.entity;
    opens com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.typehandler;
}
```

Kenapa repository diekspor hanya ke bootstrap?

```text id="0rn5n8"
Bootstrap perlu instantiate/wire MyBatisPaymentRepository.
REST dan application tidak perlu tahu implementation class.
```

Kenapa entity/mapper/typehandler di-`opens`?

```text id="1l7q0s"
Karena MyBatis bisa melakukan mapping, object construction, type handler lookup,
dan mapper binding pada runtime.
```

## 8.6 Mapper XML

```xml id="xlasvd"
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" ?>
<!DOCTYPE mapper
  PUBLIC "-//mybatis.org//DTD Mapper 3.0//EN"
  "https://mybatis.org/dtd/mybatis-3-mapper.dtd">

<mapper namespace="com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.mapper.PaymentMapper">

  <resultMap id="PaymentResultMap"
             type="com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.entity.PaymentRecord">
    <id property="id" column="id"/>
    <result property="amount" column="amount"/>
    <result property="currency" column="currency"/>
    <result property="status" column="status"/>
  </resultMap>

  <select id="findById" resultMap="PaymentResultMap">
    select id, amount, currency, status
    from payments
    where id = #{id}
  </select>

</mapper>
```

Practical recommendation:

```text id="6nn4bj"
Untuk JPMS migration, pakai fully qualified class name di XML dulu.
Jangan terlalu mengandalkan package scanning/type alias sebelum runtime stabil.
```

---

# 9. `opens` Strategy yang Aman

## 9.1 Urutan keputusan

```text id="u98kiy"
1. Apakah package perlu dipakai secara compile-time oleh module lain?
   Ya  → exports
   Tidak → jangan exports

2. Apakah framework perlu reflection ke package ini?
   Ya  → opens
   Tidak → jangan opens

3. Apakah target framework module name diketahui?
   Ya  → opens package to target.module
   Tidak → opens package sementara + dokumentasikan

4. Apakah terlalu banyak package perlu opens?
   Ya  → evaluasi module boundary atau runtime mode
```

## 9.2 Unqualified opens

```java id="lzanr6"
opens com.company.payment.api.request;
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="umq9mz"
Mudah saat migrasi.
Lebih cepat membuat app jalan.
```

Kekurangan:

```text id="aq7c2l"
Semua module yang membaca module ini bisa melakukan deep reflection ke package tersebut.
```

## 9.3 Qualified opens

```java id="haqstd"
opens com.company.payment.api.request to com.fasterxml.jackson.databind;
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="lvbu1a"
Lebih ketat.
Lebih defensif.
Lebih jelas siapa yang butuh reflection.
```

Kekurangan:

```text id="zpvt3i"
Butuh nama module target yang benar.
Bisa fragile jika dependency/module name berubah.
```

## 9.4 `open module`

```java id="4zew9c"
open module com.company.payment.api {
    exports com.company.payment.api.request;
}
```

Gunakan hanya sebagai bridge sementara.

JEP 261 juga memperingatkan bahwa opsi seperti `--add-exports` dan `--add-opens` harus digunakan dengan hati-hati karena membuka akses ke internal API dapat membuat aplikasi/library gagal jika internal API berubah atau dihapus. ([OpenJDK](https://openjdk.org/jeps/261?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 10. Practical `module-info.java` Set

## 10.1 `payment-api`

```java id="u26rfd"
module com.company.payment.api {
    requires jakarta.validation;

    exports com.company.payment.api.request;
    exports com.company.payment.api.response;
    exports com.company.payment.api.error;
    exports com.company.payment.api.validation;

    opens com.company.payment.api.request;
    opens com.company.payment.api.response;
    opens com.company.payment.api.error;
    opens com.company.payment.api.validation;
}
```

Lebih ketat, setelah validasi module names:

```java id="z4fbfw"
module com.company.payment.api {
    requires jakarta.validation;

    exports com.company.payment.api.request;
    exports com.company.payment.api.response;
    exports com.company.payment.api.error;
    exports com.company.payment.api.validation;

    opens com.company.payment.api.request
        to com.fasterxml.jackson.databind, org.hibernate.validator;

    opens com.company.payment.api.response
        to com.fasterxml.jackson.databind;

    opens com.company.payment.api.error
        to com.fasterxml.jackson.databind;
}
```

## 10.2 `payment-rest`

```java id="l4f46i"
module com.company.payment.rest {
    requires jakarta.ws.rs;

    requires com.company.payment.api;
    requires com.company.payment.application;
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    exports com.company.payment.rest.resource
        to com.company.payment.bootstrap;

    exports com.company.payment.rest.exception
        to com.company.payment.bootstrap;

    exports com.company.payment.rest.filter
        to com.company.payment.bootstrap;

    opens com.company.payment.rest.resource;
    opens com.company.payment.rest.exception;
    opens com.company.payment.rest.filter;
    opens com.company.payment.rest.provider;
}
```

## 10.3 `payment-persistence-mybatis`

```java id="vehz2f"
module com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis {
    requires java.sql;
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    // requires org.mybatis; // cek JAR aktual

    exports com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.repository
        to com.company.payment.bootstrap;

    opens com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.mapper;
    opens com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.entity;
    opens com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.typehandler;
}
```

## 10.4 `payment-domain`

```java id="a6lk3g"
module com.company.payment.domain {
    exports com.company.payment.domain.model;
    exports com.company.payment.domain.valueobject;
    exports com.company.payment.domain.port;
    exports com.company.payment.domain.exception;
}
```

Domain sengaja tidak `opens` secara default.

---

# 11. Anti-pattern

## 11.1 Membuka domain secara luas

Buruk:

```java id="58qdhc"
open module com.company.payment.domain {
    exports com.company.payment.domain.model;
}
```

Masalah:

```text id="jqepiq"
Domain adalah pusat invariant.
Kalau semua package domain terbuka reflection, boundary melemah.
```

Lebih baik:

```java id="yl5fec"
module com.company.payment.domain {
    exports com.company.payment.domain.model;
    exports com.company.payment.domain.port;
}
```

Jika benar-benar ada domain model yang perlu serialization, pertimbangkan dulu apakah seharusnya API response memakai DTO terpisah.

---

## 11.2 Memakai persistence entity sebagai API response

Buruk:

```java id="7v2j8e"
@GET
@Path("/{id}")
public PaymentRecord getPayment(@PathParam("id") UUID id) {
    return mapper.findById(id).orElseThrow();
}
```

Dampak:

```text id="u7jexw"
1. DB schema shape bocor ke API.
2. API berubah saat persistence berubah.
3. REST jadi tahu MyBatis entity.
4. JPMS boundary rusak.
```

Lebih baik:

```java id="ooxot4"
@GET
@Path("/{id}")
public PaymentResponse getPayment(@PathParam("id") UUID id) {
    var payment = getPaymentUseCase.execute(id);
    return mapper.toResponse(payment);
}
```

---

## 11.3 Menambahkan `requires` framework ke domain

Buruk:

```java id="qp5xa5"
module com.company.payment.domain {
    requires jakarta.ws.rs;
    requires jakarta.validation;
    requires com.fasterxml.jackson.databind;
}
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="2r301t"
Validation transport-level → API DTO
JSON serialization → API/REST layer
HTTP response → REST layer
Business invariant → domain
```

Domain boleh memiliki validasi murni Java, tapi jangan terikat transport/framework kecuali ada keputusan arsitektur yang sangat eksplisit.

---

## 11.4 Semua package di-export hanya agar framework jalan

Buruk:

```java id="jkp67e"
module com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis {
    exports com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.mapper;
    exports com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.entity;
    exports com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.typehandler;
}
```

Kalau tujuannya hanya runtime reflection, gunakan `opens`, bukan `exports`.

```java id="k3p9va"
module com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis {
    opens com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.mapper;
    opens com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.entity;
    opens com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.typehandler;
}
```

---

# 12. Troubleshooting Table

| Error / symptom | Kemungkinan penyebab | Fix praktis |
|---|---|---|
| `module X does not "opens pkg" to module Y` | Framework butuh reflection ke package | Tambah `opens pkg to Y` atau sementara `opens pkg` |
| JSON request jadi null semua | DTO tidak accessible, no constructor, setter tidak ada, provider mismatch | Tambah no-arg constructor/getter, buka package, integration test |
| Record DTO gagal deserialize | Provider/version/config belum support record sesuai ekspektasi | Test provider actual, configure mapper/module |
| Constraint tidak jalan | Validation provider tidak terpasang/terintegrasi | Pastikan dependency runtime, provider registration, `@Valid` |
| `No provider for Validation` | API ada tapi implementation tidak ada | Tambah Hibernate Validator/runtime provider |
| MyBatis mapper not found | XML/resource path/mapper registration salah | Cek `<mappers>`, resource packaging, namespace |
| MyBatis result object gagal dibuat | Constructor/property tidak cocok atau package tidak open | Sesuaikan resultMap/constructor args, `opens entity` |
| Works with classpath, fails module-path | Missing `requires`, `opens`, split package, automatic module issue | Audit dengan `jdeps`, `jar --describe-module` |
| REST bisa import persistence | Maven dependency dan JPMS `requires` terlalu longgar | Remove dependency dari REST; wiring di bootstrap |

---

# 13. Practical Commands

## 13.1 Cek dependency tree

```bash id="qi30j4"
mvn dependency:tree
```

Per module:

```bash id="ahgh04"
mvn -pl payment-api dependency:tree
mvn -pl payment-rest dependency:tree
mvn -pl payment-persistence-mybatis dependency:tree
```

Cari dependency tertentu:

```bash id="dgkfep"
mvn dependency:tree -Dincludes=com.fasterxml.jackson.core
mvn dependency:tree -Dincludes=org.hibernate.validator
mvn dependency:tree -Dincludes=org.mybatis
mvn dependency:tree -Dincludes=jakarta.validation
```

## 13.2 Copy runtime dependencies

```bash id="84658t"
mvn -pl payment-bootstrap dependency:copy-dependencies \
  -DincludeScope=runtime \
  -DoutputDirectory=target/dependency
```

## 13.3 Cek module name aktual

```bash id="ujjukg"
for j in payment-bootstrap/target/dependency/*.jar; do
  echo "==== $j"
  jar --describe-module --file "$j" 2>/dev/null | head -30
done
```

## 13.4 Audit module dependencies

```bash id="z3kj81"
jdeps \
  --multi-release 17 \
  --print-module-deps \
  payment-bootstrap/target/payment-bootstrap-1.0.0-SNAPSHOT.jar
```

## 13.5 Jalankan dengan temporary `--add-opens`

Untuk diagnosis saja:

```bash id="e298zh"
java \
  --add-opens com.company.payment.api/com.company.payment.api.request=com.fasterxml.jackson.databind \
  --module-path "mods:lib" \
  --module com.company.payment.bootstrap/com.company.payment.bootstrap.Main
```

Jika flag ini memperbaiki error, pindahkan ke `module-info.java` bila memang desainnya benar:

```java id="1hbgpl"
opens com.company.payment.api.request to com.fasterxml.jackson.databind;
```

# 14. Practical Lab

## Lab 1 - DTO reflection test

Buat request DTO:

```java id="q6l11u"
public class CreatePaymentRequest {
    private BigDecimal amount;
    private String currency;

    public CreatePaymentRequest() {
    }

    public BigDecimal getAmount() {
        return amount;
    }

    public String getCurrency() {
        return currency;
    }
}
```

Test:

```text id="d3lvzh"
1. Kirim POST /payments dengan JSON body.
2. Pastikan field terbaca.
3. Hapus opens package request.
4. Jalankan lagi.
5. Catat apakah provider gagal atau tetap berhasil.
```

Tujuan:

```text id="fslk5g"
Mengetahui kebutuhan reflection berdasarkan provider aktual, bukan asumsi.
```

---

## Lab 2 - Validation test

Tambahkan:

```java id="g9q8cp"
@NotNull
private BigDecimal amount;
```

Test:

```text id="tstst7"
1. Kirim request tanpa amount.
2. Pastikan HTTP response menjadi validation error.
3. Cek apakah violation message muncul.
4. Cek apakah provider runtime tersedia.
```

Tujuan:

```text id="ozjz3f"
Membedakan dependency API jakarta.validation dengan implementation provider seperti Hibernate Validator.
```

---

## Lab 3 - MyBatis entity mapping test

Buat `PaymentRecord` dengan no-arg constructor dan getter.

Test:

```text id="m0mlqy"
1. Jalankan select mapper.
2. Pastikan result object terisi.
3. Hapus opens entity package.
4. Jalankan via module path.
5. Catat error reflection/object construction jika muncul.
```

Tujuan:

```text id="21pyo5"
Menentukan package persistence mana yang benar-benar perlu opens.
```

---

## Lab 4 - REST tidak boleh tahu persistence

Di `payment-rest`, coba import:

```java id="jv33c4"
import com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.mapper.PaymentMapper;
```

Expected:

```text id="tx85cx"
Compile gagal.
```

Jika compile berhasil, berarti Maven dependency atau JPMS `requires` salah.

---

## Lab 5 - Ubah unqualified opens menjadi qualified opens

Awal:

```java id="2t951k"
opens com.company.payment.api.request;
```

Setelah tahu target module:

```java id="mdrl98"
opens com.company.payment.api.request to com.fasterxml.jackson.databind, org.hibernate.validator;
```

Test:

```text id="hufbpb"
1. Run unit test.
2. Run integration test.
3. Run Docker/container smoke test.
4. Pastikan tidak ada provider yang diam-diam butuh access.
```

---

# 15. Review Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| DTO | Apakah request/response DTO ada di `payment-api`, bukan persistence? |
| JSON provider | Apakah package DTO yang butuh reflection sudah `opens`? |
| Validation | Apakah `jakarta.validation` hanya dipakai di API/REST layer yang relevan? |
| Hibernate Validator | Apakah implementation provider tersedia di runtime? |
| MyBatis | Apakah mapper/entity/typehandler ada di persistence module? |
| MyBatis XML | Apakah namespace dan result type sesuai package aktual? |
| Domain | Apakah domain tidak dibuka reflection secara luas? |
| Exports vs opens | Apakah package yang hanya butuh reflection tidak di-`exports`? |
| Qualified opens | Apakah target module name sudah dicek dari JAR aktual? |
| Runtime parity | Apakah test dilakukan di mode runtime yang sama dengan deployment? |
| Temporary flags | Apakah `--add-opens` didokumentasikan sebagai technical debt? |

---

# 16. Practical Recommendation

Untuk aplikasi **Java 17+ Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + PostgreSQL**, strategi paling aman:

```text id="57boze"
1. Domain module tetap strict.
2. API DTO module boleh export + open DTO packages.
3. REST module boleh open resource/provider/filter/exception packages.
4. Persistence MyBatis module boleh open mapper/entity/typehandler packages.
5. Jangan export persistence internals.
6. Jangan gunakan DB entity sebagai API response.
7. Jangan gunakan open module kecuali bridge sementara.
8. Validasi nama module pihak ketiga dari JAR aktual.
9. Gunakan integration test untuk membuktikan reflection requirement.
```

Target akhirnya:

```text id="bqenpf"
Reflection access bukan dilarang.
Reflection access harus eksplisit, minimal, dan bisa dijelaskan.
```

---

# Seri 7/12 - Dependency Hygiene & Module Graph Analysis

## 1. Introduction

Seri ini fokus pada **cara mengaudit dependency dan module graph** di Java 17+ microservice berbasis **Maven, Jersey/JAX-RS, MyBatis, PostgreSQL, Docker/Kubernetes**.

Di seri sebelumnya kita sudah membahas boundary:

```text
REST → Application → Domain
Persistence → Domain
Integration → Domain/Application SPI
Bootstrap → Semua implementation yang perlu di-wiring
```

Masalahnya, boundary bagus di diagram bisa rusak diam-diam lewat dependency:

```text
payment-rest accidentally depends on payment-persistence-mybatis
payment-domain accidentally imports jakarta.ws.rs
payment-api accidentally exposes persistence entity
third-party library pulls conflicting transitive dependency
two JARs contain the same package/class
module name guessed incorrectly
classpath works but module-path fails
```

Maven Dependency Plugin menyediakan goal seperti `dependency:tree` untuk menampilkan dependency tree project, termasuk output text, DOT, GraphML, TGF, dan JSON. Ini sangat berguna untuk dependency audit dan CI evidence. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/plugins/maven-dependency-plugin/tree-mojo.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

`jdeps` adalah tool JDK untuk menunjukkan dependency package-level atau class-level dari `.class`, directory, atau JAR; tool ini juga bisa menghasilkan output DOT untuk visualisasi dependency. ([Oracle Documentation](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/specs/man/jdeps.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Mental Model

```text
Maven dependency graph
│
├── Artifact dependency
├── Direct dependency
├── Transitive dependency
├── Version conflict
├── Dependency scope
├── Exclusion
└── BOM / dependencyManagement

JPMS module graph
│
├── requires
├── requires transitive
├── requires static
├── exports
├── opens
├── automatic module
├── unnamed module
└── module path resolution

Runtime classloading graph
│
├── classpath ordering
├── duplicate classes
├── shadowed classes
├── fat JAR packaging
├── service provider discovery
└── framework reflection
```

Jangan campur tiga layer ini. Maven bisa bilang dependency tersedia, JPMS bisa tetap menolak akses karena tidak ada `requires`, dan runtime classloader bisa tetap gagal karena duplicate class, missing resource, atau service provider tidak ikut ter-package.

---

# 3. Diagram

```text
Dependency Hygiene Pipeline
│
├── 1. Maven artifact graph
│   ├── mvn dependency:tree
│   ├── direct vs transitive
│   ├── scope check
│   ├── version conflict
│   └── dependency convergence
│
├── 2. JPMS module graph
│   ├── module-info.java
│   ├── requires / exports / opens
│   ├── automatic module detection
│   ├── split package check
│   └── jdeps analysis
│
├── 3. Runtime packaging graph
│   ├── fat JAR / thin JAR
│   ├── classpath vs module-path
│   ├── duplicate classes
│   ├── service providers
│   └── Docker entrypoint parity
│
└── 4. CI guardrail
    ├── fail on convergence issue
    ├── fail on banned dependency
    ├── fail on duplicate classes
    ├── fail on forbidden layer dependency
    ├── publish dependency report
    └── smoke test runtime startup
```

---

# 4. Decomposition Map

| Area | Skill | Practical meaning | Tooling | Failure if ignored |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Dependency tree | Read Maven dependency graph | Know direct/transitive dependencies per module | `mvn dependency:tree` | Hidden dependency leaks |
| Direct dependency audit | Validate declared dependencies | Each module declares only what it uses | `dependency:analyze` | Bloated POM, accidental framework dependency |
| Transitive dependency audit | Understand what gets pulled indirectly | Jersey/Jackson/MyBatis may pull many libraries | `dependency:tree`, Enforcer | Runtime conflict, CVE exposure |
| Dependency convergence | Ensure same artifact version across tree | Avoid two paths resolving different versions | Maven Enforcer | Class/method mismatch |
| Upper bound check | Ensure resolved version is not lower than transitive requested version | Detect “nearest wins” downgrade | `requireUpperBoundDeps` | Runtime `NoSuchMethodError` |
| Banned dependency | Block unwanted libraries | Prevent `javax.*` after Jakarta migration, old logging, vulnerable libs | Enforcer `bannedDependencies` | Legacy/insecure dependency enters build |
| Duplicate classes | Detect same `.class` in multiple dependencies | Prevent class shadowing | Extra Enforcer `banDuplicateClasses` | Wrong class loaded at runtime |
| Split package | Detect same package across modules | JPMS module path is sensitive to package ownership | `jdeps`, custom script | Module-path startup failure |
| Automatic modules | Detect non-modular JARs on module path | Avoid guessed module names | `jar --describe-module` | Wrong `requires`, unstable module graph |
| Unnamed module | Understand classpath mode | Classpath code is not strict named module | Runtime command review | False confidence in JPMS enforcement |
| `jdeps` analysis | Inspect Java package/module dependencies | Find JDK internals, module deps, unexpected packages | `jdeps` | Hidden coupling |
| Layer dependency rule | Enforce architecture direction | REST must not depend on persistence | Maven/ArchUnit/custom script | Boundary erosion |
| Runtime parity | Align local, CI, Docker startup | Same classpath/module-path behavior | Docker smoke test | Works local, fails container |
| Report artifact | Produce evidence for review | Attach dependency graph/report to CI | CI artifacts | Hard to debug regressions |

---

# 5. Maven Dependency Tree

## 5.1 Basic Command

```bash
mvn dependency:tree
```

Per module:

```bash
mvn -pl payment-rest dependency:tree
mvn -pl payment-application dependency:tree
mvn -pl payment-persistence-mybatis dependency:tree
```

Filter dependency tertentu:

```bash
mvn dependency:tree -Dincludes=org.glassfish.jersey
mvn dependency:tree -Dincludes=org.mybatis
mvn dependency:tree -Dincludes=org.postgresql
mvn dependency:tree -Dincludes=com.fasterxml.jackson.core
mvn dependency:tree -Dincludes=jakarta.ws.rs
mvn dependency:tree -Dincludes=javax.ws.rs
```

Maven Dependency Plugin `dependency:tree` mendukung output text default dan juga format lain seperti DOT, GraphML, TGF, serta JSON. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/plugins/maven-dependency-plugin/tree-mojo.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 5.2 Output JSON untuk CI

```bash
mvn -pl payment-rest dependency:tree \
  -DoutputType=json \
  -DoutputFile=target/dependency-tree.json
```

Gunanya:

```text
1. Bisa disimpan sebagai CI artifact.
2. Bisa dibandingkan antar build.
3. Bisa diparse untuk guardrail custom.
4. Bisa dipakai untuk dependency review.
```

## 5.3 Output DOT untuk Graph

```bash
mvn -pl payment-bootstrap dependency:tree \
  -DoutputType=dot \
  -DoutputFile=target/dependency-tree.dot
```

Lalu:

```bash
dot -Tpng target/dependency-tree.dot -o target/dependency-tree.png
```

Useful untuk onboarding karena dependency graph lebih mudah dibaca secara visual.

---

# 6. Dependency Tree Interpretation

Contoh:

```text
com.company.payment:payment-rest:jar:1.0.0-SNAPSHOT
+- com.company.payment:payment-api:jar:1.0.0-SNAPSHOT:compile
+- com.company.payment:payment-application:jar:1.0.0-SNAPSHOT:compile
|  \- com.company.payment:payment-domain:jar:1.0.0-SNAPSHOT:compile
+- jakarta.ws.rs:jakarta.ws.rs-api:jar:3.1.0:compile
\- org.glassfish.jersey.core:jersey-server:jar:3.1.x:compile
```

Ini sehat jika `payment-rest` memang module REST.

Contoh mencurigakan:

```text
com.company.payment:payment-rest:jar:1.0.0-SNAPSHOT
+- com.company.payment:payment-persistence-mybatis:jar:1.0.0-SNAPSHOT:compile
+- org.mybatis:mybatis:jar:x.y.z:compile
\- org.postgresql:postgresql:jar:x.y.z:runtime
```

Interpretasi:

```text
REST module sekarang tahu persistence implementation.
Resource bisa bypass application use case.
Boundary rusak.
```

Perbaikan:

```text
1. Hapus dependency payment-persistence-mybatis dari payment-rest.
2. Hapus requires com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis dari module-info.java.
3. Pindahkan wiring repository ke payment-bootstrap.
4. Pastikan PaymentResource hanya depends ke use case/application.
```
