## Response:
7/15/2026, 1:33:17 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 9/12**. Fokusnya SPI praktis: bagaimana `ServiceLoader`, `uses`, dan `provides` dipakai untuk provider pattern seperti payment gateway, storage, notification, OCR, document generator, tanpa membuat application layer tergantung implementation.

# Seri 9/12 - Service Provider Interface with JPMS

## 1. Introduction

Seri ini membahas **SPI / Service Provider Interface** dalam JPMS: bagaimana `ServiceLoader`, `uses`, dan `provides ... with` dipakai untuk membuat arsitektur plugin/provider yang rapi di Java 17+ microservice.

Di konteks **Jersey/JAX-RS microservice**, SPI berguna saat application/domain butuh kemampuan yang bisa diganti implementation-nya tanpa membuat core module tergantung langsung ke adapter:

```text
PaymentProvider
├── MidtransPaymentProvider
├── StripePaymentProvider
├── InternalBillingPaymentProvider
└── MockPaymentProvider

StorageProvider
├── LocalStorageProvider
├── S3StorageProvider
└── AzureBlobStorageProvider

DocumentGenerator
├── PdfDocumentGenerator
├── ExcelDocumentGenerator
└── WordDocumentGenerator
```

`ServiceLoader` adalah fasilitas Java untuk menemukan dan memuat service provider yang tersedia di runtime. Pada aplikasi modular, module yang memakai service harus mendeklarasikan `uses`, dan module provider mendeklarasikan `provides ... with`; dokumentasi Java 17 juga menegaskan bahwa jika service type berada di module lain, module consumer tetap perlu `requires` ke module yang mengekspor service type tersebut. ([Oracle Documentation](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/api/java.base/java/util/ServiceLoader.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Mental Model

```text
SPI pattern
│
├── Service Interface
│   └── Contract yang dipakai core/application
│
├── Service Consumer
│   └── Module yang butuh service
│
├── Service Provider
│   └── Concrete implementation
│
├── ServiceLoader
│   └── Runtime discovery mechanism
│
├── uses
│   └── Consumer module menyatakan memakai service
│
└── provides ... with
    └── Provider module menyatakan implementation service
```

Prinsip utamanya:

```text
Application depends on abstraction.
Provider implementation depends on abstraction.
Application does not depend on provider implementation.
```

Dengan JPMS:

```text
payment-application
  requires payment-domain
  uses PaymentProvider

payment-integration-midtrans
  requires payment-domain
  provides PaymentProvider with MidtransPaymentProvider
```

Dev.java menjelaskan bahwa module system menjadikan service loading sebagai first-class concept melalui directive `uses` dan `provides`, sehingga consumer bisa decoupled dari implementation provider. ([Dev.java](https://dev.java/learn/decoupling-modules-with-services/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 3. Diagram

```text
Payment Service with JPMS SPI
│
├── payment-domain
│   └── exports com.company.payment.domain.spi
│       └── PaymentProvider interface
│
├── payment-application
│   ├── requires payment-domain
│   ├── uses PaymentProvider
│   └── loads provider via ServiceLoader
│
├── payment-integration-midtrans
│   ├── requires payment-domain
│   └── provides PaymentProvider
│       with MidtransPaymentProvider
│
├── payment-integration-stripe
│   ├── requires payment-domain
│   └── provides PaymentProvider
│       with StripePaymentProvider
│
└── payment-bootstrap
    ├── requires payment-application
    ├── requires payment-integration-midtrans
    └── decides which provider modules are present at runtime
```

---

# 4. Decomposition Map

| Area | Skill | Practical meaning | JPMS directive |
|---|---|---|---|
| SPI design | Membuat interface provider yang stabil | Contract kecil, jelas, tidak bocor framework | `exports spi package` |
| Consumer declaration | Menyatakan module memakai service | Application bisa load provider via `ServiceLoader` | `uses` |
| Provider declaration | Menyatakan implementation tersedia | Integration module mendaftarkan implementation | `provides ... with` |
| Provider discovery | Menemukan provider runtime | `ServiceLoader.load(...)` | Requires `uses` in modular app |
| Provider selection | Memilih satu provider dari banyak | Berdasarkan config, priority, capability | Code-level selection |
| Provider lifecycle | Menangani init, close, health check | Jangan buat provider stateful sembarangan | Contract design |
| Error handling | Jika tidak ada provider / banyak provider | Fail-fast, fallback, explicit selection | Consumer logic |
| Testing | Fake provider untuk test | Test module bisa menyediakan provider | `provides` di test/provider module |
| Runtime packaging | Provider harus ada di module path/classpath | Kalau provider tidak ikut package, tidak ditemukan | Build/runtime layout |
| Security | Provider tidak boleh asal load tidak terkontrol | Whitelist provider id / capability | Selection guardrail |
| Observability | Provider harus terlihat di log/health | Log provider class/name/version saat startup | Bootstrap/application |
| Governance | Provider contract jangan sering berubah | Versioning SPI | API discipline |

---

# 5. Kapan SPI Cocok Dipakai?

SPI cocok jika ada **lebih dari satu implementation** atau implementation bisa diganti berdasarkan deployment/configuration.

| Use case | Cocok SPI? | Alasan |
|---|---:|---|
| Payment gateway provider | Ya | Midtrans/Stripe/internal billing bisa diganti |
| Storage provider | Ya | Local/S3/Azure Blob/on-prem storage |
| Notification channel | Ya | Email/SMS/WhatsApp/push |
| Document generator | Ya | PDF/Excel/Word generator |
| OCR provider | Ya | AWS Textract/Azure/internal OCR |
| Rules evaluator | Mungkin | Local rules/Camunda/DMN/external decision service |
| Repository implementation tunggal | Tidak selalu | DI/manual wiring sering lebih jelas |
| Simple utility | Tidak | Over-engineering |
| Business use case biasa | Tidak | Use case bukan plugin |

Rule praktis:

```text
Gunakan SPI jika implementation adalah extension point.
Jangan pakai SPI hanya untuk menghindari dependency injection/manual wiring.
```

---

# 6. Contoh Domain SPI - PaymentProvider

## 6.1 Package Layout

```text
payment-domain/
└── src/main/java/
    ├── module-info.java
    └── com/company/payment/domain/spi/
        ├── PaymentProvider.java
        ├── PaymentCommand.java
        ├── PaymentResult.java
        └── PaymentProviderDescriptor.java
```

## 6.2 `module-info.java`

```java
module com.company.payment.domain {
    exports com.company.payment.domain.model;
    exports com.company.payment.domain.valueobject;
    exports com.company.payment.domain.exception;
    exports com.company.payment.domain.spi;
}
```

## 6.3 Service Interface

```java
package com.company.payment.domain.spi;

public interface PaymentProvider {

    PaymentProviderDescriptor descriptor();

    PaymentResult charge(PaymentCommand command);
}
```

## 6.4 Command

```java
package com.company.payment.domain.spi;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.Currency;
import java.util.UUID;

public record PaymentCommand(
    UUID paymentId,
    BigDecimal amount,
    Currency currency,
    String customerReference
) {
}
```

## 6.5 Result

```java
package com.company.payment.domain.spi;

public sealed interface PaymentResult
        permits PaymentResult.Success, PaymentResult.Failed {

    record Success(
        String provider,
        String providerTransactionId
    ) implements PaymentResult {
    }

    record Failed(
        String provider,
        String reasonCode,
        String message
    ) implements PaymentResult {
    }
}
```

## 6.6 Descriptor

```java
package com.company.payment.domain.spi;

import java.util.Set;

public record PaymentProviderDescriptor(
    String providerId,
    String displayName,
    Set<String> supportedCurrencies,
    int priority
) {
}
```

Kenapa perlu `descriptor()`?

```text
1. Consumer bisa memilih provider tanpa instantiate banyak logic berat.
2. Startup log bisa menampilkan provider yang tersedia.
3. Config bisa memilih provider by providerId.
4. Health check bisa expose provider aktif.
```

---

# 7. Consumer Module - `payment-application`

## 7.1 Module Descriptor

```java
module com.company.payment.application {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    exports com.company.payment.application.usecase;

    uses com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentProvider;
}
```

`uses` penting dalam aplikasi modular karena membantu module system menemukan provider yang dideklarasikan oleh module lain. Dokumentasi Java 17 menyatakan aplikasi modular yang memakai `ServiceLoader` harus punya `uses` directive untuk service tersebut. ([Oracle Documentation](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/api/java.base/java/util/ServiceLoader.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 7.2 Provider Registry

```java
package com.company.payment.application.provider;

import com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentProvider;

import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.ServiceLoader;

public final class PaymentProviderRegistry {

    private final List<PaymentProvider> providers;

    private PaymentProviderRegistry(List<PaymentProvider> providers) {
        this.providers = List.copyOf(providers);
    }

    public static PaymentProviderRegistry load() {
        var providers = ServiceLoader.load(PaymentProvider.class)
            .stream()
            .map(ServiceLoader.Provider::get)
            .sorted(Comparator.comparingInt(
                provider -> provider.descriptor().priority()
            ))
            .toList();

        if (providers.isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("No PaymentProvider found");
        }

        return new PaymentProviderRegistry(providers);
    }

    public PaymentProvider byId(String providerId) {
        return providers.stream()
            .filter(provider -> provider.descriptor().providerId().equals(providerId))
            .findFirst()
            .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException(
                "PaymentProvider not found: " + providerId
            ));
    }

    public PaymentProvider defaultProvider() {
        return providers.get(0);
    }

    public List<PaymentProvider> all() {
        return providers;
    }
}
```

`ServiceLoader.Provider` dapat dipakai lewat `stream()` untuk melihat provider class dan memilih/filter provider tanpa langsung menginstansiasi provider; ini berguna jika selection logic perlu inspect metadata/class dulu. ([Oracle Documentation](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/api/java.base/java/util/ServiceLoader.Provider.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 8. Use Case dengan Provider

```java
package com.company.payment.application.usecase;

import com.company.payment.application.provider.PaymentProviderRegistry;
import com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentCommand;
import com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentResult;

public class ChargePaymentUseCase {

    private final PaymentProviderRegistry providerRegistry;

    public ChargePaymentUseCase(PaymentProviderRegistry providerRegistry) {
        this.providerRegistry = providerRegistry;
    }

    public PaymentResult execute(ChargePaymentCommand command) {
        var provider = command.providerId() == null
            ? providerRegistry.defaultProvider()
            : providerRegistry.byId(command.providerId());

        var paymentCommand = new PaymentCommand(
            command.paymentId(),
            command.amount(),
            command.currency(),
            command.customerReference()
        );

        return provider.charge(paymentCommand);
    }
}
```

Prinsip:

```text
Use case tahu PaymentProvider interface.
Use case tidak tahu MidtransPaymentProvider / StripePaymentProvider.
```

---

# 9. Provider Module - Midtrans

## 9.1 Layout

```text
payment-integration-midtrans/
└── src/main/java/
    ├── module-info.java
    └── com/company/payment/integration/midtrans/
        ├── MidtransPaymentProvider.java
        ├── MidtransClient.java
        ├── MidtransRequest.java
        ├── MidtransResponse.java
        └── MidtransMapper.java
```

## 9.2 Module Descriptor

```java
module com.company.payment.integration.midtrans {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    provides com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentProvider
        with com.company.payment.integration.midtrans.MidtransPaymentProvider;

    opens com.company.payment.integration.midtrans;
}
```

Catatan:

```text
Provider implementation package biasanya tidak perlu diexport.
ServiceLoader bisa menemukan provider dari provides directive.
```

Dokumentasi Java 17 menyatakan service provider yang dikembangkan sebagai module harus dinyatakan dalam `provides` directive; dokumentasi tersebut juga merekomendasikan agar package yang berisi provider tidak diekspor. ([GitHub](https://github.com/AdoptOpenJDK/openjdk-jdk11/blob/master/src/java.base/share/classes/java/util/ServiceLoader.java?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 9.3 Provider Implementation

```java
package com.company.payment.integration.midtrans;

import com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentCommand;
import com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentProvider;
import com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentProviderDescriptor;
import com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentResult;

import java.util.Set;

public final class MidtransPaymentProvider implements PaymentProvider {

    private final MidtransClient client;

    public MidtransPaymentProvider() {
        this.client = new MidtransClient();
    }

    @Override
    public PaymentProviderDescriptor descriptor() {
        return new PaymentProviderDescriptor(
            "midtrans",
            "Midtrans Payment Gateway",
            Set.of("IDR"),
            100
        );
    }

    @Override
    public PaymentResult charge(PaymentCommand command) {
        var request = MidtransMapper.toRequest(command);
        var response = client.charge(request);

        if (response.success()) {
            return new PaymentResult.Success(
                descriptor().providerId(),
                response.transactionId()
            );
        }

        return new PaymentResult.Failed(
            descriptor().providerId(),
            response.errorCode(),
            response.errorMessage()
        );
    }
}
```

---

# 10. Provider Constructor Rule

`ServiceLoader` butuh cara membuat provider. Untuk provider class di module, bentuk yang paling aman adalah:

```text
1. public no-arg constructor, atau
2. public static provider() method sesuai aturan ServiceLoader modern.
```

Untuk onboarding dan kejelasan, gunakan **public no-arg constructor** dulu.

```java
public final class MidtransPaymentProvider implements PaymentProvider {

    public MidtransPaymentProvider() {
    }
}
```

Jika provider butuh config, jangan langsung ambil config global sembarangan di constructor. Lebih baik:

```text
Option A - provider ringan, config dibaca dari environment/config service.
Option B - ServiceLoader hanya load factory, bukan provider final.
Option C - bootstrap tetap manual wiring jika dependency provider kompleks.
```

---

# 11. Provider Factory Pattern

Untuk provider yang butuh config, HTTP client, secret, datasource, atau observability dependencies, lebih aman SPI-nya berupa **factory**.

## 11.1 SPI

```java
package com.company.payment.domain.spi;

public interface PaymentProviderFactory {

    PaymentProviderDescriptor descriptor();

    PaymentProvider create(PaymentProviderContext context);
}
```

## 11.2 Context

```java
package com.company.payment.domain.spi;

import java.net.http.HttpClient;
import java.util.Map;

public record PaymentProviderContext(
    HttpClient httpClient,
    Map<String, String> config
) {
    public String requiredConfig(String key) {
        var value = config.get(key);
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Missing config: " + key);
        }
        return value;
    }
}
```

## 11.3 Provider Module

```java
module com.company.payment.integration.midtrans {
    requires java.net.http;
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    provides com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentProviderFactory
        with com.company.payment.integration.midtrans.MidtransPaymentProviderFactory;
}
```

## 11.4 Factory Implementation

```java
package com.company.payment.integration.midtrans;

import com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentProvider;
import com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentProviderContext;
import com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentProviderDescriptor;
import com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentProviderFactory;

import java.util.Set;

public final class MidtransPaymentProviderFactory implements PaymentProviderFactory {

    @Override
    public PaymentProviderDescriptor descriptor() {
        return new PaymentProviderDescriptor(
            "midtrans",
            "Midtrans Payment Gateway",
            Set.of("IDR"),
            100
        );
    }

    @Override
    public PaymentProvider create(PaymentProviderContext context) {
        var baseUrl = context.requiredConfig("midtrans.baseUrl");
        var apiKey = context.requiredConfig("midtrans.apiKey");

        var client = new MidtransClient(
            context.httpClient(),
            baseUrl,
            apiKey
        );

        return new MidtransPaymentProvider(client);
    }
}
```

Rekomendasi praktis:

```text
Untuk provider sederhana: ServiceLoader<PaymentProvider>.
Untuk provider butuh config/dependency: ServiceLoader<PaymentProviderFactory>.
```

---

# 12. Bootstrap dengan Provider Factory

```java
package com.company.payment.bootstrap;

import com.company.payment.application.provider.PaymentProviderRegistry;
import com.company.payment.application.usecase.ChargePaymentUseCase;
import com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentProviderContext;
import com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentProviderFactory;

import java.net.http.HttpClient;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.ServiceLoader;

public final class PaymentObjectGraph {

    public ChargePaymentUseCase chargePaymentUseCase() {
        var context = new PaymentProviderContext(
            HttpClient.newHttpClient(),
            loadProviderConfig()
        );

        var providers = ServiceLoader.load(PaymentProviderFactory.class)
            .stream()
            .map(ServiceLoader.Provider::get)
            .map(factory -> factory.create(context))
            .toList();

        var registry = new PaymentProviderRegistry(providers);

        return new ChargePaymentUseCase(registry);
    }

    private Map<String, String> loadProviderConfig() {
        return Map.of(
            "midtrans.baseUrl", System.getenv("MIDTRANS_BASE_URL"),
            "midtrans.apiKey", System.getenv("MIDTRANS_API_KEY")
        );
    }
}
```

Jika bootstrap yang memanggil `ServiceLoader.load(PaymentProviderFactory.class)`, maka `payment-bootstrap` juga perlu `uses PaymentProviderFactory`.

```java
module com.company.payment.bootstrap {
    requires java.net.http;
    requires com.company.payment.domain;
    requires com.company.payment.application;

    uses com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentProviderFactory;
}
```

---

# 13. Module Graph Pattern

## 13.1 Interface di Domain

```text
payment-domain
└── exports com.company.payment.domain.spi
```

## 13.2 Consumer di Application

```text
payment-application
├── requires payment-domain
└── uses PaymentProvider / PaymentProviderFactory
```

## 13.3 Provider di Integration

```text
payment-integration-midtrans
├── requires payment-domain
└── provides PaymentProviderFactory with MidtransPaymentProviderFactory
```

## 13.4 Bootstrap Membawa Provider ke Runtime

```text
payment-bootstrap
├── requires payment-application
├── requires payment-integration-midtrans
└── runtime module path includes provider module
```

Poin penting:

```text
Provider module harus observable di runtime.
Kalau JAR provider tidak masuk classpath/module-path, ServiceLoader tidak akan menemukannya.
```

JEP 261 menjelaskan bahwa module definitions yang ada di module path bersama system modules membentuk universe of observable modules; artinya provider module harus benar-benar berada di runtime module path jika ingin ditemukan sebagai named module. ([OpenJDK](https://openjdk.org/jeps/261?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 14. Classpath vs Module Path untuk SPI

## 14.1 Modular style

`module-info.java` provider:

```java
module com.company.payment.integration.midtrans {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    provides com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentProvider
        with com.company.payment.integration.midtrans.MidtransPaymentProvider;
}
```

Consumer:

```java
module com.company.payment.application {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    uses com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentProvider;
}
```

## 14.2 Classpath legacy style

Untuk non-modular JAR/classpath, ServiceLoader tradisional memakai file:

```text
META-INF/services/com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentProvider
```

Isi file:

```text
com.company.payment.integration.midtrans.MidtransPaymentProvider
```

## 14.3 Migration recommendation

Jika project sedang transisi:

```text
1. Untuk named module: gunakan provides ... with.
2. Untuk legacy classpath provider: gunakan META-INF/services.
3. Selama hybrid migration, pastikan integration test membuktikan provider ditemukan.
```

ServiceLoader mendukung model provider di runtime environment, dan JPMS menambahkan integrasi eksplisit melalui `uses` dan `provides`; untuk aplikasi modular, deklarasi module menjadi bagian penting dari discovery yang reliable. ([Oracle Documentation](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/api/java.base/java/util/ServiceLoader.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 15. Provider Selection Strategy

## 15.1 Jangan Ambil Provider Pertama Sembarangan

Buruk:

```java
var provider = ServiceLoader.load(PaymentProvider.class)
    .findFirst()
    .orElseThrow();
```

Masalah:

```text
1. Ordering provider tidak boleh dijadikan kontrak bisnis utama.
2. Kalau ada dua provider, hasil bisa tidak sesuai ekspektasi.
3. Deployment beda bisa memilih provider berbeda.
```

Dokumentasi ServiceLoader untuk module/class loader ordering menyatakan ordering antar module dalam class loader/layer yang sama tidak didefinisikan secara kontrak yang aman untuk business selection; karena itu selection harus explicit di code/config. ([LIA](https://lia.deis.unibo.it/Misc/SW/Java/java9-docs/api/java/util/ServiceLoader.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 15.2 Pilih by Config

```java
public PaymentProvider activeProvider(String configuredProviderId) {
    return providers.stream()
        .filter(provider -> provider.descriptor().providerId().equals(configuredProviderId))
        .findFirst()
        .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalStateException(
            "Configured payment provider not available: " + configuredProviderId
        ));
}
```

Config:

```properties
payment.provider=midtrans
```

Startup log:

```text
Available payment providers:
- midtrans priority=100 currencies=[IDR]
- stripe priority=200 currencies=[USD, SGD]

Active payment provider:
- midtrans
```

---

# 16. Multi-Provider Capability Selection

Kadang selection bukan hanya by id, tapi berdasarkan capability.

```java
public PaymentProvider providerForCurrency(String currency) {
    return providers.stream()
        .filter(provider -> provider.descriptor().supportedCurrencies().contains(currency))
        .min(Comparator.comparingInt(provider -> provider.descriptor().priority()))
        .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalStateException(
            "No payment provider supports currency: " + currency
        ));
}
```

Use case:

```text
IDR → midtrans
USD → stripe
SGD → stripe
Internal settlement → internal-billing
```

Practical guardrail:

```text
Kalau selection memengaruhi uang/transaksi, jangan implicit.
Log provider selected.
Persist provider used in transaction table.
```

---

# 17. Persist Provider Identity

Untuk payment, provider identity harus disimpan.

```sql
create table payments (
    id uuid primary key,
    amount numeric(19, 2) not null,
    currency varchar(3) not null,
    status varchar(32) not null,
    provider_id varchar(64) not null,
    provider_transaction_id varchar(128),
    created_at timestamptz not null
);
```

Kenapa?

```text
1. Reconciliation butuh tahu provider mana yang dipakai.
2. Retry harus menuju provider yang sama.
3. Refund harus ke provider asal.
4. Audit trail harus defensible.
5. Incident analysis lebih mudah.
```

Ini bukan detail JPMS, tapi sangat penting untuk provider pattern di payment domain.

---

# 18. Error Handling SPI

Jangan buat provider melempar exception vendor-specific keluar dari module integration.

Buruk:

```java
throw new MidtransTimeoutException(...);
```

lalu application menangkapnya.

Lebih baik:

```java
public sealed interface PaymentProviderException permits
    PaymentProviderException.Timeout,
    PaymentProviderException.ProviderRejected,
    PaymentProviderException.TechnicalFailure {

    String providerId();

    record Timeout(String providerId, String message)
        implements PaymentProviderException {
    }

    record ProviderRejected(String providerId, String reasonCode, String message)
        implements PaymentProviderException {
    }

    record TechnicalFailure(String providerId, String message)
        implements PaymentProviderException {
    }
}
```

Atau result-based:

```java
return new PaymentResult.Failed(
    "midtrans",
    "TIMEOUT",
    "Provider did not respond within timeout"
);
```

Rule:

```text
Vendor-specific error diterjemahkan di provider module.
Application menerima error contract yang stabil.
```

---

# 19. Observability Pattern

Saat startup:

```java
public void logAvailableProviders(PaymentProviderRegistry registry) {
    registry.all().forEach(provider -> {
        var descriptor = provider.descriptor();
        log.info(
            "PaymentProvider loaded: id={}, name={}, currencies={}, priority={}, class={}",
            descriptor.providerId(),
            descriptor.displayName(),
            descriptor.supportedCurrencies(),
            descriptor.priority(),
            provider.getClass().getName()
        );
    });
}
```

Health endpoint:

```json
{
  "paymentProviders": [
    {
      "id": "midtrans",
      "status": "UP",
      "currencies": ["IDR"]
    }
  ]
}
```

Practical rule:

```text
Provider discovery harus observable.
Jangan biarkan ServiceLoader bekerja diam-diam tanpa startup log.
```

---

# 20. Security & Governance

SPI bisa menjadi extension point berbahaya jika provider JAR bisa masuk runtime tanpa kontrol.

Risiko:

```text
1. Provider tak dikenal ikut module path/classpath.
2. Provider malicious atau salah konfigurasi ter-load.
3. Provider id conflict.
4. Provider dipilih karena priority/order yang tidak dikontrol.
5. Secret/config bocor ke provider tidak semestinya.
```

Guardrail:

```text
1. Whitelist provider id di config.
2. Fail startup jika provider id duplicate.
3. Fail startup jika configured provider tidak tersedia.
4. Jangan pilih provider berdasarkan order ServiceLoader saja.
5. Log provider class/module saat startup.
6. Batasi provider dependency melalui Maven/deployment control.
```

Duplicate provider id check:

```java
private void validateUniqueProviderIds(List<PaymentProvider> providers) {
    var seen = new java.util.HashSet<String>();

    for (var provider : providers) {
        var providerId = provider.descriptor().providerId();

        if (!seen.add(providerId)) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "Duplicate PaymentProvider id: " + providerId
            );
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 21. Testing Strategy

## 21.1 Unit Test dengan Fake Provider Manual

```java
class FakePaymentProvider implements PaymentProvider {

    @Override
    public PaymentProviderDescriptor descriptor() {
        return new PaymentProviderDescriptor(
            "fake",
            "Fake Provider",
            Set.of("IDR"),
            1
        );
    }

    @Override
    public PaymentResult charge(PaymentCommand command) {
        return new PaymentResult.Success("fake", "FAKE-TX-001");
    }
}
```

Use case test:

```java
@Test
void chargePaymentUsesConfiguredProvider() {
    var registry = new PaymentProviderRegistry(List.of(new FakePaymentProvider()));
    var useCase = new ChargePaymentUseCase(registry);

    var result = useCase.execute(commandWithProvider("fake"));

    assertThat(result).isInstanceOf(PaymentResult.Success.class);
}
```

Unit test tidak wajib pakai `ServiceLoader`.

## 21.2 Integration Test dengan ServiceLoader

Buat test provider module:

```java
module com.company.payment.integration.fake {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    provides com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentProvider
        with com.company.payment.integration.fake.FakePaymentProvider;
}
```

Test:

```java
@Test
void serviceLoaderFindsProvider() {
    var providers = ServiceLoader.load(PaymentProvider.class)
        .stream()
        .map(ServiceLoader.Provider::get)
        .toList();

    assertThat(providers)
        .anyMatch(provider -> provider.descriptor().providerId().equals("fake"));
}
```

## 21.3 Smoke Test

Saat startup app:

```text
Expected log:
PaymentProvider loaded: id=midtrans, class=...
Active payment provider: midtrans
```

Jika tidak muncul, build/deployment mungkin tidak membawa provider module.

---

# 22. Maven Layout

```text
payment-service/
├── payment-domain/
├── payment-application/
├── payment-api/
├── payment-rest/
├── payment-integration-midtrans/
├── payment-integration-stripe/
└── payment-bootstrap/
```

`payment-application/pom.xml`:

```xml
<dependencies>
    <dependency>
        <groupId>com.company.payment</groupId>
        <artifactId>payment-domain</artifactId>
        <version>${project.version}</version>
    </dependency>
</dependencies>
```

`payment-integration-midtrans/pom.xml`:

```xml
<dependencies>
    <dependency>
        <groupId>com.company.payment</groupId>
        <artifactId>payment-domain</artifactId>
        <version>${project.version}</version>
    </dependency>
</dependencies>
```

`payment-bootstrap/pom.xml`:

```xml
<dependencies>
    <dependency>
        <groupId>com.company.payment</groupId>
        <artifactId>payment-application</artifactId>
        <version>${project.version}</version>
    </dependency>

    <dependency>
        <groupId>com.company.payment</groupId>
        <artifactId>payment-integration-midtrans</artifactId>
        <version>${project.version}</version>
    </dependency>
</dependencies>
```

Rule:

```text
Application does not depend on payment-integration-midtrans.
Bootstrap/deployment decides provider module presence.
```

---

# 23. Runtime Packaging

## 23.1 Classpath

```bash
java -cp "app.jar:lib/*" com.company.payment.bootstrap.Main
```

Provider JAR harus ada di `lib`.

```text
lib/
├── payment-domain.jar
├── payment-application.jar
├── payment-integration-midtrans.jar
└── ...
```

## 23.2 Module Path

```bash
java \
  --module-path "mods:lib" \
  --module com.company.payment.bootstrap/com.company.payment.bootstrap.Main
```

Provider module harus ada di `mods` atau `lib` module path dan observable sebagai module.

```text
mods/
├── com.company.payment.domain.jar
├── com.company.payment.application.jar
├── com.company.payment.integration.midtrans.jar
└── com.company.payment.bootstrap.jar
```

Check:

```bash
jar --describe-module --file mods/payment-integration-midtrans.jar
```

Expected:

```text
com.company.payment.integration.midtrans
requires com.company.payment.domain
provides com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentProvider with ...
```

---

# 24. Common Failure Modes

| Failure | Symptom | Cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|
| Provider tidak ditemukan | `No PaymentProvider found` | Provider JAR tidak ada di runtime | Pastikan provider masuk classpath/module-path |
| `uses` tidak ada | ServiceLoader gagal reliable di modular app | Consumer module lupa `uses` | Tambahkan `uses` |
| Service interface tidak readable | Compile/runtime error | Consumer tidak `requires` module SPI | Tambahkan `requires payment-domain` |
| Provider tidak dideklarasikan | Tidak ditemukan di module path | Provider module lupa `provides` | Tambahkan `provides ... with` |
| Provider package diexport | Implementation bocor | Salah desain module | Jangan export provider package |
| Provider constructor gagal | ServiceConfigurationError | Constructor berat/config missing | Gunakan factory pattern |
| Provider id duplicate | Provider salah terpilih | Dua provider punya id sama | Validate duplicate id saat startup |
| Selection implicit | Provider berbeda antar runtime | Ambil provider pertama | Pilih via config/capability |
| Vendor exception bocor | Application tergantung vendor | Provider throw exception specific | Map ke SPI result/error |
| Works classpath, fails module path | Missing module directive | Hybrid migration issue | Cek `uses`, `provides`, module path |

---

# 25. Anti-Pattern

## 25.1 Application Depends on Provider Implementation

Buruk:

```java
module com.company.payment.application {
    requires com.company.payment.integration.midtrans;
}
```

Dampak:

```text
Application terkunci ke Midtrans.
Provider tidak lagi pluggable.
Testing dan deployment flexibility turun.
```

Lebih baik:

```java
module com.company.payment.application {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    uses com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentProvider;
}
```

## 25.2 Provider Interface Terlalu Gemuk

Buruk:

```java
public interface PaymentProvider {
    PaymentResult charge(...);
    RefundResult refund(...);
    ReconcileResult reconcile(...);
    SettlementReport settlement(...);
    TokenizeResult tokenize(...);
    ThreeDsResult authenticate3ds(...);
    void exportCsv(...);
}
```

Masalah:

```text
Tidak semua provider support semua capability.
Implementation dipaksa method kosong/unsupported.
```

Lebih baik pecah capability:

```text
ChargeProvider
RefundProvider
ReconciliationProvider
TokenizationProvider
SettlementReportProvider
```

Atau gunakan descriptor capability.

## 25.3 Provider Mengambil Secret Sendiri Tanpa Kontrol

Buruk:

```java
var apiKey = System.getenv("MIDTRANS_API_KEY");
```

di banyak class provider.

Lebih baik:

```text
Bootstrap/config layer membaca secret.
Provider factory menerima context/config.
Access secret centralized.
```

## 25.4 Provider Selection Berdasarkan Urutan

Buruk:

```java
ServiceLoader.load(PaymentProvider.class).findFirst()
```

Lebih baik:

```text
Select by configured provider id.
Fail if provider id not available.
Log active provider.
```

---

# 26. SPI vs Dependency Injection

| Aspek | SPI / ServiceLoader | DI / Manual Wiring |
|---|---|---|
| Cocok untuk | Plugin/provider discovery | Object graph normal |
| Binding | Runtime discovery | Explicit wiring/config |
| Visibility | Consumer tidak tahu implementation | Bootstrap/container tahu implementation |
| Debuggability | Bisa lebih implicit | Lebih eksplisit |
| Provider banyak | Bagus | Bisa juga, tapi perlu config |
| Dependency kompleks | Factory pattern diperlukan | Lebih natural |
| Test unit | Bisa bypass ServiceLoader | Sangat mudah |
| Enterprise control | Perlu whitelist/guardrail | Lebih terkontrol |

Rekomendasi:

```text
Gunakan SPI untuk extension point.
Gunakan DI/manual wiring untuk dependency biasa.
```

---

# 27. Practical Lab

## Lab 1 - Buat SPI di Domain

Tambahkan:

```text
payment-domain/src/main/java/com/company/payment/domain/spi/PaymentProvider.java
payment-domain/src/main/java/com/company/payment/domain/spi/PaymentCommand.java
payment-domain/src/main/java/com/company/payment/domain/spi/PaymentResult.java
```

Descriptor:

```java
module com.company.payment.domain {
    exports com.company.payment.domain.spi;
}
```

Expected:

```text
SPI package bisa dipakai application dan provider module.
```

---

## Lab 2 - Consumer dengan `uses`

Di `payment-application/module-info.java`:

```java
module com.company.payment.application {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    uses com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentProvider;
}
```

Buat registry:

```java
ServiceLoader.load(PaymentProvider.class)
```

Expected:

```text
Application bisa load provider tanpa dependency ke implementation module.
```

---

## Lab 3 - Provider dengan `provides`

Di `payment-integration-midtrans/module-info.java`:

```java
module com.company.payment.integration.midtrans {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    provides com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentProvider
        with com.company.payment.integration.midtrans.MidtransPaymentProvider;
}
```

Expected:

```text
Provider ditemukan saat JAR/module ada di runtime.
```

---

## Lab 4 - Hapus Provider JAR dari Runtime

Run tanpa `payment-integration-midtrans.jar`.

Expected:

```text
No PaymentProvider found.
```

Lesson:

```text
ServiceLoader bukan magic.
Provider harus ada di runtime packaging.
```

---

## Lab 5 - Duplicate Provider ID

Tambahkan dua provider dengan `providerId = "midtrans"`.

Expected:

```text
Startup fail karena duplicate provider id.
```

Lesson:

```text
Provider registry harus validasi uniqueness.
```

---

## Lab 6 - Config-based Selection

Config:

```properties
payment.provider=midtrans
```

Test:

```text
1. provider available → startup success
2. provider missing → startup fail
3. provider duplicate → startup fail
```

---

# 28. Review Checklist

| Area | Question |
|---|---|
| SPI location | Apakah interface ada di domain/spi atau contract module yang tepat? |
| Consumer | Apakah consumer memakai `uses` dan tidak depends provider implementation? |
| Provider | Apakah provider module memakai `provides ... with`? |
| Export hygiene | Apakah provider implementation package tidak diexport? |
| Constructor | Apakah provider bisa dibuat aman oleh ServiceLoader? |
| Config | Apakah provider selection explicit via config/capability? |
| Duplicate id | Apakah duplicate provider id dicegah? |
| Error mapping | Apakah vendor-specific error tidak bocor ke application? |
| Runtime packaging | Apakah provider JAR/module masuk runtime image? |
| Observability | Apakah provider yang loaded/active muncul di startup log/health? |
| Testing | Apakah ada test untuk provider discovery dan missing provider? |
| Security | Apakah provider yang boleh dipakai di-whitelist? |

---

# 29. Production Recommendation

Untuk Java Jersey/JAX-RS microservice enterprise:

```text
1. Pakai SPI untuk provider yang benar-benar pluggable.
2. Taruh SPI interface di domain/spi atau contract module.
3. Consumer module pakai uses.
4. Provider module pakai provides ... with.
5. Jangan export provider implementation package.
6. Pilih provider via config, bukan urutan ServiceLoader.
7. Fail fast jika provider tidak ada, duplicate, atau tidak support capability.
8. Log provider yang tersedia dan provider aktif saat startup.
9. Persist provider_id untuk transaksi/payment.
10. Gunakan factory SPI jika provider butuh config/dependency kompleks.
```

Target akhirnya:

```text
Core application stabil.
Provider implementation bisa diganti.
Dependency direction tetap bersih.
Runtime discovery tetap observable dan terkendali.
```

# Seri 10/12 - Testing Modular Java Applications with JPMS

## 1. Introduction

Di seri ini kita fokus pada **testing modular Java application** berbasis **Java 17+, Maven, Jersey/JAX-RS, MyBatis, PostgreSQL, Docker/Kubernetes**, terutama saat project mulai memakai `module-info.java`.

Masalah utama testing di JPMS bukan sekadar “test jalan atau tidak”, tapi:

```text
1. Apakah test berjalan di classpath atau module path?
2. Apakah test butuh akses ke internal package?
3. Apakah production module-info.java dibuat longgar hanya demi test?
4. Apakah Jersey resource/provider/filter/exception mapper benar-benar dites?
5. Apakah MyBatis mapper dites dengan PostgreSQL nyata?
6. Apakah runtime test sama dengan Docker/Kubernetes startup mode?
```

Maven Surefire dipakai pada fase `test` untuk menjalankan unit test dan menghasilkan report di `target/surefire-reports`, sedangkan Maven Failsafe menyediakan goal `integration-test` dan `verify` untuk integration test. Ini penting karena unit test dan integration test sebaiknya dipisahkan secara lifecycle, bukan dicampur dalam satu fase. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/surefire/maven-surefire-plugin/))

---

# 2. Diagram Testing Strategy

```text
JPMS Modular Testing Strategy
│
├── 1. Unit Test
│   ├── domain invariant test
│   ├── application use case test
│   ├── fake repository/provider
│   ├── no Jersey
│   ├── no PostgreSQL
│   └── fast, deterministic
│
├── 2. Module Boundary Test
│   ├── compile-time check
│   ├── REST must not access persistence
│   ├── domain must not access Jersey/MyBatis
│   ├── module-info.java correctness
│   └── dependency guardrail
│
├── 3. Jersey Resource Test
│   ├── ResourceConfig
│   ├── resource registration
│   ├── filter/provider/exception mapper
│   ├── JSON serialization/deserialization
│   └── HTTP status mapping
│
├── 4. MyBatis Integration Test
│   ├── PostgreSQL container
│   ├── migration/schema setup
│   ├── mapper XML loading
│   ├── resultMap/typeHandler
│   └── transaction/session lifecycle
│
├── 5. JPMS Runtime Test
│   ├── classpath test
│   ├── module-path dry run
│   ├── --add-opens test-only
│   ├── module-info-patch.maven
│   └── Docker parity
│
└── 6. CI Guardrail
    ├── surefire unit tests
    ├── failsafe integration tests
    ├── dependency boundary check
    ├── jdeps report
    └── container smoke test
```

---

# 3. Decomposition Map

| Area | Practical skill | JPMS concern | Tooling |
|---|---|---|---|
| Unit testing | Test domain/use case without infrastructure | Avoid opening production modules unnecessarily | JUnit Jupiter, Surefire |
| Integration testing | Test REST/DB/runtime wiring | Needs reflection access, runtime resources, module-path awareness | Failsafe, Jersey Test Framework, Testcontainers |
| Test module access | Test internal package without weakening production descriptor | Use test-only `--add-opens` / `--add-exports` | Maven Compiler Plugin, Surefire/Failsafe |
| `module-info.java` for test | Separate test module or patch production module for tests | Avoid duplicating production module descriptor | `module-info-patch.maven` |
| Jersey resource test | Verify endpoint, provider, filter, mapper | Resource/provider packages may need `opens` | Jersey Test Framework |
| MyBatis mapper test | Verify SQL, XML, mapping, type handler | Persistence mapper/entity packages may need `opens` | MyBatis, PostgreSQL, Testcontainers |
| Classpath vs module path | Know how tests are executed | Runtime behavior differs | Surefire/Failsafe config |
| Dependency guardrail | Prevent layer violation | REST must not depend on persistence | Maven dependency tree, scripts |
| Runtime parity | Test same startup mode as container | Avoid local/CI/Docker mismatch | Docker smoke test |
| Temporary access flags | Keep test-only access out of production | Do not pollute production module-info | `argLine`, `module-info-patch.maven` |

---

# 4. Testing Layers untuk JPMS Microservice

## 4.1 Unit Test Layer

Target:

```text
payment-domain
payment-application
small mapper/converter class
provider selection logic
```

Tidak perlu:

```text
Jersey server
PostgreSQL
Docker
MyBatis SqlSessionFactory
HTTP client nyata
cloud SDK nyata
```

Contoh domain test:

```java
package com.company.payment.domain.model;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.UUID;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;

class PaymentTest {

    @Test
    void shouldRejectNonPositiveAmount() {
        assertThrows(
            IllegalArgumentException.class,
            () -> new Payment(
                UUID.randomUUID(),
                BigDecimal.ZERO,
                "IDR",
                PaymentStatus.CREATED
            )
        );
    }
}
```

JUnit 5 tidak mengharuskan test class atau test method menjadi `public`, tetapi dokumentasi JUnit menyebutkan bahwa membuatnya `public` kadang mempermudah testing di module path dengan Java Module System. ([JUnit](https://docs.junit.org/5.10.2/user-guide/index.html))

---

## 4.2 Application Use Case Test

Gunakan fake repository/provider.

```java
package com.company.payment.application.usecase;

import com.company.payment.domain.model.Payment;
import com.company.payment.domain.port.PaymentRepository;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.UUID;

final class FakePaymentRepository implements PaymentRepository {

    private final Map<UUID, Payment> storage = new HashMap<>();

    @Override
    public Optional<Payment> findById(UUID id) {
        return Optional.ofNullable(storage.get(id));
    }

    @Override
    public Payment save(Payment payment) {
        storage.put(payment.id(), payment);
        return payment;
    }
}
```

Test:

```java
package com.company.payment.application.usecase;

import com.company.payment.application.command.CreatePaymentCommand;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.Currency;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;

class CreatePaymentUseCaseTest {

    @Test
    void shouldCreatePayment() {
        var repository = new FakePaymentRepository();
        var useCase = new CreatePaymentUseCase(repository);

        var result = useCase.execute(new CreatePaymentCommand(
            BigDecimal.valueOf(100_000),
            Currency.getInstance("IDR")
        ));

        assertEquals("IDR", result.currency().getCurrencyCode());
    }
}
```

Prinsip:

```text
Application test tidak butuh MyBatis.
Application test tidak butuh Jersey.
Application test tidak butuh PostgreSQL.
```

Kalau use case test butuh PostgreSQL, kemungkinan boundary application sudah bocor ke persistence.

---

# 5. Maven Surefire untuk Unit Test

## 5.1 Parent POM Baseline

Gunakan versi plugin yang dipin di parent POM, jangan mengandalkan default Maven.

```xml
<properties>
    <maven.compiler.release>17</maven.compiler.release>
    <junit.jupiter.version>${approved.junit.jupiter.version}</junit.jupiter.version>
    <maven.surefire.plugin.version>${approved.maven.surefire.plugin.version}</maven.surefire.plugin.version>
    <maven.failsafe.plugin.version>${approved.maven.failsafe.plugin.version}</maven.failsafe.plugin.version>
</properties>
```

JUnit Platform membutuhkan setidaknya satu `TestEngine` agar Maven Surefire atau Failsafe bisa menjalankan test; untuk JUnit Jupiter, dependency `org.junit.jupiter:junit-jupiter` dalam scope `test` adalah konfigurasi umum. ([JUnit](https://docs.junit.org/5.10.2/user-guide/index.html))

```xml
<dependencyManagement>
    <dependencies>
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.junit</groupId>
            <artifactId>junit-bom</artifactId>
            <version>${junit.jupiter.version}</version>
            <type>pom</type>
            <scope>import</scope>
        </dependency>
    </dependencies>
</dependencyManagement>
```

Child module:

```xml
<dependencies>
    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.junit.jupiter</groupId>
        <artifactId>junit-jupiter</artifactId>
        <scope>test</scope>
    </dependency>
</dependencies>
```

Surefire:

```xml
<build>
    <plugins>
        <plugin>
            <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
            <artifactId>maven-surefire-plugin</artifactId>
            <version>${maven.surefire.plugin.version}</version>
        </plugin>
    </plugins>
</build>
```

---

## 5.2 Naming Convention

Surefire default biasanya menjalankan unit test dengan pola seperti:

```text
*Test
*Tests
*TestCase
```

Practical convention:

```text
PaymentTest.java
CreatePaymentUseCaseTest.java
PaymentProviderRegistryTest.java
```

Jangan taruh integration test berat di Surefire. Pisahkan ke Failsafe.

---

# 6. Maven Failsafe untuk Integration Test

Failsafe punya goal `integration-test` untuk menjalankan integration test dan `verify` untuk memverifikasi hasilnya. Pemisahan ini berguna karena fase `verify` tetap bisa menjalankan cleanup/teardown dengan benar dibanding sekadar menggagalkan build di tengah fase test biasa. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/surefire/maven-failsafe-plugin/plugin-info.html))

## 6.1 Naming Convention

Failsafe convention umum:

```text
*IT.java
*ITCase.java
```

Contoh:

```text
PaymentResourceIT.java
PaymentMapperIT.java
PaymentServiceModulePathIT.java
```

## 6.2 Failsafe Config

```xml
<plugin>
    <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
    <artifactId>maven-failsafe-plugin</artifactId>
    <version>${maven.failsafe.plugin.version}</version>
    <executions>
        <execution>
            <goals>
                <goal>integration-test</goal>
                <goal>verify</goal>
            </goals>
        </execution>
    </executions>
</plugin>
```

Run:

```bash
mvn clean verify
```

Skip unit test only:

```bash
mvn verify -DskipTests
```

Skip integration test only, if configured with property:

```bash
mvn verify -DskipITs
```

---

# 7. JPMS Test Access Problem

Di JPMS, production module sering sengaja menutup internal package:

```java
module com.company.payment.domain {
    exports com.company.payment.domain.model;
    exports com.company.payment.domain.port;
}
```

Package ini tidak diekspor:

```text
com.company.payment.domain.internal
```

Masalahnya, test kadang ingin menguji internal policy:

```java
import com.company.payment.domain.internal.PaymentStatePolicy;
```

Jika production `module-info.java` tidak export package tersebut, test modular bisa gagal.

Ada tiga pendekatan.

---

## 7.1 Approach A - Test via Public API

Rekomendasi default.

```text
Test behavior melalui public domain model/use case.
Jangan test internal helper secara langsung kecuali benar-benar perlu.
```

Contoh:

```java
@Test
void cancelShouldRejectAlreadySettledPayment() {
    var payment = Payment.settled(...);

    assertThrows(
        InvalidPaymentStateException.class,
        payment::cancel
    );
}
```

Kelebihan:

```text
Production boundary tetap bersih.
Test tidak fragile terhadap refactor internal.
```

---

## 7.2 Approach B - Test-Specific `--add-exports` / `--add-opens`

Jika white-box test memang perlu, gunakan test-only access.

Contoh Surefire `argLine`:

```xml
<plugin>
    <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
    <artifactId>maven-surefire-plugin</artifactId>
    <version>${maven.surefire.plugin.version}</version>
    <configuration>
        <argLine>
            --add-exports com.company.payment.domain/com.company.payment.domain.internal=ALL-UNNAMED
        </argLine>
    </configuration>
</plugin>
```

Untuk reflection test:

```xml
<argLine>
    --add-opens com.company.payment.domain/com.company.payment.domain.internal=ALL-UNNAMED
</argLine>
```

Rule:

```text
Test-only flags boleh.
Production Docker/Kubernetes flags jangan ikut tercemar.
```

---

## 7.3 Approach C - `module-info-patch.maven`

Maven Compiler Plugin 4.x menyediakan `module-info-patch.maven` agar perubahan test-specific terhadap module descriptor tidak perlu menyalin seluruh `module-info.java` test. Dokumentasinya menyebut file ini bisa mendeklarasikan hal seperti `add-reads`, `add-exports`, dan `add-opens` untuk tujuan testing, sementara path dependency tetap dikelola POM. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/plugins/maven-compiler-plugin-4.x/module-info-patch.html))

Contoh:

```text
src/test/module-info-patch.maven
```

Isi:

```text
patch-module com.company.payment.domain {
    add-modules TEST-MODULE-PATH;

    add-reads org.junit.jupiter.api;

    add-exports com.company.payment.domain.internal
        to org.junit.jupiter.api;
}
```

Kapan cocok:

```text
1. Project benar-benar modular.
2. Banyak white-box test yang butuh akses internal.
3. Tim memakai Maven Compiler Plugin 4.x / Maven 4-ready setup.
4. Tidak ingin production module-info.java dibuat longgar.
```

Untuk project Maven 3 yang belum siap Maven Compiler Plugin 4.x, pendekatan `argLine` test-only sering lebih sederhana.

---

# 8. Test Module Descriptor Pattern

Surefire punya dokumentasi contoh JPMS test dengan module descriptor di `src/test/java/module-info.java`, termasuk contoh JUnit 5 dalam Maven multi-module project. Dokumentasi tersebut menekankan JDK 9+ dan bahwa test module dapat `requires` module utama dan dependency test. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/surefire/maven-surefire-plugin/examples/jpms.html))

Contoh:

```java
module com.company.payment.domain.test {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;
    requires org.junit.jupiter.api;

    opens com.company.payment.domain.test to org.junit.platform.commons;
}
```

Tetapi untuk banyak project enterprise, pendekatan ini bisa menambah kompleksitas karena perlu menjaga test module descriptor tetap sinkron dengan dependency test.

Practical recommendation:

```text
Untuk awal migrasi JPMS:
1. Tetap gunakan test classpath jika lebih stabil.
2. Pakai argLine test-only untuk akses internal terbatas.
3. Baru gunakan test module descriptor / module-info-patch jika tim sudah siap.
```

---

# 9. Jersey Resource Test

## 9.1 Kenapa Perlu?

Unit test use case tidak membuktikan:

```text
1. Resource terdaftar.
2. Path benar.
3. HTTP status benar.
4. Request DTO bisa deserialize.
5. Response DTO bisa serialize.
6. Exception mapper aktif.
7. Filter/provider aktif.
```

Jersey Test Framework memang dirancang untuk testing server-side components dan mendukung client preconfigured, beberapa container seperti Grizzly, in-memory, JDK HTTP, Simple, Jetty, external container, serta logging traffic. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest3x/test-framework.html))

---

## 9.2 JerseyTest Example

```java
package com.company.payment.rest.resource;

import com.company.payment.application.usecase.CreatePaymentUseCase;
import com.company.payment.rest.exception.PaymentExceptionMapper;
import com.company.payment.rest.mapper.PaymentRestMapper;
import org.glassfish.jersey.server.ResourceConfig;
import org.glassfish.jersey.test.JerseyTest;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import jakarta.ws.rs.client.Entity;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Application;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.MediaType;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;

class PaymentResourceIT extends JerseyTest {

    @Override
    protected Application configure() {
        var repository = new FakePaymentRepository();
        var useCase = new CreatePaymentUseCase(repository);
        var mapper = new PaymentRestMapper();

        return new ResourceConfig()
            .register(new PaymentResource(useCase, mapper))
            .register(PaymentExceptionMapper.class);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldCreatePayment() {
        var body = """
            {
              "amount": 100000,
              "currency": "IDR"
            }
            """;

        var response = target("payments")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
            .post(Entity.json(body));

        assertEquals(201, response.getStatus());
    }
}
```

Jersey Test Framework examples use a subclass of `JerseyTest` and override `configure()` to return a `ResourceConfig` with resource/provider registration; this maps well to JPMS because explicit registration avoids hidden package scanning behavior. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest3x/test-framework.html))

---

## 9.3 JPMS Descriptor Concern

REST module:

```java
module com.company.payment.rest {
    requires jakarta.ws.rs;
    requires com.company.payment.api;
    requires com.company.payment.application;

    exports com.company.payment.rest.resource
        to com.company.payment.bootstrap;

    opens com.company.payment.rest.resource;
    opens com.company.payment.rest.exception;
}
```

Jika test berada di unnamed module/classpath, test mungkin perlu:

```xml
<argLine>
    --add-exports com.company.payment.rest/com.company.payment.rest.resource=ALL-UNNAMED
</argLine>
```

Jika test berjalan sebagai named test module, target-nya sebaiknya module test:

```text
--add-exports com.company.payment.rest/com.company.payment.rest.resource=com.company.payment.rest.test
```

Rule:

```text
Jangan export resource package ke semua module hanya karena test butuh.
Gunakan test-only access.
```

---

# 10. Testing Exception Mapper

Contoh domain exception:

```java
package com.company.payment.domain.exception;

public class PaymentNotFoundException extends RuntimeException {
    public PaymentNotFoundException(String id) {
        super("Payment not found: " + id);
    }
}
```

Mapper:

```java
package com.company.payment.rest.exception;

import com.company.payment.api.error.ErrorResponse;
import com.company.payment.domain.exception.PaymentNotFoundException;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.ExceptionMapper;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

@Provider
public class PaymentNotFoundExceptionMapper
        implements ExceptionMapper<PaymentNotFoundException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(PaymentNotFoundException exception) {
        return Response.status(Response.Status.NOT_FOUND)
            .entity(new ErrorResponse("PAYMENT_NOT_FOUND", exception.getMessage()))
            .build();
    }
}
```

Test:

```java
@Test
void shouldMapPaymentNotFoundTo404() {
    var response = target("payments")
        .path("missing-id")
        .request()
        .get();

    assertEquals(404, response.getStatus());

    var error = response.readEntity(ErrorResponse.class);
    assertEquals("PAYMENT_NOT_FOUND", error.code());
}
```

Yang diuji:

```text
1. Exception mapper registered.
2. Domain exception diterjemahkan ke HTTP 404.
3. Error response serializable.
4. Response contract stabil.
```

---

# 11. MyBatis Integration Test with PostgreSQL

## 11.1 Kenapa Perlu DB Nyata?

Mapper test dengan mock tidak membuktikan:

```text
1. SQL valid untuk PostgreSQL.
2. column name benar.
3. resultMap benar.
4. typeHandler benar.
5. transaction behavior benar.
6. PL/pgSQL function/procedure behavior benar.
```

MyBatis Java API menyatakan `SqlSession` adalah interface utama untuk menjalankan command, mengambil mapper, dan mengelola transaksi; `SqlSessionFactory` membuat `SqlSession`, dan factory bisa dibangun dari XML, annotation, atau configuration object. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html))

---

## 11.2 Testcontainers PostgreSQL

Testcontainers menyediakan PostgreSQL module; dokumentasinya menunjukkan `PostgreSQLContainer` bisa dijalankan dari Java test dan juga mendukung JDBC URL `jdbc:tc:postgresql:...`. ([Testcontainers](https://java.testcontainers.org/modules/databases/postgres/))

Catatan versi penting: rilis Testcontainers 2.0.0 mengubah naming artifact menjadi prefix `testcontainers-` dan memindahkan beberapa package class container ke `org.testcontainers.<module-name>`. Jadi jangan copy dependency lama tanpa mengecek versi yang dipakai project. ([GitHub](https://github.com/testcontainers/testcontainers-java/releases))

---

## 11.3 Mapper Integration Test

```java
package com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.mapper;

import com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.entity.PaymentRecord;
import org.apache.ibatis.session.SqlSessionFactory;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.testcontainers.junit.jupiter.Container;
import org.testcontainers.junit.jupiter.Testcontainers;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.UUID;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

@Testcontainers
class PaymentMapperIT {

    @Container
    static final PostgreSQLContainer<?> postgres =
        new PostgreSQLContainer<>("postgres:16-alpine")
            .withDatabaseName("payment_test")
            .withUsername("payment")
            .withPassword("payment");

    private SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        runSchemaMigration(postgres.getJdbcUrl(), postgres.getUsername(), postgres.getPassword());

        sqlSessionFactory = TestMyBatisFactory.create(
            postgres.getJdbcUrl(),
            postgres.getUsername(),
            postgres.getPassword()
        );
    }

    @Test
    void shouldInsertAndFindPayment() {
        var id = UUID.randomUUID();

        try (var session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false)) {
            var mapper = session.getMapper(PaymentMapper.class);

            mapper.insert(new PaymentRecord(
                id,
                BigDecimal.valueOf(100_000),
                "IDR",
                "CREATED"
            ));

            session.commit();
        }

        try (var session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession()) {
            var mapper = session.getMapper(PaymentMapper.class);

            var found = mapper.findById(id);

            assertTrue(found.isPresent());
            assertEquals("IDR", found.get().currency());
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 11.4 Test MyBatis Factory

```java
package com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis;

import org.apache.ibatis.datasource.pooled.PooledDataSource;
import org.apache.ibatis.mapping.Environment;
import org.apache.ibatis.session.Configuration;
import org.apache.ibatis.session.SqlSessionFactory;
import org.apache.ibatis.session.SqlSessionFactoryBuilder;
import org.apache.ibatis.transaction.jdbc.JdbcTransactionFactory;

public final class TestMyBatisFactory {

    private TestMyBatisFactory() {
    }

    public static SqlSessionFactory create(
            String jdbcUrl,
            String username,
            String password
    ) {
        var dataSource = new PooledDataSource(
            "org.postgresql.Driver",
            jdbcUrl,
            username,
            password
        );

        var environment = new Environment(
            "test",
            new JdbcTransactionFactory(),
            dataSource
        );

        var configuration = new Configuration(environment);
        configuration.addMapper(PaymentMapper.class);

        return new SqlSessionFactoryBuilder().build(configuration);
    }
}
```

MyBatis juga mendukung multiple environments di configuration, tetapi satu `SqlSessionFactory` hanya memilih satu environment; untuk beberapa database, perlu beberapa `SqlSessionFactory`. Ini penting untuk test agar environment test tidak tercampur dengan development/production. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html))

---

# 12. JPMS Descriptor untuk Persistence Test

Production descriptor:

```java
module com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis {
    requires java.sql;
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    exports com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.repository
        to com.company.payment.bootstrap;

    opens com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.mapper;
    opens com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.entity;
    opens com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.typehandler;
}
```

Untuk test yang butuh akses ke mapper/entity package, ada dua opsi.

## 12.1 Export Test-Only via Surefire/Failsafe

```xml
<argLine>
    --add-exports com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis/com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.mapper=ALL-UNNAMED
    --add-exports com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis/com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.entity=ALL-UNNAMED
</argLine>
```

## 12.2 Jangan Export Permanen

Jangan ubah production descriptor menjadi:

```java
exports com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.mapper;
exports com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.entity;
```

hanya karena integration test butuh import class tersebut.

Rule:

```text
Production exports = production contract.
Test access = test-only patch/flag.
```

---

# 13. Module Boundary Tests

Testing JPMS juga berarti memastikan dependency boundary tidak bocor.

## 13.1 Forbidden Dependency Script

Misal:

```text
payment-rest must not depend on payment-persistence-mybatis
payment-domain must not depend on jakarta.ws.rs
payment-application must not depend on org.mybatis
```

Command:

```bash
mvn -pl payment-rest dependency:tree \
  -DoutputType=json \
  -DoutputFile=payment-rest/target/dependency-tree.json
```

Simple check:

```bash
if grep -q "payment-persistence-mybatis" payment-rest/target/dependency-tree.json; then
  echo "ERROR: payment-rest must not depend on payment-persistence-mybatis"
  exit 1
fi
```

## 13.2 Compile-Time Boundary Test

Cukup dengan tidak memberi dependency Maven dan tidak memberi `requires`.

REST descriptor:

```java
module com.company.payment.rest {
    requires jakarta.ws.rs;
    requires com.company.payment.api;
    requires com.company.payment.application;
}
```

Kalau developer mencoba:

```java
import com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.mapper.PaymentMapper;
```

harus compile gagal.

Ini test paling murah: boundary dijaga oleh compiler.

---

# 14. Classpath vs Module Path Test Strategy

## 14.1 Classpath Test

Ini paling kompatibel:

```xml
<plugin>
    <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
    <artifactId>maven-surefire-plugin</artifactId>
    <configuration>
        <useModulePath>false</useModulePath>
    </configuration>
</plugin>
```

Cocok untuk:

```text
1. Migrasi awal.
2. Banyak framework reflection.
3. Legacy test setup.
4. Target production masih java -jar/classpath.
```

## 14.2 Module Path Test

Cocok jika:

```text
1. Target runtime adalah module path.
2. module-info.java sudah stabil.
3. third-party module names sudah diinventaris.
4. split package sudah dibersihkan.
5. opens/exports sudah jelas.
```

Surefire memiliki dokumentasi khusus untuk JPMS tests dan contoh dengan TestNG, JUnit 4, serta JUnit 5 dalam Maven multi-module project. Ini menunjukkan bahwa testing module path memang perlu diperlakukan sebagai konfigurasi eksplisit, bukan diasumsikan otomatis. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/surefire/maven-surefire-plugin/examples/jpms.html))

---

# 15. Dual Mode Testing

Untuk migrasi enterprise, jalankan dua mode.

```text
Mode A - blocking:
mvn clean verify
classpath-compatible tests

Mode B - report-only awalnya:
module-path dry run
jdeps report
module name inventory
```

Script:

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

mvn clean verify

mkdir -p target/jpms

mvn -pl payment-bootstrap dependency:copy-dependencies \
  -DincludeScope=runtime \
  -DoutputDirectory=payment-bootstrap/target/lib

for j in payment-bootstrap/target/lib/*.jar; do
  echo "==== $j" >> target/jpms/module-name-inventory.txt
  jar --describe-module --file "$j" >> target/jpms/module-name-inventory.txt 2>&1 || true
done

jdeps --multi-release 17 \
  --summary \
  payment-bootstrap/target/payment-bootstrap-*.jar \
  > target/jpms/jdeps-summary.txt || true
```

Setelah stabil, module-path dry run bisa dibuat blocking.

---

# 16. Testing SPI / ServiceLoader

Dari seri 9, provider harus ditemukan di runtime.

## 16.1 Unit Test Tanpa ServiceLoader

```java
class PaymentProviderRegistryTest {

    @Test
    void shouldSelectProviderById() {
        var registry = new PaymentProviderRegistry(List.of(
            new FakePaymentProvider("midtrans"),
            new FakePaymentProvider("stripe")
        ));

        var provider = registry.byId("midtrans");

        assertEquals("midtrans", provider.descriptor().providerId());
    }
}
```

## 16.2 Integration Test dengan ServiceLoader

```java
@Test
void shouldLoadPaymentProviderFromRuntime() {
    var providers = ServiceLoader.load(PaymentProvider.class)
        .stream()
        .map(ServiceLoader.Provider::get)
        .toList();

    assertTrue(
        providers.stream()
            .anyMatch(provider -> provider.descriptor().providerId().equals("midtrans"))
    );
}
```

JPMS requirement:

```java
module com.company.payment.application {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    uses com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentProvider;
}
```

Provider module:

```java
module com.company.payment.integration.midtrans {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    provides com.company.payment.domain.spi.PaymentProvider
        with com.company.payment.integration.midtrans.MidtransPaymentProvider;
}
```

Yang diuji:

```text
1. Provider module masuk runtime.
2. uses/provides benar.
3. Provider id tidak duplicate.
4. Config selection berhasil.
```

---

# 17. Test Fixture Module

Untuk multi-module project, hindari copy-paste fake object di banyak module.

Buat:

```text
payment-test-fixture
├── FakePaymentRepository
├── FakePaymentProvider
├── PaymentTestData
├── TestClock
└── TestIds
```

Descriptor:

```java
module com.company.payment.test.fixture {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    exports com.company.payment.test.fixture;
}
```

POM dependency:

```xml
<dependency>
    <groupId>com.company.payment</groupId>
    <artifactId>payment-test-fixture</artifactId>
    <version>${project.version}</version>
    <scope>test</scope>
</dependency>
```

Rule:

```text
Test fixture boleh depends ke domain.
Test fixture tidak boleh depends ke Jersey/MyBatis kecuali memang fixture integration khusus.
```

Jika fixture mulai punya Jersey, MyBatis, PostgreSQL, cloud SDK, pecah:

```text
payment-domain-test-fixture
payment-rest-test-fixture
payment-persistence-test-fixture
```

---

# 18. Testcontainers Scope dan JPMS

Dependency test untuk PostgreSQL container:

```xml
<dependency>
    <groupId>org.testcontainers</groupId>
    <artifactId>${testcontainers.postgres.artifact}</artifactId>
    <version>${testcontainers.version}</version>
    <scope>test</scope>
</dependency>
```

Karena Testcontainers 2.0 mengubah artifact naming dan package relocation, simpan artifact id di property atau BOM internal project agar transisi versi tidak tersebar di banyak POM. ([GitHub](https://github.com/testcontainers/testcontainers-java/releases))

Practical rule:

```text
Testcontainers = test scope.
Jangan sampai masuk runtime image production.
```

Cek:

```bash
mvn -pl payment-bootstrap dependency:tree -Dincludes=org.testcontainers
```

Expected:

```text
Tidak muncul di runtime/compile dependency production module.
```

---

# 19. Test Data Strategy

## 19.1 Domain Test Data

```java
public final class PaymentTestData {

    private PaymentTestData() {
    }

    public static Payment createdPayment() {
        return new Payment(
            UUID.randomUUID(),
            BigDecimal.valueOf(100_000),
            Currency.getInstance("IDR"),
            PaymentStatus.CREATED
        );
    }
}
```

## 19.2 SQL Test Data

Untuk integration test, prefer setup eksplisit:

```sql
insert into payments (
    id,
    amount,
    currency,
    status,
    created_at
) values (
    '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001',
    100000,
    'IDR',
    'CREATED',
    now()
);
```

## 19.3 Anti-pattern

```text
1. Test bergantung pada urutan test lain.
2. Test memakai database shared antar developer.
3. Test data tidak dibersihkan.
4. Test memakai production-like secret.
5. Test silently skips karena Docker tidak jalan tanpa laporan jelas.
```

---

# 20. Production `module-info.java` Hygiene

Jangan lakukan ini demi test:

```java
open module com.company.payment.domain {
    exports com.company.payment.domain.model;
    exports com.company.payment.domain.port;
    exports com.company.payment.domain.internal;
}
```

Lebih baik:

```java
module com.company.payment.domain {
    exports com.company.payment.domain.model;
    exports com.company.payment.domain.port;
}
```

Lalu test internal via:

```text
1. public behavior test
2. test-only --add-exports
3. test-only module-info-patch.maven
4. test fixture module
```

Rule:

```text
Production module descriptor adalah production contract.
Test jangan mengubah production architecture.
```

---

# 21. Failure Modes

| Failure | Symptom | Likely cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|
| Test cannot access internal class | compile error package not visible | package not exported | Test via public API or use test-only `--add-exports` |
| JUnit cannot discover tests | no tests run | missing TestEngine / wrong plugin config | Add `junit-jupiter` test dependency |
| Works in IDE, fails Maven | IDE uses classpath, Maven uses module path or vice versa | runtime mode mismatch | Align IDE/Maven config |
| Works Surefire, fails Failsafe | integration test classpath different | plugin config not mirrored | Share plugin config carefully |
| Jersey endpoint 404 in test | resource not registered | missing ResourceConfig registration | Explicit `register(...)` |
| Exception mapper ignored | provider not registered | mapper not in ResourceConfig/package scan | Register mapper explicitly |
| DTO deserialize error | package not open / constructor issue | JPMS reflection or DTO shape | Add `opens`, constructor/getter, provider config |
| MyBatis mapper not found | XML not loaded / mapper not registered | resource path/config issue | Verify mapper XML packaging |
| PostgreSQL test flaky | shared DB / ordering / parallel issue | no isolation | Testcontainers per class/suite, cleanup |
| Provider not found | ServiceLoader no provider | provider JAR absent or missing `provides` | Include provider module and descriptor |

---

# 22. CI Pipeline Recommendation

```yaml
name: Java Modular Test Pipeline

on:
  pull_request:
  push:
    branches: [ main ]

jobs:
  test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: '17'
          cache: maven

      - name: Unit and integration tests
        run: mvn -B clean verify

      - name: Dependency boundary reports
        run: |
          for module in payment-domain payment-api payment-application payment-rest payment-persistence-mybatis payment-bootstrap; do
            mvn -B -pl "$module" dependency:tree \
              -DoutputType=json \
              -DoutputFile="$module/target/dependency-tree.json"
          done

      - name: Check dependency boundaries
        run: python3 scripts/check-dependency-boundaries.py

      - name: JPMS inventory
        run: |
          mkdir -p target/jpms
          mvn -B -pl payment-bootstrap dependency:copy-dependencies \
            -DincludeScope=runtime \
            -DoutputDirectory=payment-bootstrap/target/lib

          for j in payment-bootstrap/target/lib/*.jar; do
            echo "==== $j" >> target/jpms/module-name-inventory.txt
            jar --describe-module --file "$j" >> target/jpms/module-name-inventory.txt 2>&1 || true
          done

          jdeps --multi-release 17 \
            --summary \
            payment-bootstrap/target/payment-bootstrap-*.jar \
            > target/jpms/jdeps-summary.txt || true

      - name: Upload reports
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: java-modular-test-reports
          path: |
            **/target/surefire-reports/**
            **/target/failsafe-reports/**
            **/target/dependency-tree.json
            target/jpms/*.txt
```

Tahap awal `jdeps` dan module inventory boleh report-only. Setelah stabil, buat blocking untuk boundary violation.

---

# 23. Review Checklist

| Area | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Unit test | Apakah domain/application bisa dites tanpa Jersey/MyBatis/PostgreSQL? |
| Integration test | Apakah REST dan DB behavior dites di Failsafe? |
| JPMS access | Apakah test-only access tidak membuat production module terbuka? |
| Surefire/Failsafe | Apakah lifecycle unit vs integration test terpisah? |
| JUnit engine | Apakah `junit-jupiter` tersedia sebagai test dependency? |
| Jersey test | Apakah resource, provider, filter, exception mapper terdaftar eksplisit? |
| MyBatis test | Apakah mapper XML, resultMap, typeHandler dites dengan PostgreSQL nyata? |
| Testcontainers | Apakah dependency hanya test scope dan versi/artifact sesuai standar project? |
| Module path | Apakah test mode classpath/module-path jelas? |
| CI parity | Apakah CI menjalankan mode yang sama dengan runtime target? |
| Temporary flags | Apakah `--add-opens` / `--add-exports` punya alasan dan tidak masuk production? |
| Dependency guardrail | Apakah REST tetap tidak bisa depends persistence? |

---

# 24. Practical Recommendation

Untuk Java Jersey/JAX-RS + JPMS enterprise microservice:

```text
1. Test domain/application secara cepat dengan unit test biasa.
2. Jangan paksa semua test langsung module-path pada awal migrasi.
3. Gunakan Surefire untuk unit test dan Failsafe untuk integration test.
4. Test Jersey resource dengan ResourceConfig eksplisit.
5. Test MyBatis dengan PostgreSQL nyata via Testcontainers.
6. Jangan membuka production module hanya karena test butuh akses internal.
7. Gunakan test-only --add-exports / --add-opens atau module-info-patch.maven.
8. Pisahkan test fixture module agar fake/test data reusable.
9. Jadikan dependency boundary check bagian dari CI.
10. Selaraskan mode test dengan Docker/Kubernetes startup target.
```

Target akhirnya:

```text
Production module-info.java tetap ketat.
Test tetap powerful.
Runtime behavior tetap terbukti.
Boundary architecture tetap defensible.
```
