## Response:
7/15/2026, 1:40:11 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 11/12**. Fokusnya runtime dan deployment: bagaimana JPMS berpengaruh ke packaging, Docker image, Kubernetes manifest, `jlink`, JVM flags, health check, observability, dan strategi rollout produksi.

# Seri 11/12 - Runtime, Docker, Kubernetes & `jlink` untuk JPMS Microservice

## 1. Introduction

Seri ini membahas bagaimana JPMS berpengaruh ke **runtime packaging dan deployment** untuk Java 17+ Jersey/JAX-RS microservice.

Di tahap sebelumnya kita sudah membahas:

```text
1. module-info.java
2. Maven multi-module
3. Jersey/JAX-RS reflection
4. MyBatis reflection
5. dependency hygiene
6. classpath vs module path migration
7. ServiceLoader/SPI
8. modular testing
```

Sekarang fokusnya adalah:

```text
Bagaimana aplikasi modular itu dijalankan di Docker/Kubernetes?
Apakah tetap java -jar?
Apakah pakai --module-path?
Kapan pakai jlink?
Bagaimana health check dan rollout strategy-nya?
```

`jlink` adalah tool JDK yang menghubungkan sekumpulan module beserta transitive dependency-nya untuk membuat **custom runtime image**; dokumentasi Oracle juga menekankan bahwa developer bertanggung jawab memperbarui custom runtime image tersebut. Artinya, `jlink` bisa membantu membuat runtime lebih kecil dan terkontrol, tetapi juga menambah tanggung jawab patching/security update. ([Oracle Documentation](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/specs/man/jlink.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Runtime Deployment Options

Untuk Java Jersey/JAX-RS microservice, ada empat pola runtime utama.

```text
Runtime Packaging Options
│
├── 1. Fat JAR / executable JAR
│   └── java -jar app.jar
│
├── 2. Thin JAR + lib folder
│   └── java -cp "app.jar:lib/*" Main
│
├── 3. Module path runtime
│   └── java --module-path "mods:lib" --module module/main
│
└── 4. jlink custom runtime image
    └── custom-java/bin/java --module ...
```

Jangan langsung menganggap opsi 4 paling baik. Untuk service enterprise yang memakai Jersey, MyBatis, JSON serializer, Bean Validation, observability agent, dan cloud/on-prem runtime, opsi yang paling aman sering dimulai dari **classpath runtime + JPMS-aware build**, lalu baru dievaluasi ke module path atau `jlink`.

---

# 3. Diagram Deployment Flow

```text
Source Code
│
├── Maven modules
│   ├── payment-domain
│   ├── payment-api
│   ├── payment-application
│   ├── payment-rest
│   ├── payment-persistence-mybatis
│   └── payment-bootstrap
│
├── Build
│   ├── mvn clean verify
│   ├── unit tests
│   ├── integration tests
│   ├── dependency guardrail
│   └── jdeps/module inventory
│
├── Runtime Packaging
│   ├── fat JAR
│   ├── thin JAR + lib
│   ├── module path layout
│   └── jlink runtime image
│
├── Docker Image
│   ├── base image
│   ├── app artifacts
│   ├── JVM flags
│   ├── non-root user
│   └── entrypoint
│
└── Kubernetes
    ├── Deployment
    ├── ConfigMap/Secret
    ├── livenessProbe
    ├── readinessProbe
    ├── startupProbe
    ├── resources
    ├── rolling update
    └── observability
```

---

# 4. Decomposition Map

| Area | Skill | Practical usage | Failure mode |
|---|---|---|---|
| Runtime mode | Membedakan `java -jar`, classpath, module path, `jlink` | Menentukan cara app dijalankan | Salah klaim sudah JPMS runtime |
| Fat JAR | Satu artifact executable | Simpel untuk Docker/K8s | Bisa menyembunyikan classpath conflict |
| Thin JAR | App JAR + dependency folder | Lebih eksplisit dependency runtime | Lib folder tidak sinkron |
| Module path | Strict JPMS runtime | Enforce `requires`, `exports`, `opens` | Reflection/split package error |
| `jlink` | Custom Java runtime | Runtime lebih kecil/terkontrol | Perlu patching runtime image sendiri |
| Docker base image | Pilih JDK/JRE/runtime base | Build dan run image | Salah pakai JDK di runtime |
| Multi-stage build | Build dengan JDK, run dengan JRE/custom runtime | Image lebih bersih | Build artifact tidak reproducible |
| JVM flags | Memory, GC, module access, observability | Tuning container runtime | Flags liar di Helm/ENV |
| Health endpoint | `/live`, `/ready`, `/startup` | K8s probe target | Probe salah restart pod terus |
| Graceful shutdown | SIGTERM handling | Rolling update aman | Request putus saat deploy |
| Config/Secret | Environment/config file/secret | Provider config, DB URL, API key | Secret bocor ke image/log |
| Observability | Logs, metrics, traces | Debug production behavior | Provider/module/runtime tidak terlihat |
| Rollout strategy | Rolling/canary/blue-green | Safe production migration | Module-path change langsung full blast |

---

# 5. Runtime Mode 1 - Fat JAR / `java -jar`

## 5.1 Contoh Dockerfile

```dockerfile
FROM eclipse-temurin:17-jre

WORKDIR /app

COPY payment-bootstrap/target/payment-service.jar app.jar

EXPOSE 8080

ENTRYPOINT ["java", "-jar", "app.jar"]
```

Eclipse Temurin tersedia sebagai official Docker image dan umum dipakai untuk containerized Java workload; Adoptium juga menyediakan dokumentasi container image untuk varian Temurin. ([Adoptium](https://adoptium.net/installation/containers?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 5.2 Kapan Cocok?

```text
1. Service existing sudah stabil dengan java -jar.
2. Banyak framework reflection-heavy.
3. Tim belum siap module-path runtime.
4. Deployment pipeline sudah mapan.
5. Fokus awal adalah boundary source/build, bukan runtime strict JPMS.
```

## 5.3 JPMS Implication

Jika app berjalan dengan:

```bash
java -jar app.jar
```

atau:

```bash
java -cp "app.jar:lib/*" ...
```

maka runtime umumnya masih classpath-style. JAR modular yang diletakkan di classpath tetap masuk unnamed module, sehingga runtime tidak setara dengan strict module path. Dev.java menjelaskan bahwa semua JAR di classpath, modular atau tidak, menjadi bagian dari unnamed module. ([Dev.java](https://dev.java/learn/modules/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical conclusion:

```text
Fat JAR boleh tetap dipakai.
Tapi jangan klaim production runtime sudah strict JPMS kalau entrypoint masih java -jar.
```

---

# 6. Runtime Mode 2 - Thin JAR + `lib/`

## 6.1 Layout

```text
/app
├── app.jar
└── lib/
    ├── payment-domain.jar
    ├── payment-api.jar
    ├── payment-application.jar
    ├── payment-rest.jar
    ├── payment-persistence-mybatis.jar
    ├── jersey-server.jar
    ├── mybatis.jar
    └── postgresql.jar
```

## 6.2 Dockerfile

```dockerfile
FROM eclipse-temurin:17-jre

WORKDIR /app

COPY payment-bootstrap/target/payment-bootstrap.jar app.jar
COPY payment-bootstrap/target/lib/ lib/

EXPOSE 8080

ENTRYPOINT [
  "java",
  "-cp",
  "app.jar:lib/*",
  "com.company.payment.bootstrap.Main"
]
```

## 6.3 Kapan Cocok?

```text
1. Ingin dependency runtime lebih terlihat daripada fat JAR.
2. Ingin debug versi dependency di container.
3. Belum siap module path.
4. Ingin transisi bertahap menuju module path layout.
```

## 6.4 Risiko

```text
1. Classpath order bisa memengaruhi class yang dimuat.
2. Duplicate classes bisa tersembunyi.
3. Lib folder harus sinkron dengan hasil build.
4. Service provider file harus ikut benar.
```

Tambahkan CI guardrail:

```bash
mvn -pl payment-bootstrap dependency:copy-dependencies \
  -DincludeScope=runtime \
  -DoutputDirectory=payment-bootstrap/target/lib

mvn dependency:tree
jdeps --multi-release 17 --summary payment-bootstrap/target/payment-bootstrap-*.jar
```

---

# 7. Runtime Mode 3 - Module Path

## 7.1 Layout

```text
/app
├── mods/
│   ├── payment-domain.jar
│   ├── payment-api.jar
│   ├── payment-application.jar
│   ├── payment-rest.jar
│   ├── payment-persistence-mybatis.jar
│   └── payment-bootstrap.jar
│
└── lib/
    ├── jersey-server.jar
    ├── mybatis.jar
    ├── postgresql.jar
    └── other-runtime-dependencies.jar
```

## 7.2 Entrypoint

```bash
java \
  --module-path "mods:lib" \
  --module com.company.payment.bootstrap/com.company.payment.bootstrap.Main
```

JEP 261 menjelaskan bahwa application module path berisi compiled definitions dari library/application modules, dan module definitions pada path tersebut bersama system modules membentuk universe of observable modules. ([OpenJDK](https://openjdk.org/jeps/261?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 7.3 Dockerfile

```dockerfile
FROM eclipse-temurin:17-jre

WORKDIR /app

COPY runtime/mods/ mods/
COPY runtime/lib/ lib/

EXPOSE 8080

ENTRYPOINT [
  "java",
  "--module-path", "mods:lib",
  "--module", "com.company.payment.bootstrap/com.company.payment.bootstrap.Main"
]
```

## 7.4 Kapan Cocok?

```text
1. Semua internal modules punya module-info.java yang stabil.
2. Package reflection sudah jelas dengan opens.
3. Split package sudah dibersihkan.
4. Dependency third-party sudah diinventaris.
5. Integration test module-path sudah hijau.
6. Docker/K8s entrypoint sudah sama dengan CI test.
```

## 7.5 Failure Mode Umum

| Error | Arti | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| `Module not found` | JAR tidak ada di module path / nama module salah | Cek `jar --describe-module` |
| `package is not visible` | Missing `requires` / `exports` | Perbaiki descriptor jika boundary sah |
| `does not open package` | Reflection ditolak | Tambahkan `opens` minimal |
| `reads package from both` | Split package | Rename/consolidate package |
| Provider tidak ditemukan | SPI provider module tidak observable | Pastikan provider JAR masuk module path |

---

# 8. Runtime Mode 4 - `jlink` Custom Runtime Image

## 8.1 Apa Itu `jlink`

`jlink` membuat custom runtime image dari module yang dibutuhkan aplikasi beserta transitive dependency-nya. Ini berguna jika ingin runtime Java lebih kecil, lebih terkontrol, dan tidak membawa seluruh JDK/JRE standar. ([Oracle Documentation](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/specs/man/jlink.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 8.2 Command Dasar

```bash
jlink \
  --module-path "$JAVA_HOME/jmods:runtime/mods:runtime/lib" \
  --add-modules com.company.payment.bootstrap \
  --output target/payment-runtime
```

Dengan optimasi umum:

```bash
jlink \
  --module-path "$JAVA_HOME/jmods:runtime/mods:runtime/lib" \
  --add-modules com.company.payment.bootstrap \
  --strip-debug \
  --no-header-files \
  --no-man-pages \
  --compress=2 \
  --output target/payment-runtime
```

## 8.3 Struktur Output

```text
target/payment-runtime/
├── bin/
│   └── java
├── conf/
├── legal/
├── lib/
└── release
```

Run:

```bash
target/payment-runtime/bin/java \
  --module com.company.payment.bootstrap/com.company.payment.bootstrap.Main
```

## 8.4 Dockerfile dengan `jlink`

```dockerfile
FROM eclipse-temurin:17-jdk AS build

WORKDIR /workspace

COPY . .

RUN ./mvnw -B clean package

RUN mkdir -p runtime/mods runtime/lib && \
    cp payment-domain/target/payment-domain-*.jar runtime/mods/ && \
    cp payment-api/target/payment-api-*.jar runtime/mods/ && \
    cp payment-application/target/payment-application-*.jar runtime/mods/ && \
    cp payment-rest/target/payment-rest-*.jar runtime/mods/ && \
    cp payment-persistence-mybatis/target/payment-persistence-mybatis-*.jar runtime/mods/ && \
    cp payment-bootstrap/target/payment-bootstrap-*.jar runtime/mods/

RUN ./mvnw -B -pl payment-bootstrap dependency:copy-dependencies \
    -DincludeScope=runtime \
    -DoutputDirectory=runtime/lib

RUN jlink \
    --module-path "$JAVA_HOME/jmods:runtime/mods:runtime/lib" \
    --add-modules com.company.payment.bootstrap \
    --strip-debug \
    --no-header-files \
    --no-man-pages \
    --compress=2 \
    --output /opt/payment-runtime

FROM debian:bookworm-slim

WORKDIR /app

COPY --from=build /opt/payment-runtime /opt/payment-runtime
COPY --from=build /workspace/runtime/mods mods
COPY --from=build /workspace/runtime/lib lib

EXPOSE 8080

ENTRYPOINT [
  "/opt/payment-runtime/bin/java",
  "--module-path", "mods:lib",
  "--module", "com.company.payment.bootstrap/com.company.payment.bootstrap.Main"
]
```

## 8.5 Caveat Penting

`jlink` cocok setelah module-path runtime stabil. Jika dependency masih banyak automatic/non-modular modules, reflection belum jelas, atau split package belum bersih, `jlink` biasanya memperbesar effort migrasi. Oracle juga menekankan custom runtime image harus dipelihara/diupdate oleh developer, jadi jangan lupa security patching runtime image. ([Oracle Documentation](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/specs/man/jlink.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical rule:

```text
Do not start JPMS migration with jlink.
Start with dependency hygiene and module-path dry run.
Use jlink after runtime graph is stable.
```

---

# 9. Build Pipeline untuk Runtime Artifacts

## 9.1 Build Stages

```text
CI Pipeline
│
├── compile
│   └── mvn clean compile
│
├── unit test
│   └── mvn test
│
├── integration test
│   └── mvn verify
│
├── dependency hygiene
│   ├── dependency:tree
│   ├── banned dependencies
│   ├── duplicate classes
│   └── boundary guardrail
│
├── JPMS reports
│   ├── jar --describe-module
│   ├── jdeps
│   └── split package check
│
├── package runtime
│   ├── fat JAR / thin JAR / module path / jlink
│
├── docker build
│   └── container image
│
├── smoke test
│   └── run container + curl health endpoint
│
└── deploy
    └── Kubernetes rollout
```

## 9.2 Script: Module Name Inventory

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

mkdir -p target/jpms

mvn -B -pl payment-bootstrap dependency:copy-dependencies \
  -DincludeScope=runtime \
  -DoutputDirectory=payment-bootstrap/target/lib

for j in payment-bootstrap/target/lib/*.jar; do
  echo "==== $j" >> target/jpms/module-name-inventory.txt
  jar --describe-module --file "$j" >> target/jpms/module-name-inventory.txt 2>&1 || true
done
```

## 9.3 Script: `jdeps`

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

mkdir -p target/jpms

jdeps \
  --multi-release 17 \
  --summary \
  payment-bootstrap/target/payment-bootstrap-*.jar \
  > target/jpms/jdeps-summary.txt || true

jdeps \
  --multi-release 17 \
  --print-module-deps \
  payment-bootstrap/target/payment-bootstrap-*.jar \
  > target/jpms/jdeps-module-deps.txt || true
```

---

# 10. JVM Flags in Container

## 10.1 Container-Aware Memory

Untuk Java 17, JVM sudah container-aware secara default, tetapi tetap penting menentukan memory behavior secara eksplisit di production.

Contoh:

```bash
java \
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=75 \
  -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=50 \
  -XX:+ExitOnOutOfMemoryError \
  -jar app.jar
```

Atau module path:

```bash
java \
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=75 \
  -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=50 \
  -XX:+ExitOnOutOfMemoryError \
  --module-path "mods:lib" \
  --module com.company.payment.bootstrap/com.company.payment.bootstrap.Main
```

## 10.2 Logging JVM Flags

```bash
-Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,level,tags
```

Contoh:

```bash
java \
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=75 \
  -XX:+ExitOnOutOfMemoryError \
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,level,tags \
  -jar app.jar
```

## 10.3 Module Access Flags

Hindari menjadikan ini permanen:

```bash
--add-opens
--add-exports
--add-reads
```

Jika diperlukan sementara:

```bash
--add-opens=com.company.payment.api/com.company.payment.api.request=com.fasterxml.jackson.databind
```

Policy:

```text
1. Setiap --add-opens harus punya owner.
2. Harus ada reason.
3. Harus ada target removal.
4. Jangan tersebar di banyak Helm values.
5. Jika permanen, pindahkan ke module-info.java bila desainnya benar.
```

---

# 11. Docker Best Practices untuk JPMS Service

## 11.1 Gunakan Multi-stage Build

```dockerfile
FROM eclipse-temurin:17-jdk AS build

WORKDIR /workspace

COPY . .

RUN ./mvnw -B clean verify package

FROM eclipse-temurin:17-jre

WORKDIR /app

COPY --from=build /workspace/payment-bootstrap/target/payment-service.jar app.jar

EXPOSE 8080

ENTRYPOINT ["java", "-jar", "app.jar"]
```

## 11.2 Jalankan sebagai Non-root User

```dockerfile
FROM eclipse-temurin:17-jre

WORKDIR /app

RUN useradd --system --uid 10001 appuser

COPY payment-bootstrap/target/payment-service.jar app.jar

USER 10001

EXPOSE 8080

ENTRYPOINT ["java", "-jar", "app.jar"]
```

## 11.3 Jangan Bake Secret ke Image

Buruk:

```dockerfile
ENV DB_PASSWORD=super-secret
ENV MIDTRANS_API_KEY=secret
```

Lebih baik:

```text
Secret masuk dari Kubernetes Secret / secret manager / runtime environment.
Image harus reusable across environment.
```

---

# 12. Kubernetes Probes

Kubernetes menyediakan **liveness**, **readiness**, dan **startup probes** untuk memonitor container; probe adalah diagnostic berkala oleh kubelet, dan hasilnya bisa membuat container direstart atau dikeluarkan dari traffic service. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/pods/probes/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 12.1 Endpoint Design

```text
/live
  - Apakah proses JVM masih hidup?
  - Tidak cek DB/external dependency berat.
  - Jika gagal, pod boleh direstart.

/ready
  - Apakah service siap menerima traffic?
  - Boleh cek dependency kritikal ringan.
  - Jika gagal, pod dikeluarkan dari traffic.

/startup
  - Apakah aplikasi sudah selesai startup?
  - Berguna untuk app startup lama.
  - Selama startupProbe belum sukses, liveness/readiness bisa ditunda.
```

## 12.2 Jersey Resource Health Example

```java
package com.company.payment.rest.resource;

import jakarta.ws.rs.GET;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Path;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;

@Path("/health")
public class HealthResource {

    @GET
    @Path("/live")
    public Response live() {
        return Response.ok().build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/ready")
    public Response ready() {
        // Keep this lightweight.
        // Optionally check DB pool readiness or essential dependency.
        return Response.ok().build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/startup")
    public Response startup() {
        return Response.ok().build();
    }
}
```

## 12.3 Kubernetes Deployment Probe Example

```yaml
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: payment-service
spec:
  replicas: 3
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: payment-service
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: payment-service
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: payment-service
          image: registry.example.com/payment-service:1.0.0
          ports:
            - containerPort: 8080

          startupProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/startup
              port: 8080
            failureThreshold: 30
            periodSeconds: 2

          readinessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/ready
              port: 8080
            initialDelaySeconds: 5
            periodSeconds: 5
            timeoutSeconds: 2
            failureThreshold: 3

          livenessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/live
              port: 8080
            initialDelaySeconds: 30
            periodSeconds: 10
            timeoutSeconds: 2
            failureThreshold: 3
```

## 12.4 Probe Rule of Thumb

```text
Liveness:
  Jangan cek PostgreSQL/external payment gateway.
  Kalau DB down lalu liveness gagal, Kubernetes akan restart pod terus tanpa menyelesaikan masalah DB.

Readiness:
  Boleh cek apakah service bisa menerima traffic.
  Jika DB kritikal dan tidak ada degraded mode, readiness boleh fail.

Startup:
  Pakai untuk service yang startup-nya lama karena migration, warmup, cache, atau module scanning.
```

---

# 13. Graceful Shutdown

Kubernetes mengirim SIGTERM saat pod akan dihentikan. Service harus:

```text
1. Stop menerima traffic baru.
2. Selesaikan request in-flight.
3. Close HTTP server.
4. Close DB pool.
5. Close background executor.
6. Flush logs/traces/metrics.
```

## 13.1 Java Shutdown Hook

```java
package com.company.payment.bootstrap;

public final class Main {

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        var server = PaymentServer.start();

        Runtime.getRuntime().addShutdownHook(new Thread(() -> {
            server.stopGracefully();
        }));
    }
}
```

## 13.2 Kubernetes Lifecycle

```yaml
terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 30
```

Practical note:

```text
terminationGracePeriodSeconds harus cukup untuk average in-flight request + cleanup.
Jangan terlalu kecil untuk service yang melakukan external call/payment transaction.
```

---

# 14. Resource Requests & Limits

Contoh:

```yaml
resources:
  requests:
    cpu: "250m"
    memory: "512Mi"
  limits:
    cpu: "1000m"
    memory: "1024Mi"
```

JVM flags harus diselaraskan:

```yaml
env:
  - name: JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS
    value: >-
      -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=75
      -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=50
      -XX:+ExitOnOutOfMemoryError
      -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,level,tags
```

Practical rule:

```text
Container memory limit 1024Mi bukan berarti heap boleh 1024Mi.
Masih ada metaspace, thread stack, direct memory, native memory, code cache, agent overhead.
```

---

# 15. JPMS Runtime Flags di Kubernetes

Jika module-path runtime masih butuh temporary open:

```yaml
env:
  - name: JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS
    value: >-
      --add-opens=com.company.payment.api/com.company.payment.api.request=com.fasterxml.jackson.databind
      -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=75
```

Practical warning:

```text
JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS mudah tersebar dan terlupakan.
Untuk JPMS migration, lebih baik centralize flags di satu config dan dokumentasikan.
```

Jika flag dibutuhkan permanen, evaluasi apakah harus dipindah ke `module-info.java`:

```java
opens com.company.payment.api.request to com.fasterxml.jackson.databind;
```

---

# 16. Observability untuk Modular Runtime

## 16.1 Startup Log Minimum

Saat startup, log:

```text
1. Java version
2. Runtime mode: classpath / module path / jlink
3. Active profile/environment
4. Service version / git commit
5. JPMS module inventory summary
6. Active provider modules
7. DB pool config summary, tanpa secret
8. HTTP port
9. Health endpoint registered
10. Temporary JPMS flags, jika ada
```

Contoh:

```text
payment-service starting
java.version=17.0.x
runtime.mode=module-path
service.version=1.0.0
git.commit=abc123
active.payment.provider=midtrans
health.live=/health/live
health.ready=/health/ready
temporary.jpms.flags=0
```

## 16.2 Provider Observability

Dari seri SPI:

```text
Available PaymentProviders:
- id=midtrans, module=com.company.payment.integration.midtrans
- id=stripe, module=com.company.payment.integration.stripe

Active PaymentProvider:
- id=midtrans
```

## 16.3 Log Dependency Risk

Jika ada automatic module atau temporary `--add-opens`, log sebagai warning:

```text
WARN JPMS automatic module detected: some.library
WARN temporary add-opens active: com.company.payment.api/request -> jackson
```

---

# 17. Deployment Config Design

## 17.1 ConfigMap

```yaml
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: payment-service-config
data:
  PAYMENT_PROVIDER: "midtrans"
  DB_HOST: "postgresql.default.svc.cluster.local"
  DB_PORT: "5432"
  DB_NAME: "payment"
```

## 17.2 Secret

```yaml
apiVersion: v1
kind: Secret
metadata:
  name: payment-service-secret
type: Opaque
stringData:
  DB_USERNAME: "payment"
  DB_PASSWORD: "change-me"
  MIDTRANS_API_KEY: "change-me"
```

## 17.3 Deployment Env

```yaml
env:
  - name: PAYMENT_PROVIDER
    valueFrom:
      configMapKeyRef:
        name: payment-service-config
        key: PAYMENT_PROVIDER

  - name: DB_PASSWORD
    valueFrom:
      secretKeyRef:
        name: payment-service-secret
        key: DB_PASSWORD
```

Practical rule:

```text
Config boleh terlihat.
Secret tidak boleh masuk image, log, atau repo.
```

---

# 18. Rollout Strategy untuk JPMS Runtime Change

Mengubah dari `java -jar` ke `--module-path` atau `jlink` adalah **runtime behavior change**, bukan sekadar refactor.

## 18.1 Recommended Rollout

```text
Stage 1:
  CI module-path dry run, report-only.

Stage 2:
  Staging deploy dengan module-path runtime.

Stage 3:
  Canary 1 pod / small traffic.

Stage 4:
  Compare metrics:
    - startup time
    - memory
    - error rate
    - p95 latency
    - GC logs
    - readiness flaps
    - provider loading
    - DB pool behavior

Stage 5:
  Gradual rollout.

Stage 6:
  Keep rollback image/entrypoint ready.
```

## 18.2 Kubernetes Rolling Update

```yaml
strategy:
  type: RollingUpdate
  rollingUpdate:
    maxUnavailable: 0
    maxSurge: 1
```

For critical services:

```text
maxUnavailable: 0
```

helps keep capacity during rollout.

---

# 19. Rollback Plan

Before production module-path rollout, prepare:

```text
1. Previous Docker image tag.
2. Previous entrypoint mode.
3. Helm value toggle:
   runtimeMode: classpath | modulepath
4. DB schema compatibility.
5. Provider config compatibility.
6. Smoke test command.
7. Clear rollback owner.
```

Example Helm value:

```yaml
runtime:
  mode: "classpath"
```

Template concept:

```yaml
command:
  - "java"
args:
  {{- if eq .Values.runtime.mode "modulepath" }}
  - "--module-path"
  - "mods:lib"
  - "--module"
  - "com.company.payment.bootstrap/com.company.payment.bootstrap.Main"
  {{- else }}
  - "-jar"
  - "app.jar"
  {{- end }}
```

Practical rule:

```text
Jangan rollout module-path runtime tanpa path rollback ke classpath runtime.
```

---

# 20. Security & Patch Management

## 20.1 Standard JRE Base Image

Jika memakai base image seperti Temurin JRE:

```text
Patch dilakukan dengan update base image tag/digest dan rebuild image.
```

## 20.2 `jlink` Runtime Image

Jika memakai `jlink`:

```text
Patch dilakukan dengan rebuild custom runtime dari patched JDK.
```

Karena Oracle menegaskan developer bertanggung jawab memperbarui custom runtime images, proses patching harus masuk pipeline dependency/security update. ([Oracle Documentation](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/specs/man/jlink.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 20.3 Practical Policy

```text
1. Pin base image by digest untuk reproducibility.
2. Rebuild image saat ada JDK security update.
3. Scan image.
4. Scan dependencies.
5. Simpan SBOM jika organisasi mensyaratkan.
6. Jangan pakai jlink runtime tanpa patch process.
```

---

# 21. Runtime Decision Matrix

| Kondisi | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|
| Service existing, banyak reflection | Tetap `java -jar` dulu |
| Butuh dependency lebih transparan | Thin JAR + `lib/` |
| Boundary JPMS sudah stabil | Module path runtime |
| Image size/security surface sangat penting | Evaluasi `jlink` |
| Banyak automatic modules | Jangan mulai dari `jlink` |
| K8s probes belum benar | Jangan ubah runtime dulu |
| CI belum smoke test container | Jangan production module-path |
| Ada temporary `--add-opens` banyak | Stabilkan descriptor dulu |
| Startup lambat karena scanning | Pakai explicit registration + startupProbe |
| Payment/transaction-critical service | Canary + rollback wajib |

---

# 22. Example: Complete Classpath Docker + K8s

## 22.1 Dockerfile

```dockerfile
FROM eclipse-temurin:17-jre

WORKDIR /app

RUN useradd --system --uid 10001 appuser

COPY payment-bootstrap/target/payment-service.jar app.jar

USER 10001

EXPOSE 8080

ENTRYPOINT [
  "java",
  "-XX:MaxRAMPercentage=75",
  "-XX:+ExitOnOutOfMemoryError",
  "-Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,level,tags",
  "-jar",
  "app.jar"
]
```

## 22.2 Kubernetes Deployment

```yaml
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: payment-service
spec:
  replicas: 3
  strategy:
    type: RollingUpdate
    rollingUpdate:
      maxUnavailable: 0
      maxSurge: 1
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: payment-service
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: payment-service
    spec:
      terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 30
      containers:
        - name: payment-service
          image: registry.example.com/payment-service:1.0.0
          ports:
            - containerPort: 8080

          env:
            - name: PAYMENT_PROVIDER
              valueFrom:
                configMapKeyRef:
                  name: payment-service-config
                  key: PAYMENT_PROVIDER

            - name: DB_PASSWORD
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef:
                  name: payment-service-secret
                  key: DB_PASSWORD

          resources:
            requests:
              cpu: "250m"
              memory: "512Mi"
            limits:
              cpu: "1000m"
              memory: "1024Mi"

          startupProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/startup
              port: 8080
            failureThreshold: 30
            periodSeconds: 2

          readinessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/ready
              port: 8080
            periodSeconds: 5
            timeoutSeconds: 2
            failureThreshold: 3

          livenessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/live
              port: 8080
            initialDelaySeconds: 30
            periodSeconds: 10
            timeoutSeconds: 2
            failureThreshold: 3
```

---

# 23. Example: Complete Module Path Docker

```dockerfile
FROM eclipse-temurin:17-jre

WORKDIR /app

RUN useradd --system --uid 10001 appuser

COPY runtime/mods/ mods/
COPY runtime/lib/ lib/

USER 10001

EXPOSE 8080

ENTRYPOINT [
  "java",
  "-XX:MaxRAMPercentage=75",
  "-XX:+ExitOnOutOfMemoryError",
  "-Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,level,tags",
  "--module-path", "mods:lib",
  "--module", "com.company.payment.bootstrap/com.company.payment.bootstrap.Main"
]
```

Before using this in production, run:

```bash
java \
  --module-path "runtime/mods:runtime/lib" \
  --validate-modules
```

The `java` command has options such as `--module-path`, `--module`, and `--validate-modules`; `--validate-modules` is useful for finding conflicts or errors among modules on the module path. ([Oracle Documentation](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/docs/specs/man/java.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

# 24. Practical Lab

## Lab 1 - Identify Runtime Mode

Check:

```text
Dockerfile
Helm chart
Kubernetes Deployment
startup.sh
README
GitHub Actions
```

Classify:

```text
[ ] java -jar
[ ] java -cp
[ ] java --module-path
[ ] jlink custom runtime
```

---

## Lab 2 - Add Runtime Startup Log

Add startup log:

```text
runtime.mode=classpath/module-path/jlink
java.version=...
service.version=...
git.commit=...
temporary.jpms.flags=...
active.provider=...
```

Expected:

```text
Saat incident, engineer langsung tahu runtime mode dan provider aktif.
```

---

## Lab 3 - Create Module Path Image Locally

```bash
mvn clean package

mkdir -p runtime/mods runtime/lib

cp payment-domain/target/payment-domain-*.jar runtime/mods/
cp payment-api/target/payment-api-*.jar runtime/mods/
cp payment-application/target/payment-application-*.jar runtime/mods/
cp payment-rest/target/payment-rest-*.jar runtime/mods/
cp payment-persistence-mybatis/target/payment-persistence-mybatis-*.jar runtime/mods/
cp payment-bootstrap/target/payment-bootstrap-*.jar runtime/mods/

mvn -pl payment-bootstrap dependency:copy-dependencies \
  -DincludeScope=runtime \
  -DoutputDirectory=runtime/lib

java \
  --module-path "runtime/mods:runtime/lib" \
  --validate-modules
```

Expected:

```text
Module validation either passes or gives clear conflict.
```

---

## Lab 4 - Build `jlink` Runtime

```bash
jlink \
  --module-path "$JAVA_HOME/jmods:runtime/mods:runtime/lib" \
  --add-modules com.company.payment.bootstrap \
  --strip-debug \
  --no-header-files \
  --no-man-pages \
  --compress=2 \
  --output target/payment-runtime
```

Run:

```bash
target/payment-runtime/bin/java \
  --module-path "runtime/mods:runtime/lib" \
  --module com.company.payment.bootstrap/com.company.payment.bootstrap.Main
```

Expected:

```text
App starts with custom runtime, or failure list identifies missing module/reflection/split package issues.
```

---

## Lab 5 - Probe Behavior Test

Run container locally:

```bash
docker run --rm -p 8080:8080 payment-service:local
```

Test:

```bash
curl -i http://localhost:8080/health/live
curl -i http://localhost:8080/health/ready
curl -i http://localhost:8080/health/startup
```

Expected:

```text
/live returns 200 if JVM process is healthy.
/ready returns 200 only if service can accept traffic.
/startup returns 200 after startup complete.
```

---

# 25. Review Checklist

| Area | Question |
|---|---|
| Runtime mode | Apakah app jalan via `java -jar`, `-cp`, `--module-path`, atau `jlink`? |
| Dockerfile | Apakah build dan runtime stage terpisah? |
| Base image | Apakah JDK hanya dipakai build, JRE/custom runtime dipakai run? |
| Non-root | Apakah container tidak berjalan sebagai root? |
| JVM memory | Apakah heap disesuaikan dengan container memory limit? |
| JPMS flags | Apakah `--add-opens` sementara terdokumentasi? |
| Health endpoint | Apakah `/live`, `/ready`, `/startup` punya semantics berbeda? |
| K8s probes | Apakah liveness tidak mengecek external dependency berat? |
| Shutdown | Apakah SIGTERM ditangani graceful? |
| Observability | Apakah runtime mode/provider/module info muncul di startup log? |
| Rollout | Apakah module-path/jlink rollout dilakukan canary? |
| Rollback | Apakah ada path balik ke runtime lama? |
| Security | Apakah `jlink` runtime image ikut patch process? |

---

# 26. Practical Recommendation

Untuk Java 17+ Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis microservice:

```text
1. Mulai dari classpath runtime yang stabil.
2. Tambahkan JPMS untuk source/build boundary dulu.
3. Gunakan module-path dry run di CI sebelum production.
4. Jangan pakai jlink sebelum module-path runtime stabil.
5. Gunakan explicit Jersey registration untuk mengurangi scanning ambiguity.
6. Dokumentasikan semua --add-opens.
7. Buat health endpoint dengan semantic yang benar.
8. Jangan jadikan liveness sebagai dependency check.
9. Log runtime mode, provider aktif, dan temporary JPMS flags saat startup.
10. Rollout module-path/jlink sebagai runtime behavior change, bukan refactor biasa.
```

Target akhirnya:

```text
Runtime dapat diprediksi.
Deployment aman.
Module boundary tetap defensible.
Observability cukup untuk debugging production.
Rollback tetap tersedia.
```

**Seri 11/12 selesai - Runtime, Docker, Kubernetes & `jlink`.**

# Seri 12/12 - JPMS Governance & Production Readiness

## 1. Introduction

Seri terakhir ini fokus pada **governance**: bagaimana memastikan JPMS tidak berhenti sebagai eksperimen teknis, tetapi menjadi bagian dari **engineering discipline** di project Java 17+ Jersey/JAX-RS microservice.

Kalau seri sebelumnya membahas “cara membuat module”, seri ini membahas:

```text
Bagaimana menjaga module tetap sehat setelah masuk production?
```

Governance dibutuhkan karena boundary yang rapi bisa rusak perlahan lewat:

```text
1. Dependency baru yang salah tempat
2. Package internal yang mulai diexport
3. opens yang terlalu luas
4. --add-opens yang tidak pernah dihapus
5. REST module yang diam-diam depends ke persistence
6. Domain yang mulai tahu framework
7. SPI provider yang tidak terkontrol
8. Docker/Kubernetes runtime yang beda dari CI
```

JPMS memberi mekanisme seperti `requires`, `exports`, `opens`, `uses`, dan `provides`, tetapi mekanisme itu hanya efektif kalau ada ownership, review rule, CI guardrail, dan documentation standard. JEP 261 menjelaskan JPMS sebagai sistem module dengan module path, readability, exports, opens, serta opsi seperti `--add-exports` dan `--add-opens`; opsi escape hatch seperti itu harus digunakan hati-hati karena bisa melewati encapsulation yang ingin dijaga. ([OpenJDK](https://openjdk.org/jeps/261?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Governance Mental Model

```text
JPMS Governance
│
├── Architecture Governance
│   ├── module ownership
│   ├── dependency direction
│   ├── package export policy
│   ├── reflection opens policy
│   └── SPI/provider policy
│
├── Build Governance
│   ├── Maven dependency rules
│   ├── dependency convergence
│   ├── banned dependencies
│   ├── duplicate class checks
│   └── module-info.java review
│
├── Runtime Governance
│   ├── classpath vs module-path decision
│   ├── Docker entrypoint standard
│   ├── Kubernetes probe standard
│   ├── JVM flag governance
│   └── jlink patching policy
│
├── Security Governance
│   ├── dependency CVE review
│   ├── internal package exposure
│   ├── secret handling
│   ├── provider whitelist
│   └── runtime image patching
│
└── Operational Governance
    ├── observability
    ├── startup logs
    ├── health checks
    ├── rollback plan
    └── production readiness checklist
```

---

# 3. Decomposition Map

| Area | Governance rule | Practical implementation | Failure if ignored |
|---|---|---|---|
| Module ownership | Setiap module punya owner dan responsibility | `CODEOWNERS`, README module, ADR | Tidak jelas siapa approve dependency/export |
| Dependency direction | Dependency harus mengikuti boundary | REST → application → domain; persistence → domain | REST bypass business rule |
| Export policy | Export hanya package contract | Review setiap `exports` baru | Internal API bocor |
| Opens policy | `opens` hanya untuk reflection yang terbukti perlu | Dokumentasi reason/target framework | Semua package terbuka reflection |
| Runtime flags | `--add-opens` / `--add-exports` tidak boleh permanen tanpa owner | `docs/jpms-runtime-flags.md` | Technical debt tersembunyi |
| Maven dependency | Dependency dideklarasikan di module yang tepat | Dependency tree review | Parent POM jadi god dependency |
| Dependency convergence | Versi dependency harus konsisten | Maven Enforcer | Runtime `NoSuchMethodError` |
| Banned dependency | Blok dependency legacy/forbidden | Enforcer `bannedDependencies` | `javax.*` masuk lagi setelah migrasi |
| Duplicate classes | Tidak boleh ada class duplicate tanpa alasan | Extra Enforcer rule / script | Classpath shadowing |
| Split package | Satu package dimiliki satu module | Package ownership audit | Module-path failure |
| SPI provider | Provider harus eksplisit, observable, dan whitelisted | `uses`, `provides`, startup log | Provider salah ter-load |
| Testing | Production descriptor tidak dilonggarkan demi test | test-only flags / patch module | Production boundary rusak |
| Runtime mode | Runtime mode harus jelas | `runtime.mode=classpath/module-path/jlink` log | Debugging production sulit |
| Docker/K8s | Entrypoint dan probe harus standar | Dockerfile/Helm template review | Works in CI, fails in pod |
| Documentation | Setiap module punya contract doc | README/ADR/checklist | Onboarding lambat, review subjektif |

---

# 4. Module Ownership Standard

Setiap module harus punya ownership eksplisit.

Contoh struktur:

```text
payment-service/
├── payment-domain/
│   └── README.md
├── payment-api/
│   └── README.md
├── payment-application/
│   └── README.md
├── payment-rest/
│   └── README.md
├── payment-persistence-mybatis/
│   └── README.md
├── payment-integration-midtrans/
│   └── README.md
└── payment-bootstrap/
    └── README.md
```

Contoh `payment-domain/README.md`:

```markdown
# payment-domain

## Responsibility

Owns payment business model, value objects, domain events, domain exceptions, and domain ports.

## Allowed dependencies

- Java SE
- Small annotation-only dependency if explicitly approved

## Forbidden dependencies

- jakarta.ws.rs
- org.glassfish.jersey
- org.mybatis
- org.postgresql
- cloud SDKs
- JSON serializer implementation

## Exported packages

- com.company.payment.domain.model
- com.company.payment.domain.valueobject
- com.company.payment.domain.port
- com.company.payment.domain.event
- com.company.payment.domain.exception

## Internal packages

- com.company.payment.domain.internal
- com.company.payment.domain.policy.internal

## Review rule

Any new `exports`, `requires`, or dependency must be reviewed by payment-domain owner.
```

Tambahkan `CODEOWNERS`:

```text
/payment-domain/ @payment-domain-owners
/payment-api/ @payment-api-owners
/payment-rest/ @payment-platform-owners
/payment-persistence-mybatis/ @payment-data-access-owners
/payment-bootstrap/ @payment-platform-owners
```

---

# 5. Dependency Direction Governance

## 5.1 Allowed Dependency Graph

```text
payment-bootstrap
│
├── payment-rest
├── payment-application
├── payment-persistence-mybatis
├── payment-integration-*
└── runtime/server dependencies

payment-rest
│
├── payment-api
├── payment-application
└── jakarta.ws.rs

payment-application
│
└── payment-domain

payment-persistence-mybatis
│
└── payment-domain

payment-integration-*
│
└── payment-domain / SPI contract

payment-domain
│
└── Java SE only
```

## 5.2 Forbidden Direction

```text
payment-domain              → payment-rest
payment-domain              → jakarta.ws.rs
payment-domain              → org.mybatis
payment-application         → payment-persistence-mybatis
payment-application         → org.postgresql
payment-rest                → payment-persistence-mybatis
payment-api                 → payment-persistence-mybatis
payment-persistence-mybatis → payment-rest
```

## 5.3 CI Rule

Generate dependency tree JSON:

```bash
mvn -pl payment-rest dependency:tree \
  -DoutputType=json \
  -DoutputFile=payment-rest/target/dependency-tree.json
```

Maven Dependency Plugin supports `dependency:tree` and multiple output formats including text, DOT, GraphML, TGF, and JSON, so this is suitable for CI guardrails and artifact reports. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/plugins/maven-dependency-plugin/tree-mojo.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Simple check:

```bash
if grep -q "payment-persistence-mybatis" payment-rest/target/dependency-tree.json; then
  echo "ERROR: payment-rest must not depend on payment-persistence-mybatis"
  exit 1
fi
```

Better long-term: parse JSON with a script and produce readable violation reports.

---

# 6. `module-info.java` Review Standard

Every PR that changes `module-info.java` must answer:

```text
1. Why is this new requires needed?
2. Is this dependency aligned with layer direction?
3. Why is this package exported?
4. Is this exported package a real public contract?
5. Why is this package opened?
6. Which framework needs reflection?
7. Can opens be qualified to a specific module?
8. Is requires transitive really necessary?
9. Is requires static truly compile-time only?
10. Does this change affect runtime mode?
```

## 6.1 Example Review Diff

```diff
 module com.company.payment.rest {
     requires jakarta.ws.rs;
     requires com.company.payment.api;
     requires com.company.payment.application;
+    requires com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis;

     exports com.company.payment.rest.resource;
 }
```

Review response:

```text
Reject.

Reason:
payment-rest must not require payment-persistence-mybatis.
REST should call application use case only.
Persistence implementation must be wired in payment-bootstrap.
```

## 6.2 Another Example

```diff
 module com.company.payment.api {
     exports com.company.payment.api.request;
     exports com.company.payment.api.response;
+    exports com.company.payment.api.internal;
 }
```

Review response:

```text
Reject unless api.internal is being promoted to official contract.

If the reason is JSON serialization or validation reflection,
use opens instead of exports.
```

---

# 7. Exports Policy

## 7.1 Default Rule

```text
Default: package is internal.
Export only if another module needs compile-time access as a real contract.
```

## 7.2 Allowed Exports by Module

| Module | Export allowed |
|---|---|
| `payment-domain` | `model`, `valueobject`, `port`, `event`, `exception`, `spi` |
| `payment-api` | `request`, `response`, `error`, public validation annotation |
| `payment-application` | `usecase`, `command`, `query`, public application service |
| `payment-rest` | Resource/provider package only to bootstrap if needed |
| `payment-persistence-mybatis` | Repository implementation only to bootstrap, if manual wiring |
| `payment-integration-*` | Usually no implementation export; SPI provider via `provides` |
| `payment-bootstrap` | Usually no export |

## 7.3 Anti-pattern

```java
module com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis {
    exports com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.mapper;
    exports com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.entity;
    exports com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.typehandler;
}
```

Correction:

```java
module com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis {
    requires com.company.payment.domain;

    exports com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.repository
        to com.company.payment.bootstrap;

    opens com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.mapper;
    opens com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.entity;
    opens com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.typehandler;
}
```

---

# 8. Opens Policy

## 8.1 Default Rule

```text
Default: no opens.
Add opens only when reflection is required by framework/runtime.
```

Common packages that may need `opens`:

```text
payment-api.request
payment-api.response
payment-api.error
payment-rest.resource
payment-rest.exception
payment-rest.filter
payment-rest.provider
payment-persistence-mybatis.mapper
payment-persistence-mybatis.entity
payment-persistence-mybatis.typehandler
```

## 8.2 Opens Decision Tree

```text
Does another module need normal compile-time access?
│
├── Yes → exports
│
└── No
    │
    └── Does a framework need runtime reflection?
        │
        ├── Yes → opens
        │   └── Is target module known?
        │       ├── Yes → opens package to target.module
        │       └── No  → temporary unqualified opens + document
        │
        └── No → keep package closed
```

## 8.3 Governance Record

Create:

```text
docs/jpms-opens-register.md
```

Example:

```markdown
# JPMS Opens Register

| Module | Package | Opened to | Reason | Owner | Expiry |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| payment-api | com.company.payment.api.request | com.fasterxml.jackson.databind | Request DTO JSON deserialization | Platform | Permanent if Jackson remains provider |
| payment-rest | com.company.payment.rest.resource | unqualified | Jersey resource reflection during migration | Platform | Replace with qualified opens after module inventory |
| payment-persistence-mybatis | com.company.payment.persistence.mybatis.entity | unqualified | MyBatis result mapping | Data Access | Review after mapper integration test |
```

---

# 9. Runtime Flag Governance

Runtime flags such as:

```text
--add-opens
--add-exports
--add-reads
--add-modules
--patch-module
```

must not appear silently in Dockerfile, Helm values, or `JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS`.

JEP 261 and related OpenJDK notes warn that access-bypassing options such as `--add-exports` and `--add-opens` should be used with care because they can expose internal APIs and weaken encapsulation. ([OpenJDK](https://openjdk.org/jeps/261?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Create:

```text
docs/jpms-runtime-flags.md
```

Example:

```markdown
# JPMS Runtime Flags Register

## Flag

--add-opens=com.company.payment.api/com.company.payment.api.request=com.fasterxml.jackson.databind

## Reason

Jackson deserialization needs reflective access to CreatePaymentRequest during module-path runtime migration.

## Owner

Payment Platform Team

## Risk

Bypasses JPMS encapsulation outside module-info.java.

## Target Fix

Move to qualified opens in payment-api/module-info.java.

## Expiry

Before module-path production rollout.
```

Policy:

```text
1. No runtime JPMS flag without documented owner.
2. No production JPMS flag added only in Helm without CI parity.
3. No broad ALL-UNNAMED opens in production unless explicitly approved.
4. No permanent flag without ADR.
```

---

# 10. Maven Dependency Governance

## 10.1 Parent POM Rule

Parent POM should manage versions, not inject all framework dependencies into every child.

Bad:

```xml
<dependencies>
    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey.core</groupId>
        <artifactId>jersey-server</artifactId>
        <version>${jersey.version}</version>
    </dependency>
    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.mybatis</groupId>
        <artifactId>mybatis</artifactId>
        <version>${mybatis.version}</version>
    </dependency>
</dependencies>
```

Better:

```xml
<dependencyManagement>
    <dependencies>
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey.core</groupId>
            <artifactId>jersey-server</artifactId>
            <version>${jersey.version}</version>
        </dependency>
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.mybatis</groupId>
            <artifactId>mybatis</artifactId>
            <version>${mybatis.version}</version>
        </dependency>
    </dependencies>
</dependencyManagement>
```

Rule:

```text
dependencyManagement = version control
dependencies = actual usage
module-info.java = module readability
```

## 10.2 Enforcer Rules

Recommended gradual rollout:

```xml
<rules>
    <dependencyConvergence/>
    <requireUpperBoundDeps/>

    <bannedDependencies>
        <excludes>
            <exclude>javax.ws.rs:javax.ws.rs-api</exclude>
        </excludes>
        <searchTransitive>true</searchTransitive>
    </bannedDependencies>
</rules>
```

Maven Enforcer’s `requireUpperBoundDeps` requires the resolved dependency version to be equal to or higher than the highest transitive version found, which helps catch Maven “nearest wins” downgrades. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/enforcer/enforcer-rules/requireUpperBoundDeps.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Maven Enforcer also provides rules such as `dependencyConvergence` and `bannedDependencies`, useful for preventing inconsistent dependency versions or forbidden libraries from entering the build. ([Oracle Blogs](https://blogs.oracle.com/developers/mastering-maven-the-enforcer-plugin?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 10.3 Dependency PR Checklist

Every PR that changes `pom.xml` must answer:

```text
1. Which module owns this dependency?
2. Is dependency scope correct?
3. What transitive dependencies are introduced?
4. Does this dependency violate layer direction?
5. Is version controlled by dependencyManagement/BOM?
6. Does it require module-info.java changes?
7. Does it require opens for reflection?
8. Does it affect Docker/runtime packaging?
9. Does it introduce CVE/license/compliance concern?
10. Does it need integration test coverage?
```

---

# 11. `jdeps` Governance

`jdeps` should be used as an architecture audit tool.

The `jdeps` command shows package-level or class-level dependencies for class files, directories, and JARs, and supports options such as summary output and DOT graph generation. ([Oracle Documentation](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/specs/man/jdeps.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 11.1 CI Command

```bash
jdeps \
  --multi-release 17 \
  --summary \
  payment-bootstrap/target/payment-bootstrap-*.jar \
  > target/jpms/jdeps-summary.txt
```

## 11.2 Module Dependency Report

```bash
jdeps \
  --multi-release 17 \
  --print-module-deps \
  payment-bootstrap/target/payment-bootstrap-*.jar \
  > target/jpms/jdeps-module-deps.txt
```

## 11.3 JDK Internal API Check

```bash
jdeps \
  --multi-release 17 \
  --jdk-internals \
  payment-bootstrap/target/payment-bootstrap-*.jar \
  > target/jpms/jdk-internals.txt
```

Governance rule:

```text
If jdeps shows domain depending on REST/Jersey/MyBatis/PostgreSQL package,
treat it as architecture violation.
```

---

# 12. Split Package Governance

## 12.1 Policy

```text
One package belongs to one module.
```

Bad:

```text
payment-domain.jar
└── com.company.payment.common

payment-api.jar
└── com.company.payment.common

payment-rest.jar
└── com.company.payment.common
```

Better:

```text
payment-domain.jar
└── com.company.payment.domain.common

payment-api.jar
└── com.company.payment.api.common

payment-rest.jar
└── com.company.payment.rest.common
```

## 12.2 Review Rule

A new package must answer:

```text
1. Which module owns this package?
2. Does the package name reflect ownership?
3. Is it too generic?
4. Could this become split package later?
5. Should this be a dedicated contract module instead?
```

## 12.3 CI Script

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

mkdir -p target/all-jars
find . -path "*/target/*.jar" -type f -exec cp {} target/all-jars/ \;

for jar in target/all-jars/*.jar; do
  jar tf "$jar" \
    | grep '\.class$' \
    | sed 's|/[^/]*\.class$||' \
    | sed 's|/|.|g' \
    | sort -u \
    | awk -v jar="$jar" '{ print $0 " " jar }'
done \
| sort \
| awk '
{
  pkg=$1
  jar=$2
  jars[pkg]=jars[pkg] " " jar
  count[pkg]++
}
END {
  for (pkg in count) {
    if (count[pkg] > 1) {
      print pkg ":" jars[pkg]
    }
  }
}'
```

At first, report-only. After cleanup, make it blocking for internal packages.

---

# 13. Automatic Module Governance

When using module path, third-party JARs without explicit module descriptors may become automatic modules. Do not guess module names.

Command:

```bash
jar --describe-module --file target/dependency/some-library.jar
```

Inventory:

```bash
for j in payment-bootstrap/target/dependency/*.jar; do
  echo "==== $j"
  jar --describe-module --file "$j" 2>/dev/null | head -30
done > target/jpms/module-name-inventory.txt
```

Governance document:

```text
docs/jpms-module-name-inventory.md
```

Example:

```markdown
# JPMS Module Name Inventory

| Artifact | Module status | Module name | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| jakarta.ws.rs-api | explicit | jakarta.ws.rs | Used by payment-rest |
| jackson-databind | explicit/automatic depending version | com.fasterxml.jackson.databind | Validate on upgrade |
| mybatis | explicit/automatic depending version | TBD | Validate before module-path runtime |
```

Policy:

```text
1. No requires to third-party module without inventory evidence.
2. No qualified opens to third-party module without inventory evidence.
3. Update inventory when dependency version changes.
```

---

# 14. SPI Provider Governance

From Seri 9, SPI is powerful but risky if provider loading is uncontrolled.

## 14.1 Policy

```text
1. SPI interface must live in domain/spi or dedicated contract module.
2. Consumer module declares uses.
3. Provider module declares provides ... with.
4. Provider implementation package should not be exported.
5. Provider selection must be config-based or capability-based.
6. Provider identity must be logged.
7. Duplicate provider id must fail startup.
8. Provider JAR/module must be explicit runtime dependency.
9. Payment provider used must be persisted for audit/reconciliation.
```

## 14.2 Provider Registry Startup Checks

```java
public final class ProviderGovernance {

    public static void validate(List<PaymentProvider> providers, String configuredProviderId) {
        if (providers.isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("No PaymentProvider found");
        }

        var ids = new java.util.HashSet<String>();

        for (var provider : providers) {
            var id = provider.descriptor().providerId();

            if (!ids.add(id)) {
                throw new IllegalStateException("Duplicate PaymentProvider id: " + id);
            }
        }

        var configuredProviderExists = providers.stream()
            .anyMatch(provider -> provider.descriptor().providerId().equals(configuredProviderId));

        if (!configuredProviderExists) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "Configured PaymentProvider is not available: " + configuredProviderId
            );
        }
    }
}
```

## 14.3 Startup Log

```text
PaymentProviders loaded:
- id=midtrans module=com.company.payment.integration.midtrans class=MidtransPaymentProvider
- id=stripe module=com.company.payment.integration.stripe class=StripePaymentProvider

Active PaymentProvider:
- id=midtrans
```

---

# 15. Testing Governance

## 15.1 Principle

```text
Production module-info.java must not be weakened for tests.
```

Bad:

```java
open module com.company.payment.domain {
    exports com.company.payment.domain.model;
    exports com.company.payment.domain.internal;
}
```

Better:

```text
1. Test through public API.
2. Use fake repository/provider.
3. Use test fixture module.
4. Use test-only --add-exports or --add-opens.
5. Use module-info-patch.maven if project is ready.
```

Maven Surefire is used in the `test` phase for unit tests, while Failsafe is designed for integration tests using `integration-test` and `verify` goals; that separation is useful for modular service testing and CI lifecycle control. ([Oracle Documentation](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 15.2 Test Policy

| Test type | Tool | Rule |
|---|---|---|
| Domain unit test | Surefire/JUnit | No DB, no Jersey |
| Application unit test | Surefire/JUnit | Fake port/provider |
| REST integration test | Failsafe/Jersey test | Explicit resource/provider registration |
| Persistence integration test | Failsafe/PostgreSQL | Real SQL and mapper test |
| Module-path smoke | Script/CI | Report-only first, blocking later |
| Dependency boundary test | Script/CI | Blocking for high-confidence violations |

---

# 16. Runtime Governance

## 16.1 Runtime Mode Must Be Explicit

At startup, log:

```text
runtime.mode=classpath
```

or:

```text
runtime.mode=module-path
```

or:

```text
runtime.mode=jlink
```

## 16.2 Docker Entrypoint Standard

Classpath:

```dockerfile
ENTRYPOINT ["java", "-jar", "app.jar"]
```

Module path:

```dockerfile
ENTRYPOINT [
  "java",
  "--module-path", "mods:lib",
  "--module", "com.company.payment.bootstrap/com.company.payment.bootstrap.Main"
]
```

jlink:

```dockerfile
ENTRYPOINT [
  "/opt/payment-runtime/bin/java",
  "--module-path", "mods:lib",
  "--module", "com.company.payment.bootstrap/com.company.payment.bootstrap.Main"
]
```

## 16.3 Kubernetes Probe Standard

```text
/live
  Process health only.
  Do not check DB/external gateway.

/ready
  Can accept traffic.
  May check critical dependencies lightly.

/startup
  Startup complete.
  Useful for slow startup.
```

Kubernetes probes are periodic diagnostics performed by kubelet, and liveness/readiness/startup probes have different effects on restart and traffic routing behavior, so they must not be treated as interchangeable. ([Oracle Documentation](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 17. Production Readiness Checklist

## 17.1 Architecture

| Check | Required |
|---|---:|
| Module ownership documented | Yes |
| Allowed dependency graph documented | Yes |
| Domain free from Jersey/MyBatis/PostgreSQL | Yes |
| REST not depending on persistence | Yes |
| Application not depending on infrastructure implementation | Yes |
| API DTO not reusing persistence entity | Yes |
| Bootstrap owns composition root | Yes |
| SPI provider selection explicit | If SPI used |

## 17.2 JPMS

| Check | Required |
|---|---:|
| `module-info.java` reviewed | Yes |
| `exports` minimal | Yes |
| `opens` minimal and documented | Yes |
| `requires transitive` justified | If used |
| `requires static` justified | If used |
| No broad `open module` unless approved | Yes |
| No undocumented `--add-opens` | Yes |
| Third-party module names inventoried | For module-path runtime |
| Split package audit done | For module-path runtime |

## 17.3 Maven / Dependency

| Check | Required |
|---|---:|
| Parent uses `dependencyManagement`, not inherited god dependencies | Yes |
| Dependency tree report generated | Yes |
| Dependency convergence checked | Recommended / Yes for strict build |
| Banned dependencies configured | Recommended |
| Duplicate class check configured | Recommended |
| Test-only dependencies not in runtime | Yes |
| CVE/security scan done | Organization dependent, strongly recommended |

## 17.4 Testing

| Check | Required |
|---|---:|
| Unit tests for domain/application | Yes |
| REST integration tests | Yes |
| MyBatis mapper integration tests | Yes |
| JSON serialization/deserialization tested | Yes |
| Validation tested if used | Yes |
| SPI provider discovery tested if used | Yes |
| Module-path dry run if target runtime is module path | Yes |
| Docker smoke test | Yes |

## 17.5 Runtime / Ops

| Check | Required |
|---|---:|
| Runtime mode documented | Yes |
| Docker entrypoint reviewed | Yes |
| K8s probes defined correctly | Yes |
| JVM memory flags aligned with container limits | Yes |
| Graceful shutdown handled | Yes |
| Startup log includes runtime mode and provider info | Yes |
| Rollback plan exists | Yes |
| jlink patching policy exists if jlink used | Yes |

---

# 18. Governance CI Pipeline

```yaml
name: JPMS Governance

on:
  pull_request:
  push:
    branches: [ main ]

jobs:
  governance:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: '17'
          cache: maven

      - name: Build and test
        run: mvn -B clean verify

      - name: Generate dependency trees
        run: |
          for module in payment-domain payment-api payment-application payment-rest payment-persistence-mybatis payment-bootstrap; do
            mvn -B -pl "$module" dependency:tree \
              -DoutputType=json \
              -DoutputFile="$module/target/dependency-tree.json"
          done

      - name: Check dependency boundaries
        run: python3 scripts/check-dependency-boundaries.py

      - name: JPMS module inventory
        run: |
          mkdir -p target/jpms
          mvn -B -pl payment-bootstrap dependency:copy-dependencies \
            -DincludeScope=runtime \
            -DoutputDirectory=payment-bootstrap/target/dependency

          for j in payment-bootstrap/target/dependency/*.jar; do
            echo "==== $j" >> target/jpms/module-name-inventory.txt
            jar --describe-module --file "$j" >> target/jpms/module-name-inventory.txt 2>&1 || true
          done

      - name: jdeps reports
        run: |
          mkdir -p target/jpms
          jdeps --multi-release 17 \
            --summary \
            payment-bootstrap/target/payment-bootstrap-*.jar \
            > target/jpms/jdeps-summary.txt || true

          jdeps --multi-release 17 \
            --jdk-internals \
            payment-bootstrap/target/payment-bootstrap-*.jar \
            > target/jpms/jdk-internals.txt || true

      - name: Upload governance reports
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: jpms-governance-reports
          path: |
            **/target/dependency-tree.json
            target/jpms/*.txt
            **/target/surefire-reports/**
            **/target/failsafe-reports/**
```

# 19. Dependency Boundary Script Example

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

RULES = {
    "payment-domain/target/dependency-tree.json": [
        "jakarta.ws.rs",
        "org.glassfish.jersey",
        "org.mybatis",
        "org.postgresql",
        "com.fasterxml.jackson.databind",
    ],
    "payment-application/target/dependency-tree.json": [
        "jakarta.ws.rs",
        "org.glassfish.jersey",
        "org.mybatis",
        "org.postgresql",
    ],
    "payment-rest/target/dependency-tree.json": [
        "payment-persistence-mybatis",
        "org.postgresql",
    ],
    "payment-api/target/dependency-tree.json": [
        "payment-persistence-mybatis",
        "org.mybatis",
        "org.postgresql",
    ],
    "payment-persistence-mybatis/target/dependency-tree.json": [
        "payment-rest",
        "jakarta.ws.rs",
        "org.glassfish.jersey",
    ],
}

def walk(node):
    yield node
    for child in node.get("children", []) or []:
        yield from walk(child)

def artifact_text(node):
    return ":".join([
        node.get("groupId", ""),
        node.get("artifactId", ""),
        node.get("version", ""),
        node.get("scope", ""),
    ])

failed = False

for file_name, banned_fragments in RULES.items():
    path = Path(file_name)

    if not path.exists():
        print(f"SKIP: {file_name} not found")
        continue

    root = json.loads(path.read_text())
    artifacts = [artifact_text(node) for node in walk(root)]

    for banned in banned_fragments:
        matches = [artifact for artifact in artifacts if banned in artifact]

        if matches:
            failed = True
            print(f"ERROR: {file_name} contains forbidden dependency fragment: {banned}")

            for match in matches:
                print(f"  - {match}")

if failed:
    sys.exit(1)

print("Dependency boundary checks passed.")
```

# 20. ADR Template for JPMS Decisions

Create:

```text
docs/adr/000X-jpms-module-boundary.md
```

Template:

```markdown
# ADR-000X: JPMS Module Boundary for <module-name>

## Status

Accepted / Proposed / Deprecated

## Context

Describe the service, module, dependency, or runtime issue.

## Decision

Describe the decision:
- New module
- New export
- New opens
- New requires
- Module-path runtime
- jlink runtime
- SPI provider

## Rationale

Why this decision is better than alternatives.

## Alternatives Considered

1. Keep classpath runtime
2. Manual wiring
3. ServiceLoader SPI
4. Broad open module
5. Test-only patch

## Consequences

Positive:
- ...

Negative:
- ...

## Guardrails

- CI checks
- dependency rules
- documentation
- owner

## Review Date

YYYY-MM-DD
```

Use ADR for:

```text
1. New module boundary
2. Runtime migration to module path
3. jlink adoption
4. Long-term --add-opens
5. SPI provider framework
6. Broad open module exception
```

# 21. Documentation Standard

Minimum docs:

```text
docs/
├── architecture/
│   ├── module-graph.md
│   ├── dependency-rules.md
│   ├── jpms-opens-register.md
│   ├── jpms-runtime-flags.md
│   └── module-name-inventory.md
│
├── adr/
│   └── 0001-jpms-boundary.md
│
├── operations/
│   ├── runtime-mode.md
│   ├── docker-entrypoint.md
│   ├── kubernetes-probes.md
│   └── rollback.md
│
└── testing/
    ├── unit-test-strategy.md
    ├── integration-test-strategy.md
    └── module-path-smoke-test.md
```

Each module should have:

```text
1. Responsibility
2. Owner
3. Allowed dependencies
4. Forbidden dependencies
5. Exported packages
6. Opened packages
7. Runtime considerations
8. Test strategy
```

---

# 22. Common Governance Failures

| Failure | Early signal | Preventive control |
|---|---|---|
| Domain starts depending on framework | New dependency in domain POM | Dependency boundary CI |
| REST bypasses application | REST imports repository/mapper | No REST → persistence dependency |
| Internal package becomes public | New `exports internal` | Module-info PR review |
| Reflection access grows uncontrolled | Many unqualified `opens` | Opens register |
| Runtime flags pile up | `JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS` grows | Runtime flag register |
| SPI provider mismatch | Provider selected unexpectedly | Provider whitelist + startup log |
| Module-path drift | CI uses classpath, Docker uses module path | Runtime parity smoke test |
| jlink image stale | JDK CVE not patched in image | Runtime patching policy |
| Duplicate classes hidden | Fat JAR works until runtime conflict | Duplicate class check |
| Split package blocks module path | `reads package from both` | Package ownership check |

---

# 23. Final Production Go / No-Go

## Go if

```text
1. Architecture dependency graph is clean.
2. module-info.java is reviewed and minimal.
3. Exports are contract-only.
4. Opens are justified and documented.
5. No undocumented runtime JPMS flags.
6. Dependency tree guardrail is active.
7. REST, MyBatis, JSON, validation paths are integration-tested.
8. Runtime mode is explicitly logged.
9. Docker/Kubernetes entrypoint is tested.
10. Rollback plan exists.
```

## No-Go if

```text
1. REST still depends on persistence.
2. Domain depends on Jersey/MyBatis/PostgreSQL.
3. Production needs many undocumented --add-opens.
4. Module-path runtime only works locally.
5. Docker startup differs from CI.
6. Health probes are not reliable.
7. SPI provider selection is implicit.
8. jlink is used without patching policy.
9. Split package issues remain.
10. There is no owner for module boundary decisions.
```

---

│

# 25. Final Recommendation

Untuk tim enterprise yang memakai Java 17+, Jersey/JAX-RS, Maven, MyBatis, PostgreSQL, Docker, dan Kubernetes, target governance yang sehat adalah:

```text
JPMS bukan sekadar module-info.java.
JPMS adalah cara membuat boundary arsitektur menjadi eksplisit, reviewable, testable, dan enforceable.
```

Urutan maturity yang realistis:

```text
Level 1 - Dependency hygiene
  Maven dependency tree bersih, parent POM tidak menjadi god dependency.

Level 2 - Compile-time module boundary
  Domain/application/API mulai punya module-info.java.

Level 3 - Reflection governance
  opens terdokumentasi dan minimal.

Level 4 - CI guardrail
  Forbidden dependency, jdeps report, module inventory, split package check.

Level 5 - Runtime clarity
  classpath/module-path/jlink mode jelas dan logged.

Level 6 - Production modular readiness
  module-path atau jlink hanya jika test, ops, rollback, dan patching sudah siap.
```

Untuk onboarding cepat sebagai senior engineer, prioritas praktisnya:

```text
1. Pahami module graph.
2. Hafalkan forbidden dependency direction.
3. Review setiap pom.xml dan module-info.java change dengan curiga sehat.
4. Jangan izinkan REST bypass application.
5. Jangan izinkan domain tahu framework.
6. Jangan izinkan opens/exports tanpa alasan.
7. Jangan izinkan runtime flags tanpa owner.
8. Selalu samakan CI, Docker, dan Kubernetes runtime behavior.
```
